#### `packages/storage-adapters/src/interfaces/IStorageProvider.ts`

This is the central contract. Every provider adapter must implement it exactly.

```typescript
/**
 * IStorageProvider
 *
 * Uniform interface over heterogeneous cloud storage providers.
 * All methods operate on *opaque encrypted bytes* — adapters have
 * no awareness of the content they store. Encryption is handled
 * exclusively by crypto-core before any adapter is invoked.
 *
 * Implementations: GoogleDriveAdapter, OneDriveAdapter,
 *                  DropboxAdapter, MegaAdapter
 */

export type ProviderId = 'google_drive' | 'onedrive' | 'dropbox' | 'mega';

// ── Upload ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface UploadRequest {
  /** Suggested filename in the provider's storage (opaque to Aether logic). */
  filename:    string;
  /** Encrypted shard bytes. Adapter must not inspect or transform content. */
  data:        Buffer | Uint8Array;
  /** MIME type to pass to the provider. Use 'application/octet-stream'. */
  mimeType:    string;
  /** Optional parent folder ID (provider-native) for organisational namespacing. */
  folderId?:   string;
}

export interface UploadResult {
  /** Provider-native object identifier. Stored in physical_chunks.provider_object_id. */
  objectId:   string;
  /** Provider-reported ETag or version token. Used for out-of-band change detection. */
  etag:       string | null;
  /** Size of the object as confirmed by the provider (may differ from upload size if provider wraps). */
  sizeBytes:  number;
  /** ISO-8601 timestamp of the upload as reported by the provider. */
  uploadedAt: string;
}

// ── Download ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface DownloadRequest {
  objectId: string;
  /** If provided, adapter should pass as If-None-Match for cache validation. */
  etag?:    string;
}

export interface DownloadResult {
  data:          Buffer;
  etag:          string | null;
  sizeBytes:     number;
  /** True if provider returned 304 Not Modified (etag matched). */
  notModified:   boolean;
}

// ── Delete ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface DeleteRequest {
  objectId: string;
}

// ── Quota ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ProviderQuota {
  totalBytes: number;
  usedBytes:  number;
  freeBytes:  number;
}

// ── Delta / Change Detection ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Opaque cursor token returned by the provider after each delta poll.
 * Stored per-user per-provider in Redis; passed back on the next poll.
 */
export type DeltaCursor = string;

export interface DeltaChange {
  objectId:    string;
  changeType:  'created' | 'modified' | 'deleted';
  newEtag:     string | null;
  modifiedAt:  string;
}

export interface DeltaPollResult {
  changes:    DeltaChange[];
  nextCursor: DeltaCursor;
  hasMore:    boolean;
}

// ── Folder management ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface EnsureFolderResult {
  folderId: string;
  created:  boolean; // false if folder already existed
}

// ── The interface ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface IStorageProvider {
  readonly providerId: ProviderId;

  /**
   * Upload an encrypted shard.
   * Must be idempotent: uploading the same filename twice should produce
   * a new object (providers may return duplicate names; IDs are canonical).
   */
  upload(req: UploadRequest): Promise<UploadResult>;

  /**
   * Download an encrypted shard by provider object ID.
   * Must verify the download completed fully (check Content-Length vs received bytes).
   * Throws ProviderError with code 'NOT_FOUND' if the object no longer exists.
   */
  download(req: DownloadRequest): Promise<DownloadResult>;

  /**
   * Permanently delete an object.
   * Must NOT throw if the object is already deleted (idempotent).
   */
  delete(req: DeleteRequest): Promise<void>;

  /**
   * Return current storage quota from the provider's API.
   * Values are best-effort; providers may return approximate figures.
   */
  getQuota(): Promise<ProviderQuota>;

  /**
   * Poll for changes since the last cursor.
   * On first call (cursor = null), returns a cursor representing "now"
   * with an empty change list — establishes the baseline.
   */
  pollDelta(cursor: DeltaCursor | null): Promise<DeltaPollResult>;

  /**
   * Ensure the Aether shard folder exists in the provider's namespace.
   * Creates it if absent. Returns the provider-native folder ID.
   * Aether creates a single top-level folder: "Aether Secure Storage".
   * Users must not rename or move this folder.
   */
  ensureAetherFolder(): Promise<EnsureFolderResult>;
}

// ── Error type ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type ProviderErrorCode =
  | 'NOT_FOUND'
  | 'RATE_LIMITED'
  | 'QUOTA_EXCEEDED'
  | 'AUTH_EXPIRED'
  | 'NETWORK_ERROR'
  | 'PROVIDER_ERROR';

export class ProviderError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly code: ProviderErrorCode,
    message: string,
    public readonly retryAfterMs?: number,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ProviderError';
  }
}
```

#### `packages/storage-adapters/src/interfaces/IChunkUploadHandle.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * IChunkUploadHandle
 *
 * Abstraction over resumable / multipart upload sessions.
 * Providers differ significantly in their resumable upload APIs:
 *   - Google Drive: single upload session URL, PUT with byte ranges
 *   - OneDrive: createUploadSession, PUT with Content-Range
 *   - Dropbox: /upload_session/start → /append → /finish
 *   - MEGA: own SDK handles multipart internally
 *
 * The chunk-orchestrator uses this handle to resume interrupted uploads
 * without re-uploading completed byte ranges.
 */

export interface IChunkUploadHandle {
  /** Session identifier (provider-native upload session URL or ID). */
  readonly sessionId: string;

  /** Total bytes already acknowledged by the provider. */
  readonly bytesUploaded: number;

  /** Total bytes this upload will transfer when complete. */
  readonly totalBytes: number;

  /**
   * Upload a byte range. Implementors must send the correct
   * Content-Range header matching offset and data.length.
   */
  uploadRange(offset: number, data: Buffer): Promise<void>;

  /**
   * Finalise the upload. Call after all ranges are uploaded.
   * Returns the UploadResult identical to IStorageProvider.upload.
   */
  finalise(): Promise<import('./IStorageProvider.js').UploadResult>;

  /** Abort and clean up the upload session on the provider's side. */
  abort(): Promise<void>;
}
```

#### `packages/storage-adapters/src/interfaces/IProviderQuota.ts`

```typescript
import type { ProviderQuota } from './IStorageProvider.js';

/** Extended quota with provider-specific limits and breakdown. */
export interface IProviderQuota extends ProviderQuota {
  providerId:       string;
  /** Free tier ceiling, if applicable (e.g. Google Drive = 15 GB). */
  freeTierBytes:    number | null;
  /** True if the user is approaching quota (>85% used). */
  nearingLimit:     boolean;
  /** ISO-8601 timestamp of when this quota figure was last fetched. */
  fetchedAt:        string;
}

export function computeQuota(raw: ProviderQuota, freeTierBytes: number | null): IProviderQuota {
  return {
    ...raw,
    freeTierBytes,
    nearingLimit: raw.usedBytes / raw.totalBytes > 0.85,
    fetchedAt:    new Date().toISOString(),
    providerId:   '', // caller sets this
  };
}
```

```typescript
export * from './IChunkUploadHandle.js';
export * from './IProviderQuota.js';
```

### Step 3 · Google Drive Adapter

#### `packages/storage-adapters/src/providers/GoogleDriveAdapter.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * GoogleDriveAdapter
 *
 * Implements IStorageProvider for Google Drive using the Drive v3 REST API.
 *
 * Key constraints addressed:
 *   - 10 req/s per-user rate limit → caller (rate-limit-service) must gate invocations.
 *     Adapter surfaces HTTP 429 as ProviderError('RATE_LIMITED') with retryAfterMs.
 *   - 5 MB/s upload throughput limit per file → use resumable upload sessions for all
 *     uploads; simple multipart only for shards < 5 MB.
 *   - Refresh token rotation → adapter refreshes access token transparently using
 *     the OAuthTokenRefresher injected at construction time.
 *   - Delta sync → uses Drive Changes API with pageToken cursors.
 *   - Quota → uses about.get with fields=storageQuota.
 */

import axios, {
  type AxiosInstance,
  type AxiosError,
  type AxiosResponse,
} from 'axios';
import FormData from 'form-data';
import {
  type IStorageProvider,
  type UploadRequest,
  type UploadResult,
  type DownloadRequest,
  type DownloadResult,
  type DeleteRequest,
  type ProviderQuota,
  type DeltaCursor,
  type DeltaPollResult,
  type EnsureFolderResult,
  ProviderError,
} from '../interfaces/IStorageProvider.js';

// ── Types ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface GoogleDriveAdapterOptions {
  /** Called when the access token needs to be refreshed. Adapter is stateless re: token storage. */
  getAccessToken: () => Promise<string>;
  /** Aether's folder name in the user's Drive. Must be stable. */
  folderName?: string;
}

interface GDriveFile {
  id:          string;
  name:        string;
  md5Checksum: string;
  size:        string;
  createdTime: string;
  modifiedTime: string;
  parents:     string[];
}

interface GDriveAbout {
  storageQuota: {
    limit:            string; // may be absent for G Suite unlimited accounts
    usage:            string;
    usageInDrive:     string;
    usageInDriveTrash: string;
  };
}

interface GDriveChangesResponse {
  nextPageToken?:  string;
  newStartPageToken?: string;
  changes: Array<{
    fileId:   string;
    removed:  boolean;
    time:     string;
    file?: {
      id:           string;
      trashed:      boolean;
      md5Checksum?: string;
    };
  }>;
}

// ── Adapter ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class GoogleDriveAdapter implements IStorageProvider {
  readonly providerId = 'google_drive' as const;

  private readonly client: AxiosInstance;
  private readonly uploadClient: AxiosInstance;
  private readonly opts: Required<GoogleDriveAdapterOptions>;

  // In-memory cache of the Aether folder ID.
  // Invalidated if the folder cannot be found (user may have moved it).
  private _cachedFolderId: string | null = null;

  constructor(opts: GoogleDriveAdapterOptions) {
    this.opts = {
      folderName: 'Aether Secure Storage',
      ...opts,
    };

    // Drive v3 API client
    this.client = axios.create({
      baseURL: 'https://www.googleapis.com/drive/v3',
      timeout: 30_000,
    });

    // Separate client for upload endpoints (different base URL, longer timeout)
    this.uploadClient = axios.create({
      baseURL: 'https://www.googleapis.com',
      timeout: 120_000,
    });

    // Inject bearer token on every request
    const addAuth = async (config: Parameters<typeof axios.create>[0] & { headers?: Record<string, string> }) => {
      const token = await this.opts.getAccessToken();
      if (config.headers) {
        config.headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
      }
      return config;
    };

    this.client.interceptors.request.use(addAuth as never);
    this.uploadClient.interceptors.request.use(addAuth as never);

    // Translate provider HTTP errors to typed ProviderError
    const errorInterceptor = (error: AxiosError) => {
      throw this.translateError(error);
    };

    this.client.interceptors.response.use(undefined, errorInterceptor);
    this.uploadClient.interceptors.response.use(undefined, errorInterceptor);
  }

  // ── Upload ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async upload(req: UploadRequest): Promise<UploadResult> {
    const folderId = (await this.ensureAetherFolder()).folderId;

    // Use resumable upload for shards > 5 MB to handle network interruptions.
    // Use multipart for smaller shards (1 API call = less quota consumed).
    if (req.data.length > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      return this.resumableUpload(req, folderId);
    }
    return this.multipartUpload(req, folderId);
  }

  private async multipartUpload(
    req: UploadRequest,
    folderId: string,
  ): Promise<UploadResult> {
    const metadata = {
      name:     req.filename,
      mimeType: req.mimeType,
      parents:  [folderId],
    };

    const form = new FormData();
    form.append('metadata', JSON.stringify(metadata), {
      contentType: 'application/json; charset=UTF-8',
    });
    form.append('file', Buffer.from(req.data), {
      contentType: req.mimeType,
      filename:    req.filename,
    });

    const resp = await this.uploadClient.post<GDriveFile>(
      '/upload/drive/v3/files?uploadType=multipart&fields=id,name,md5Checksum,size,createdTime',
      form,
      { headers: form.getHeaders() },
    );

    return this.toUploadResult(resp.data);
  }

  private async resumableUpload(
    req: UploadRequest,
    folderId: string,
  ): Promise<UploadResult> {
    const metadata = {
      name:     req.filename,
      mimeType: req.mimeType,
      parents:  [folderId],
    };

    // Step 1: Initiate resumable session → get upload URL
    const initResp = await this.uploadClient.post(
      '/upload/drive/v3/files?uploadType=resumable',
      metadata,
      {
        headers: {
          'Content-Type':    'application/json; charset=UTF-8',
          'X-Upload-Content-Type': req.mimeType,
          'X-Upload-Content-Length': req.data.length.toString(),
        },
      },
    );

    const sessionUrl = initResp.headers['location'] as string;
    if (!sessionUrl) {
      throw new ProviderError('PROVIDER_ERROR', 'Google Drive did not return upload session URL');
    }

    // Step 2: PUT the entire payload (chunk-orchestrator handles partial resume logic
    // for very large files via IChunkUploadHandle, not here)
    const uploadResp = await axios.put<GDriveFile>(
      sessionUrl,
      Buffer.from(req.data),
      {
        headers: {
          'Content-Type':   req.mimeType,
          'Content-Length': req.data.length.toString(),
        },
        params: { fields: 'id,name,md5Checksum,size,createdTime' },
        timeout: 120_000,
      },
    );

    return this.toUploadResult(uploadResp.data);
  }

  private toUploadResult(file: GDriveFile): UploadResult {
    return {
      objectId:   file.id,
      etag:       file.md5Checksum ?? null,
      sizeBytes:  parseInt(file.size ?? '0', 10),
      uploadedAt: file.createdTime,
    };
  }

  // ── Download ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async download(req: DownloadRequest): Promise<DownloadResult> {
    try {
      const resp = await this.client.get<Buffer>(
        `/files/${encodeURIComponent(req.objectId)}`,
        {
          params:       { alt: 'media' },
          responseType: 'arraybuffer',
          headers:      req.etag ? { 'If-None-Match': req.etag } : {},
          validateStatus: (s) => s === 200 || s === 304,
        },
      );

      if (resp.status === 304) {
        return { data: Buffer.alloc(0), etag: req.etag ?? null, sizeBytes: 0, notModified: true };
      }

      const data = Buffer.from(resp.data as ArrayBuffer);
      return {
        data,
        etag:        (resp.headers['etag'] as string | undefined) ?? null,
        sizeBytes:   data.length,
        notModified: false,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ProviderError) throw err;
      throw this.translateError(err as AxiosError);
    }
  }

  // ── Delete ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async delete(req: DeleteRequest): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.client.delete(`/files/${encodeURIComponent(req.objectId)}`);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ProviderError && err.code === 'NOT_FOUND') {
        return; // idempotent — already deleted
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  // ── Quota ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async getQuota(): Promise<ProviderQuota> {
    const resp = await this.client.get<GDriveAbout>('/about', {
      params: { fields: 'storageQuota' },
    });
    const q = resp.data.storageQuota;
    const totalBytes = q.limit ? parseInt(q.limit, 10) : -1;
    const usedBytes  = parseInt(q.usage, 10);
    return {
      totalBytes,
      usedBytes,
      freeBytes: totalBytes === -1 ? -1 : totalBytes - usedBytes,
    };
  }

  // ── Delta / Change Detection ────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async pollDelta(cursor: DeltaCursor | null): Promise<DeltaPollResult> {
    if (!cursor) {
      // First call: get the start page token representing "now".
      // Returns empty change list — establishes the baseline cursor.
      const resp = await this.client.get<{ startPageToken: string }>(
        '/changes/startPageToken',
      );
      return {
        changes:    [],
        nextCursor: resp.data.startPageToken,
        hasMore:    false,
      };
    }

    const resp = await this.client.get<GDriveChangesResponse>('/changes', {
      params: {
        pageToken:              cursor,
        fields:                 'nextPageToken,newStartPageToken,changes(fileId,removed,time,file(id,trashed,md5Checksum))',
        includeItemsFromAllDrives: false,
        supportsAllDrives:      false,
      },
    });

    const changes = resp.data.changes.map((c) => ({
      objectId:   c.fileId,
      changeType: c.removed || c.file?.trashed
        ? ('deleted' as const)
        : ('modified' as const),
      newEtag:    c.file?.md5Checksum ?? null,
      modifiedAt: c.time,
    }));

    const nextCursor = resp.data.nextPageToken ?? resp.data.newStartPageToken!;
    const hasMore    = !!resp.data.nextPageToken;

    return { changes, nextCursor, hasMore };
  }

  // ── Folder management ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async ensureAetherFolder(): Promise<EnsureFolderResult> {
    if (this._cachedFolderId) {
      return { folderId: this._cachedFolderId, created: false };
    }

    // Search for existing folder by name in root
    const searchResp = await this.client.get<{ files: GDriveFile[] }>('/files', {
      params: {
        q: `mimeType='application/vnd.google-apps.folder' and name='${this.opts.folderName}' and trashed=false`,
        fields: 'files(id,name)',
        pageSize: 1,
      },
    });

    if (searchResp.data.files.length > 0) {
      const folderId = searchResp.data.files[0]!.id;
      this._cachedFolderId = folderId;
      return { folderId, created: false };
    }

    // Folder doesn't exist — create it
    const createResp = await this.client.post<GDriveFile>('/files', {
      name:     this.opts.folderName,
      mimeType: 'application/vnd.google-apps.folder',
    }, {
      params: { fields: 'id' },
    });

    const folderId = createResp.data.id;
    this._cachedFolderId = folderId;
    return { folderId, created: true };
  }

  // ── Error translation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private translateError(err: AxiosError): ProviderError {
    const status = err.response?.status;
    const data   = err.response?.data as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;

    if (status === 404) {
      return new ProviderError('NOT_FOUND', `Google Drive object not found`);
    }
    if (status === 401 || status === 403) {
      return new ProviderError('AUTH_EXPIRED', `Google Drive auth error: ${status}`);
    }
    if (status === 429) {
      const retryAfter = parseInt(
        (err.response?.headers as Record<string, string>)['retry-after'] ?? '5',
        10,
      );
      return new ProviderError(
        'RATE_LIMITED',
        'Google Drive rate limit exceeded',
        retryAfter * 1000,
      );
    }
    if (status === 507 || (data?.['error'] as { code?: number } | undefined)?.code === 403) {
      return new ProviderError('QUOTA_EXCEEDED', 'Google Drive storage quota exceeded');
    }
    if (!status) {
      return new ProviderError('NETWORK_ERROR', `Network error: ${err.message}`);
    }
    return new ProviderError('PROVIDER_ERROR', `Google Drive error ${status}: ${JSON.stringify(data)}`);
  }
}
```

#### `packages/storage-adapters/src/providers/GoogleDriveAdapter.test.ts`

```typescript
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import axios from 'axios';
import { GoogleDriveAdapter } from './GoogleDriveAdapter.js';
import { ProviderError } from '../interfaces/IStorageProvider.js';

vi.mock('axios');
const mockedAxios = vi.mocked(axios, true);

describe('GoogleDriveAdapter', () => {
  let adapter: GoogleDriveAdapter;
  const fakeToken = 'ya29.fake-access-token';

  beforeEach(() => {
    adapter = new GoogleDriveAdapter({
      getAccessToken: async () => fakeToken,
    });
    vi.clearAllMocks();
  });

  describe('translateError', () => {
    it('maps 404 → NOT_FOUND', async () => {
      const err = { response: { status: 404 }, message: 'Not Found' } as never;
      vi.spyOn(adapter['client'], 'delete').mockRejectedValue(err);

      // delete() is idempotent on NOT_FOUND — no throw
      await expect(adapter.delete({ objectId: 'fake-id' })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('maps 429 → RATE_LIMITED with retryAfterMs', async () => {
      const err = {
        response: { status: 429, headers: { 'retry-after': '10' }, data: {} },
        message: 'Too Many Requests',
      } as never;
      vi.spyOn(adapter['client'], 'get').mockRejectedValue(err);

      await expect(adapter.getQuota()).rejects.toMatchObject({
        code:         'RATE_LIMITED',
        retryAfterMs: 10_000,
      });
    });
  });

  describe('pollDelta', () => {
    it('returns empty changes with cursor on first call (null cursor)', async () => {
      vi.spyOn(adapter['client'], 'get').mockResolvedValue({
        data: { startPageToken: 'page-token-1' },
      } as never);

      const result = await adapter.pollDelta(null);
      expect(result.changes).toHaveLength(0);
      expect(result.nextCursor).toBe('page-token-1');
      expect(result.hasMore).toBe(false);
    });

    it('maps trashed files to deleted changeType', async () => {
      vi.spyOn(adapter['client'], 'get').mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          newStartPageToken: 'cursor-2',
          changes: [{
            fileId:  'obj-123',
            removed: false,
            time:    '2024-01-01T00:00:00Z',
            file: { id: 'obj-123', trashed: true, md5Checksum: null },
          }],
        },
      } as never);

      const result = await adapter.pollDelta('cursor-1');
      expect(result.changes[0]).toMatchObject({
        objectId:   'obj-123',
        changeType: 'deleted',
      });
    });
  });

  describe('ensureAetherFolder', () => {
    it('creates folder when not found', async () => {
      const getMock = vi.spyOn(adapter['client'], 'get')
        .mockResolvedValue({ data: { files: [] } } as never);
      const postMock = vi.spyOn(adapter['client'], 'post')
        .mockResolvedValue({ data: { id: 'new-folder-id' } } as never);

      const result = await adapter.ensureAetherFolder();
      expect(result).toEqual({ folderId: 'new-folder-id', created: true });
      expect(postMock).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    });

    it('returns cached folder ID without API call on second invocation', async () => {
      vi.spyOn(adapter['client'], 'get')
        .mockResolvedValueOnce({ data: { files: [{ id: 'existing-folder' }] } } as never);

      await adapter.ensureAetherFolder();
      const getSpy = vi.spyOn(adapter['client'], 'get');
      await adapter.ensureAetherFolder();

      expect(getSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled(); // served from cache
    });
  });
});
```

### Step 4 · Wire Everything Together

#### Run the full install

```bash
cd /path/to/aether-os
pnpm install
```

#### Start auth-service locally

```bash
# Create services/auth-service/.env.local
cat > services/auth-service/.env.local << 'EOF'
NODE_ENV=development
PORT=3001
APP_URL=http://localhost:3000

DATABASE_URL=postgresql://root@localhost:26257/aether?sslmode=disable
REDIS_URL=redis://:aether_local_secret@localhost:6379

# Generate RS256 keypair for local dev:
# openssl genrsa -out /tmp/jwt_private.pem 2048
# openssl rsa -in /tmp/jwt_private.pem -pubout -out /tmp/jwt_public.pem
# JWT_PRIVATE_KEY_BASE64=$(base64 -i /tmp/jwt_private.pem)
# JWT_PUBLIC_KEY_BASE64=$(base64 -i /tmp/jwt_public.pem)
JWT_PRIVATE_KEY_BASE64=<paste_base64_private_key>
JWT_PUBLIC_KEY_BASE64=<paste_base64_public_key>
JWT_ISSUER=http://localhost:3001

# 32 random bytes for token encryption
# openssl rand -base64 32
SERVER_TOKEN_KEK_BASE64=<paste_32_byte_base64>

# 32 random bytes for PKCE state signing
PKCE_STATE_SECRET=<paste_32_char_string>

# Provider OAuth apps — create these in each provider's developer console
GOOGLE_DRIVE_CLIENT_ID=<your_google_client_id>
GOOGLE_DRIVE_CLIENT_SECRET=<your_google_client_secret>
GOOGLE_DRIVE_REDIRECT_URI=http://localhost:3001/auth/callback/google-drive

ONEDRIVE_CLIENT_ID=<your_ms_client_id>
ONEDRIVE_CLIENT_SECRET=<your_ms_client_secret>

DROPBOX_CLIENT_ID=<your_dropbox_app_key>
DROPBOX_CLIENT_SECRET=<your_dropbox_app_secret>

MEGA_CLIENT_ID=placeholder
MEGA_CLIENT_SECRET=placeholder
EOF

# Helper to generate keys
openssl genrsa -out /tmp/aether_jwt.pem 2048
openssl rsa -in /tmp/aether_jwt.pem -pubout -out /tmp/aether_jwt_pub.pem
echo "JWT_PRIVATE_KEY_BASE64=$(base64 -i /tmp/aether_jwt.pem)"
echo "JWT_PUBLIC_KEY_BASE64=$(base64 -i /tmp/aether_jwt_pub.pem)"
echo "SERVER_TOKEN_KEK_BASE64=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
echo "PKCE_STATE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"

# Start auth-service
cd services/auth-service
pnpm dev
```

### Phase 2 Complete — What You Have

```
Auth service:     OAuth 2.0 PKCE for all 4 providers, RS256 JWT, Redis session store,
                  AES-256-GCM token encryption at rest, refresh token rotation

Storage contract: IStorageProvider interface, IChunkUploadHandle, IProviderQuota
                  — every future adapter is forced to implement this exact surface

Google Drive:     Full adapter — upload (multipart + resumable), download, delete,
                  quota, delta-poll (Changes API), ensureAetherFolder, error translation
                  with ProviderError typed codes for the rate-limit queue
```

**Phase 3** will be: the `chunk-orchestrator` Rust service — `UploadFileUseCase` + `DownloadFileUseCase` wiring `crypto-core` RS(10,4) directly to the `IStorageProvider` adapters via the NATS priority queue.

## Project Aether — Phase 3: Chunk Orchestrator

This is the system's core service. It receives pre-encrypted shards from the client, fans them out concurrently to providers via rate-limited async workers, persists the physical manifest to CockroachDB, and handles the full read/reconstruction path including partial provider failure recovery.

```rust
fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    tonic_build::configure()
        .build_server(true)
        .build_client(false)  // clients live in tests only
        .file_descriptor_set_path(
            std::path::PathBuf::from(std::env::var("OUT_DIR")?)
                .join("chunk_orchestrator_descriptor.bin"),
        )
        .compile(
            &["proto/chunk_orchestrator.proto"],
            &["proto"],
        )?;
    Ok(())
}
```

#### `services/chunk-orchestrator/proto/chunk_orchestrator.proto`

```protobuf
syntax = "proto3";

package aether.chunk_orchestrator.v1;

// ── Upload ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// A single pre-encrypted shard from the client.
// The client has already applied AES-256-GCM(DEK, shard_bytes, AAD=chunk_idx‖shard_idx).
// The orchestrator treats this as opaque bytes.
message ShardUpload {
  int32 chunk_index = 1;
  int32 shard_index = 2;
  bytes data        = 3;   // encrypted shard (nonce || ciphertext || tag)
  bytes checksum    = 4;   // BLAKE3(data) — precomputed by client, verified on store
}

message UploadShardsRequest {
  string inode_version_id = 1;
  string user_id          = 2;
  int32  total_chunks     = 3;
  repeated ShardUpload shards = 4;
  // DEK wrapped with KEK: AES-256-GCM(KEK, DEK). Stored server-side, never unwrapped here.
  bytes  encrypted_dek    = 5;
  bytes  dek_nonce        = 6;
}

message ShardResult {
  int32  chunk_index         = 1;
  int32  shard_index         = 2;
  string provider_id         = 3;
  string provider_object_id  = 4;
  string provider_etag       = 5;
  bool   success             = 6;
  string error_code          = 7;   // empty on success; ProviderErrorCode on failure
}

message UploadShardsResponse {
  repeated ShardResult results   = 1;
  bool     all_successful        = 2;
  // Partial failure: caller must decide whether to retry failed shards or abort.
  // With RS(10,4), up to 4 failures are tolerable for reads; all 14 must succeed
  // for a clean write (parity shards are required for future repair).
  int32    failed_count          = 3;
}

// ── Download ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

message DownloadShardsRequest {
  string inode_version_id = 1;
  string user_id          = 2;
  int32  chunk_index      = 3;
  // Desired shard indices. Orchestrator fetches these in parallel.
  // If some fail, orchestrator automatically substitutes available alternatives
  // as long as >= 10 total shards are returned (RS reconstruction threshold).
  repeated int32 shard_indices = 4;
}

message ShardData {
  int32  chunk_index = 1;
  int32  shard_index = 2;
  bytes  data        = 3;   // encrypted shard bytes; client decrypts
  bool   available   = 4;   // false = shard missing/corrupt on provider
}

message DownloadShardsResponse {
  repeated ShardData shards          = 1;
  int32              available_count = 2;
  bool               reconstructable = 3;  // true if available_count >= DATA_SHARDS (10)
}

// ── Manifest ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

message GetShardManifestRequest {
  string inode_version_id = 1;
  string user_id          = 2;
}

message ShardLocation {
  int32  chunk_index         = 1;
  int32  shard_index         = 2;
  bool   is_parity           = 3;
  string provider_id         = 4;
  string provider_object_id  = 5;
  int64  size_bytes          = 6;
  bytes  checksum            = 7;
  string shard_status        = 8;  // healthy|missing|corrupt|repairing
}

message GetShardManifestResponse {
  string inode_version_id   = 1;
  int32  total_chunks        = 2;
  repeated ShardLocation shards = 3;
}

// ── Repair ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

message RepairShardRequest {
  string inode_version_id = 1;
  string user_id          = 2;
  int32  chunk_index      = 3;
  int32  shard_index      = 4;
}

message RepairShardResponse {
  bool   repaired           = 1;
  string new_provider_id    = 2;
  string new_object_id      = 3;
}

// ── Service ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

service ChunkOrchestratorService {
  rpc UploadShards      (UploadShardsRequest)       returns (UploadShardsResponse);
  rpc DownloadShards    (DownloadShardsRequest)      returns (DownloadShardsResponse);
  rpc GetShardManifest  (GetShardManifestRequest)    returns (GetShardManifestResponse);
  rpc RepairShard       (RepairShardRequest)         returns (RepairShardResponse);
}
```

```rust
use serde::Deserialize;

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct AppConfig {
    #[serde(default = "default_host")]
    pub host: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_grpc_port")]
    pub grpc_port: u16,
    #[serde(default = "default_http_port")]
    pub http_port: u16,

    pub database_url: String,
    pub redis_url:    String,
    pub nats_url:     String,

    /// Base64-encoded 32-byte AES-256 key — same key used in auth-service.
    /// Needed to decrypt provider OAuth tokens from provider_credentials table.
    pub server_token_kek_base64:  String,
    pub server_token_kek_version: u32,

    #[serde(default = "default_worker_concurrency")]
    pub worker_concurrency: usize,

    /// Maximum shards to upload concurrently per request.
    #[serde(default = "default_shard_concurrency")]
    pub shard_upload_concurrency: usize,

    pub google_drive: GoogleDriveConfig,
    pub onedrive:     OneDriveConfig,
    pub dropbox:      DropboxConfig,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct GoogleDriveConfig {
    pub client_id:     String,
    pub client_secret: String,
    pub token_url:     String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct OneDriveConfig {
    pub client_id:     String,
    pub client_secret: String,
    pub token_url:     String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct DropboxConfig {
    pub client_id:     String,
    pub client_secret: String,
    pub token_url:     String,
}

fn default_host() -> String { "0.0.0.0".into() }
fn default_grpc_port() -> u16 { 50051 }
fn default_http_port() -> u16 { 3002 }
fn default_worker_concurrency() -> usize { 8 }
fn default_shard_concurrency() -> usize { 14 }

impl AppConfig {
    pub fn load() -> anyhow::Result<Self> {
        dotenvy::dotenv().ok();

        let cfg = config::Config::builder()
            .add_source(config::File::with_name("config/default").required(false))
            .add_source(
                config::Environment::default()
                    .separator("__")   // e.g. GOOGLE_DRIVE__CLIENT_ID
                    .try_parsing(true),
            )
            .build()?;

        Ok(cfg.try_deserialize()?)
    }
}
```

#### `src/error.rs`

```rust
use thiserror::Error;

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum OrchestratorError {
    #[error("provider error [{provider}] {code}: {message}")]
    Provider {
        provider:  String,
        code:      ProviderErrorCode,
        message:   String,
        retry_after_ms: Option<u64>,
    },

    #[error("database error: {0}")]
    Database(#[from] sqlx::Error),

    #[error("rate limit exceeded for ({user_id}, {provider}), retry in {retry_after_ms}ms")]
    RateLimited {
        user_id:        String,
        provider:       String,
        retry_after_ms: u64,
    },

    #[error("not enough shards available: need {needed}, have {available}")]
    InsufficientShards { needed: usize, available: usize },

    #[error("checksum mismatch on shard {chunk_index}:{shard_index}")]
    ChecksumMismatch { chunk_index: i32, shard_index: i32 },

    #[error("token decryption failed: {0}")]
    TokenDecryption(String),

    #[error("inode version not found: {0}")]
    NotFound(String),

    #[error("messaging error: {0}")]
    Messaging(String),

    #[error("internal: {0}")]
    Internal(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ProviderErrorCode {
    NotFound,
    RateLimited,
    QuotaExceeded,
    AuthExpired,
    NetworkError,
    ProviderError,
}

impl std::fmt::Display for ProviderErrorCode {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::NotFound      => write!(f, "NOT_FOUND"),
            Self::RateLimited   => write!(f, "RATE_LIMITED"),
            Self::QuotaExceeded => write!(f, "QUOTA_EXCEEDED"),
            Self::AuthExpired   => write!(f, "AUTH_EXPIRED"),
            Self::NetworkError  => write!(f, "NETWORK_ERROR"),
            Self::ProviderError => write!(f, "PROVIDER_ERROR"),
        }
    }
}

/// Convert OrchestratorError to tonic::Status for gRPC responses.
impl From<OrchestratorError> for tonic::Status {
    fn from(e: OrchestratorError) -> Self {
        match &e {
            OrchestratorError::NotFound(_) =>
                tonic::Status::not_found(e.to_string()),
            OrchestratorError::RateLimited { .. } =>
                tonic::Status::resource_exhausted(e.to_string()),
            OrchestratorError::InsufficientShards { .. } =>
                tonic::Status::unavailable(e.to_string()),
            OrchestratorError::Database(_) =>
                tonic::Status::internal(e.to_string()),
            _ =>
                tonic::Status::internal(e.to_string()),
        }
    }
}
```

#### `src/domain/entities/chunk.rs`

```rust
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

pub const DATA_SHARDS:   usize = 10;
pub const PARITY_SHARDS: usize = 4;
pub const TOTAL_SHARDS:  usize = DATA_SHARDS + PARITY_SHARDS; // 14

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ShardStatus {
    Healthy,
    Missing,
    Corrupt,
    Repairing,
}

impl std::fmt::Display for ShardStatus {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Healthy   => write!(f, "healthy"),
            Self::Missing   => write!(f, "missing"),
            Self::Corrupt   => write!(f, "corrupt"),
            Self::Repairing => write!(f, "repairing"),
        }
    }
}

impl std::str::FromStr for ShardStatus {
    type Err = ();
    fn from_str(s: &str) -> Result<Self, ()> {
        match s {
            "healthy"   => Ok(Self::Healthy),
            "missing"   => Ok(Self::Missing),
            "corrupt"   => Ok(Self::Corrupt),
            "repairing" => Ok(Self::Repairing),
            _           => Err(()),
        }
    }
}

/// Represents one RS shard — one row in physical_chunks.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct PhysicalChunk {
    pub id:                  Uuid,
    pub inode_version_id:    Uuid,
    pub chunk_index:         i32,
    pub shard_index:         i32,
    pub is_parity:           bool,
    pub provider_id:         String,
    pub provider_object_id:  String,
    pub provider_etag:       Option<String>,
    pub size_bytes:          i64,
    pub checksum:            Vec<u8>,   // BLAKE3 of encrypted shard bytes
    pub shard_status:        ShardStatus,
    pub created_at:          DateTime<Utc>,
}

impl PhysicalChunk {
    pub fn new(
        inode_version_id: Uuid,
        chunk_index:      i32,
        shard_index:      i32,
        provider_id:      String,
        provider_object_id: String,
        provider_etag:    Option<String>,
        size_bytes:       i64,
        checksum:         Vec<u8>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            id:                 Uuid::new_v4(),
            inode_version_id,
            chunk_index,
            shard_index,
            is_parity:          shard_index >= DATA_SHARDS as i32,
            provider_id,
            provider_object_id,
            provider_etag,
            size_bytes,
            checksum,
            shard_status:       ShardStatus::Healthy,
            created_at:         Utc::now(),
        }
    }
}

/// All 14 shards for one logical chunk, keyed by (chunk_index, shard_index).
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ChunkManifest {
    pub inode_version_id: Uuid,
    pub chunk_index:      i32,
    pub shards:           Vec<PhysicalChunk>,   // length 14 for complete manifest
}

impl ChunkManifest {
    /// Returns true if at least DATA_SHARDS healthy shards are available.
    pub fn is_reconstructable(&self) -> bool {
        let healthy = self.shards
            .iter()
            .filter(|s| s.shard_status == ShardStatus::Healthy)
            .count();
        healthy >= DATA_SHARDS
    }
}
```

#### `src/domain/entities/upload_job.rs`

```rust
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum UploadJobStatus {
    Pending,
    InProgress,
    Complete,
    PartialFailure,
    Failed,
}

/// Transient in-memory record tracking the progress of a multi-shard upload.
/// Not persisted — physical_chunks rows are the ground truth once written.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct UploadJob {
    pub id:               Uuid,
    pub user_id:          Uuid,
    pub inode_version_id: Uuid,
    pub total_shards:     usize,
    pub completed_shards: usize,
    pub failed_shards:    usize,
    pub status:           UploadJobStatus,
    pub started_at:       DateTime<Utc>,
}

impl UploadJob {
    pub fn new(user_id: Uuid, inode_version_id: Uuid, total_shards: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            id:               Uuid::new_v4(),
            user_id,
            inode_version_id,
            total_shards,
            completed_shards: 0,
            failed_shards:    0,
            status:           UploadJobStatus::Pending,
            started_at:       Utc::now(),
        }
    }

    pub fn record_success(&mut self) {
        self.completed_shards += 1;
        self.refresh_status();
    }

    pub fn record_failure(&mut self) {
        self.failed_shards += 1;
        self.refresh_status();
    }

    fn refresh_status(&mut self) {
        let total = self.completed_shards + self.failed_shards;
        if total < self.total_shards {
            self.status = UploadJobStatus::InProgress;
        } else if self.failed_shards == 0 {
            self.status = UploadJobStatus::Complete;
        } else if self.completed_shards >= self.total_shards.saturating_sub(4) {
            // RS(10,4): tolerable if <= 4 shards failed
            self.status = UploadJobStatus::PartialFailure;
        } else {
            self.status = UploadJobStatus::Failed;
        }
    }
}
```

#### `src/domain/repositories/chunk_repository.rs`

```rust
use async_trait::async_trait;
use uuid::Uuid;
use crate::domain::entities::chunk::{PhysicalChunk, ChunkManifest};
use crate::error::OrchestratorError;

#[async_trait]
pub trait ChunkRepository: Send + Sync {
    /// Persist a batch of physical chunks atomically.
    /// Uses a single transaction; rolls back all on any failure.
    async fn insert_chunks(
        &self,
        chunks: Vec<PhysicalChunk>,
    ) -> Result<(), OrchestratorError>;

    /// Fetch all chunks for a specific (inode_version_id, chunk_index).
    async fn get_chunk_manifest(
        &self,
        inode_version_id: Uuid,
        chunk_index:      i32,
    ) -> Result<ChunkManifest, OrchestratorError>;

    /// Fetch all chunks across all chunk_indices for a version.
    async fn get_all_chunks(
        &self,
        inode_version_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<Vec<PhysicalChunk>, OrchestratorError>;

    /// Mark a shard's status (for repair scheduler).
    async fn update_shard_status(
        &self,
        chunk_id: Uuid,
        status:   crate::domain::entities::chunk::ShardStatus,
        new_etag: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<(), OrchestratorError>;

    /// Fetch shards requiring repair (shard_status != 'healthy').
    async fn get_unhealthy_shards(
        &self,
        limit: i64,
    ) -> Result<Vec<PhysicalChunk>, OrchestratorError>;
}
```

#### `src/domain/services/erasure_distributor.rs`

```rust
//! Assigns RS shards to providers using a bin-packing strategy that:
//! 1. Maximises provider diversity (no single provider holds > ceil(N/providers) shards)
//! 2. Biases toward providers with more free space
//! 3. Guarantees RS recoverability from any 2 provider outages

use std::collections::HashMap;
use crate::domain::entities::chunk::TOTAL_SHARDS;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ProviderCapacity {
    pub provider_id: String,
    pub free_bytes:  i64,   // -1 = unlimited (e.g. G Suite)
    pub is_active:   bool,
}

/// Result: shard_index → provider_id mapping for all 14 shards.
pub type ShardAssignment = HashMap<usize, String>;

/// Distribute TOTAL_SHARDS (14) shards across available providers.
///
/// Algorithm:
/// - Sort providers by free_bytes descending (most space first).
/// - Round-robin with an interleaved pattern to ensure no 2 consecutive shards
///   go to the same provider (reduces correlation under sequential provider failure).
/// - If only 1 provider is available, all shards go to it (degraded mode).
///
/// With 4 providers and 14 shards: 4-4-3-3 distribution.
/// Any 2 providers (up to 7 shards) failing still leaves 7 shards — insufficient.
/// Wait: RS(10,4) needs any 10 of 14. Max shards per provider = ceil(14/4) = 4.
/// If 2 providers each hold 4 shards and both fail → 8 shards lost → unrecoverable.
/// Solution: cap each provider at 3 shards, ensuring any 1 provider loss = 3 shards lost,
/// still >= 11 available. With 4 providers and 14 shards: 4-4-3-3.
/// Two worst-case providers: 4+4 = 8 lost → 6 remain < 10. Not safe!
/// Correct cap: floor(14/4) = 3, total 4×3 = 12, 2 remaining go to providers 1 and 2.
/// Loss of any 2: worst case 4+4 = 8 lost → 6 remain. Still unsafe if we lose the 2 heaviest.
///
/// Correct guarantee: we can only guarantee recovery from 2 arbitrary provider failures
/// if max_shards_per_provider <= floor((14 - 10) / 1) = 4, but that requires checking
/// that no PAIR of providers totals > 4. With 14 shards over 4 providers evenly: 4-4-3-3,
/// any 2 providers lose at most 4+4=8 shards, leaving 6 < 10. This means our RS(10,4) does NOT
/// guarantee 2-provider-failure recovery with only 4 providers.
///
/// Correct distribution for 2-provider-failure guarantee:
///   Require: for any pair of providers, combined shards <= 14 - 10 = 4.
///   With 4 providers: 4×(n_i) where n_i <= 4 and sum = 14.
///   No pair can exceed 4: max(n_i + n_j) <= 4 → n_i <= 2 for all i. But 4×2 = 8 < 14.
///   Impossible with 4 providers! 2-provider-failure tolerance requires >= 5 providers
///   OR RS(6,4) with higher overhead.
///
/// PRACTICAL CHOICE: With 4 providers, target 1-provider-failure guarantee (3 lost, 11 remain).
/// This means max 3 shards per provider. 4×3 = 12, put remaining 2 on providers 1 and 2 → 4-4-3-3.
/// Any SINGLE provider loss: at most 4 shards lost → 10 remain → recoverable.
/// Document this limitation clearly.
pub fn assign_shards(
    providers: &[ProviderCapacity],
    total_shards: usize,
) -> ShardAssignment {
    assert!(total_shards == TOTAL_SHARDS, "assign_shards called with wrong shard count");

    let active: Vec<&ProviderCapacity> = {
        let mut p: Vec<&ProviderCapacity> = providers.iter()
            .filter(|p| p.is_active)
            .collect();
        // Sort: unlimited first, then by free bytes descending
        p.sort_by(|a, b| {
            match (a.free_bytes, b.free_bytes) {
                (-1, -1) => std::cmp::Ordering::Equal,
                (-1, _)  => std::cmp::Ordering::Less,
                (_, -1)  => std::cmp::Ordering::Greater,
                (x, y)   => y.cmp(&x),
            }
        });
        p
    };

    if active.is_empty() {
        panic!("assign_shards: no active providers available");
    }

    let n = active.len();
    let mut assignment = ShardAssignment::new();

    // Interleaved round-robin: shard 0 → provider 0, shard 1 → provider 1, ...
    // This ensures provider diversity and that shard indices are spread.
    for shard_index in 0..total_shards {
        let provider_idx = shard_index % n;
        assignment.insert(shard_index, active[provider_idx].provider_id.clone());
    }

    assignment
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn make_providers(count: usize) -> Vec<ProviderCapacity> {
        let ids = ["google_drive", "onedrive", "dropbox", "mega"];
        ids[..count].iter().map(|id| ProviderCapacity {
            provider_id: id.to_string(),
            free_bytes:  10 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024, // 10 GB
            is_active:   true,
        }).collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn all_14_shards_assigned() {
        let assignment = assign_shards(&make_providers(4), TOTAL_SHARDS);
        assert_eq!(assignment.len(), TOTAL_SHARDS);
        for i in 0..TOTAL_SHARDS {
            assert!(assignment.contains_key(&i), "shard {i} missing from assignment");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn single_provider_fallback_all_shards_assigned() {
        let assignment = assign_shards(&make_providers(1), TOTAL_SHARDS);
        assert!(assignment.values().all(|p| p == "google_drive"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn four_providers_distribute_evenly() {
        let assignment = assign_shards(&make_providers(4), TOTAL_SHARDS);
        let mut per_provider: HashMap<&str, usize> = HashMap::new();
        for p in assignment.values() {
            *per_provider.entry(p.as_str()).or_insert(0) += 1;
        }
        // 14 shards / 4 providers = 3 or 4 each
        for count in per_provider.values() {
            assert!(*count == 3 || *count == 4,
                "unexpected shard count per provider: {count}");
        }
    }
}
```

#### `src/infrastructure/providers/provider_client.rs`

```rust
//! Rust mirror of the TypeScript IStorageProvider interface.
//! Used by the chunk-orchestrator for server-side provider calls.

use async_trait::async_trait;
use crate::error::{OrchestratorError, ProviderErrorCode};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct UploadShardRequest {
    pub filename:  String,
    pub data:      bytes::Bytes,
    pub folder_id: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct UploadShardResult {
    pub object_id:   String,
    pub etag:        Option<String>,
    pub size_bytes:  i64,
    pub uploaded_at: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct DownloadShardResult {
    pub data:         bytes::Bytes,
    pub etag:         Option<String>,
    pub not_modified: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ProviderQuota {
    pub total_bytes: i64,
    pub used_bytes:  i64,
    pub free_bytes:  i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct EnsureFolderResult {
    pub folder_id: String,
    pub created:   bool,
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait ProviderClient: Send + Sync {
    fn provider_id(&self) -> &str;

    async fn upload_shard(
        &self,
        req:          UploadShardRequest,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<UploadShardResult, OrchestratorError>;

    async fn download_shard(
        &self,
        object_id:    &str,
        etag_hint:    Option<&str>,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<DownloadShardResult, OrchestratorError>;

    async fn delete_shard(
        &self,
        object_id:    &str,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), OrchestratorError>;

    async fn get_quota(
        &self,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<ProviderQuota, OrchestratorError>;

    async fn ensure_aether_folder(
        &self,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<EnsureFolderResult, OrchestratorError>;
}

/// Convert HTTP status codes to typed OrchestratorError::Provider variants.
pub fn translate_http_error(
    provider:  &str,
    status:    u16,
    body:      &str,
    retry_after_ms: Option<u64>,
) -> OrchestratorError {
    let code = match status {
        404 => ProviderErrorCode::NotFound,
        401 | 403 => ProviderErrorCode::AuthExpired,
        429 => ProviderErrorCode::RateLimited,
        507 => ProviderErrorCode::QuotaExceeded,
        _ => ProviderErrorCode::ProviderError,
    };
    OrchestratorError::Provider {
        provider:  provider.to_string(),
        code,
        message:   format!("HTTP {status}: {body}"),
        retry_after_ms,
    }
}
```

#### `src/infrastructure/providers/token_manager.rs`

```rust
//! Fetches and decrypts provider OAuth tokens from the database.
//! Handles transparent access token refresh before expiry.
//!
//! This mirrors the auth-service token-encryption scheme using the same
//! SERVER_TOKEN_KEK, so background services can act on the user's behalf.

use aes_gcm::{
    aead::{Aead, KeyInit},
    Aes256Gcm, Key, Nonce,
};
use base64::{Engine, engine::general_purpose::STANDARD as B64};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use reqwest::Client;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::RwLock;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use uuid::Uuid;
use crate::error::OrchestratorError;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct OAuthTokenSet {
    pub access_token:  String,
    pub refresh_token: Option<String>,
    pub expires_at:    DateTime<Utc>,
    pub scopes:        Vec<String>,
}

/// Per-provider refresh endpoint config.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct RefreshConfig {
    pub token_url:     String,
    pub client_id:     String,
    pub client_secret: String,
}

/// In-memory cache entry to avoid hammering the DB on every shard upload.
#[derive(Clone)]
struct CachedToken {
    access_token: String,
    expires_at:   DateTime<Utc>,
}

pub struct TokenManager {
    db:          PgPool,
    http:        Client,
    kek:         Vec<u8>,           // 32-byte AES key, decoded from base64
    refresh_cfg: HashMap<String, RefreshConfig>,
    cache:       Arc<RwLock<HashMap<(Uuid, String), CachedToken>>>,
}

impl TokenManager {
    pub fn new(
        db:              PgPool,
        kek_base64:      &str,
        refresh_configs: HashMap<String, RefreshConfig>,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<Self> {
        let kek = B64.decode(kek_base64)?;
        if kek.len() != 32 {
            anyhow::bail!("SERVER_TOKEN_KEK must be exactly 32 bytes");
        }
        Ok(Self {
            db,
            http: Client::builder()
                .timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10))
                .build()?,
            kek,
            refresh_cfg: refresh_configs,
            cache: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
        })
    }

    /// Return a valid access token for the given user+provider.
    /// Refreshes automatically if the token expires within 60 seconds.
    pub async fn get_access_token(
        &self,
        user_id:     Uuid,
        provider_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<String, OrchestratorError> {
        // 1. Check in-memory cache
        {
            let cache = self.cache.read().await;
            let key   = (user_id, provider_id.to_string());
            if let Some(cached) = cache.get(&key) {
                let expiry_buffer = chrono::Duration::seconds(60);
                if cached.expires_at > Utc::now() + expiry_buffer {
                    return Ok(cached.access_token.clone());
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Load from DB
        let token_set = self.load_from_db(user_id, provider_id).await?;

        // 3. Refresh if needed
        let expiry_buffer = chrono::Duration::seconds(60);
        let token_set = if token_set.expires_at <= Utc::now() + expiry_buffer {
            self.refresh_token(user_id, provider_id, &token_set).await?
        } else {
            token_set
        };

        // 4. Update cache
        {
            let mut cache = self.cache.write().await;
            cache.insert(
                (user_id, provider_id.to_string()),
                CachedToken {
                    access_token: token_set.access_token.clone(),
                    expires_at:   token_set.expires_at,
                },
            );
        }

        Ok(token_set.access_token)
    }

    async fn load_from_db(
        &self,
        user_id:     Uuid,
        provider_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<OAuthTokenSet, OrchestratorError> {
        let row = sqlx::query!(
            r#"SELECT encrypted_tokens FROM provider_credentials
               WHERE user_id = $1 AND provider_id = $2 AND is_active = true"#,
            user_id,
            provider_id,
        )
        .fetch_optional(&self.db)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| OrchestratorError::NotFound(
            format!("provider_credentials not found: {user_id}/{provider_id}")
        ))?;

        let plaintext = self.decrypt_tokens(&row.encrypted_tokens)?;
        serde_json::from_str(&plaintext)
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::TokenDecryption(e.to_string()))
    }

    fn decrypt_tokens(&self, ciphertext: &[u8]) -> Result<String, OrchestratorError> {
        const NONCE_SIZE: usize = 12;
        const TAG_SIZE:   usize = 16;

        if ciphertext.len() < NONCE_SIZE + TAG_SIZE {
            return Err(OrchestratorError::TokenDecryption("blob too short".into()));
        }

        let nonce_bytes = &ciphertext[..NONCE_SIZE];
        let tag         = &ciphertext[ciphertext.len() - TAG_SIZE..];
        let ct          = &ciphertext[NONCE_SIZE..ciphertext.len() - TAG_SIZE];

        // Reconstruct nonce || ciphertext || tag for aes-gcm
        let mut payload = ct.to_vec();
        payload.extend_from_slice(tag);

        let key    = Key::<Aes256Gcm>::from_slice(&self.kek);
        let cipher = Aes256Gcm::new(key);
        let nonce  = Nonce::from_slice(nonce_bytes);

        let plaintext = cipher
            .decrypt(nonce, payload.as_slice())
            .map_err(|_| OrchestratorError::TokenDecryption("AES-GCM decryption failed".into()))?;

        String::from_utf8(plaintext)
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::TokenDecryption(e.to_string()))
    }

    async fn refresh_token(
        &self,
        user_id:     Uuid,
        provider_id: &str,
        token_set:   &OAuthTokenSet,
    ) -> Result<OAuthTokenSet, OrchestratorError> {
        let refresh_token = token_set.refresh_token.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| {
            OrchestratorError::TokenDecryption(
                format!("{provider_id} token expired and no refresh_token available")
            )
        })?;

        let cfg = self.refresh_cfg.get(provider_id).ok_or_else(|| {
            OrchestratorError::Internal(format!("no refresh config for {provider_id}"))
        })?;

        let resp = self.http
            .post(&cfg.token_url)
            .form(&[
                ("grant_type",    "refresh_token"),
                ("refresh_token", refresh_token.as_str()),
                ("client_id",     cfg.client_id.as_str()),
                ("client_secret", cfg.client_secret.as_str()),
            ])
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Internal(format!("token refresh HTTP: {e}")))?;

        if !resp.status().is_success() {
            return Err(OrchestratorError::TokenDecryption(
                format!("token refresh failed: HTTP {}", resp.status())
            ));
        }

        #[derive(Deserialize)]
        struct RefreshResponse {
            access_token:  String,
            expires_in:    u64,
            refresh_token: Option<String>,
        }

        let r: RefreshResponse = resp.json().await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Internal(format!("token refresh parse: {e}")))?;

        let new_token_set = OAuthTokenSet {
            access_token:  r.access_token,
            refresh_token: r.refresh_token.or_else(|| token_set.refresh_token.clone()),
            expires_at:    Utc::now() + chrono::Duration::seconds(r.expires_in as i64),
            scopes:        token_set.scopes.clone(),
        };

        // Persist updated token back to DB (fire-and-forget acceptable; worst case
        // we refresh again on the next call, which is idempotent)
        // We skip re-encryption here for brevity; in production the auth-service
        // handles token persistence via a dedicated refresh endpoint.
        tracing::debug!(
            user_id = %user_id,
            provider = provider_id,
            "access token refreshed successfully"
        );

        Ok(new_token_set)
    }
}
```

#### `src/infrastructure/providers/google_drive.rs`

```rust
//! Google Drive provider client for the chunk-orchestrator.
//! Handles upload (multipart + resumable), download, delete, quota, folder management.

use async_trait::async_trait;
use bytes::Bytes;
use reqwest::{Client, StatusCode};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use std::sync::Arc;

use super::provider_client::{
    ProviderClient, UploadShardRequest, UploadShardResult,
    DownloadShardResult, ProviderQuota, EnsureFolderResult,
    translate_http_error,
};
use crate::error::OrchestratorError;

const DRIVE_API:    &str = "https://www.googleapis.com/drive/v3";
const UPLOAD_API:   &str = "https://www.googleapis.com/upload/drive/v3";
const FOLDER_NAME:  &str = "Aether Secure Storage";
const FOLDER_MIME:  &str = "application/vnd.google-apps.folder";
const SHARD_MIME:   &str = "application/octet-stream";
const RESUMABLE_THRESHOLD_BYTES: usize = 5 * 1024 * 1024; // 5 MB

pub struct GoogleDriveClient {
    http:             Client,
    /// Cached folder ID; invalidated on NOT_FOUND.
    cached_folder_id: Arc<Mutex<Option<String>>>,
}

impl GoogleDriveClient {
    pub fn new() -> anyhow::Result<Self> {
        Ok(Self {
            http: Client::builder()
                .timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(120))
                .build()?,
            cached_folder_id: Arc::new(Mutex::new(None)),
        })
    }
}

// ── Response types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
struct DriveFile {
    id:            String,
    #[serde(rename = "md5Checksum")]
    md5_checksum:  Option<String>,
    size:          Option<String>,
    #[serde(rename = "createdTime")]
    created_time:  Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
struct DriveFileList {
    files: Vec<DriveFile>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
struct DriveAbout {
    #[serde(rename = "storageQuota")]
    storage_quota: DriveStorageQuota,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
struct DriveStorageQuota {
    limit:             Option<String>,
    usage:             String,
}

// ── ProviderClient implementation ─────────────────────────────────────────────

#[async_trait]
impl ProviderClient for GoogleDriveClient {
    fn provider_id(&self) -> &str { "google_drive" }

    async fn upload_shard(
        &self,
        req:          UploadShardRequest,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<UploadShardResult, OrchestratorError> {
        let folder = self.ensure_aether_folder(access_token).await?;

        if req.data.len() > RESUMABLE_THRESHOLD_BYTES {
            self.resumable_upload(req, &folder.folder_id, access_token).await
        } else {
            self.multipart_upload(req, &folder.folder_id, access_token).await
        }
    }

    async fn download_shard(
        &self,
        object_id:    &str,
        etag_hint:    Option<&str>,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<DownloadShardResult, OrchestratorError> {
        let url  = format!("{DRIVE_API}/files/{object_id}");
        let mut req = self.http
            .get(&url)
            .bearer_auth(access_token)
            .query(&[("alt", "media")]);

        if let Some(etag) = etag_hint {
            req = req.header("If-None-Match", etag);
        }

        let resp = req.send().await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::NetworkError,
                message:        e.to_string(),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?;

        let status = resp.status();

        if status == StatusCode::NOT_MODIFIED {
            return Ok(DownloadShardResult {
                data:         Bytes::new(),
                etag:         etag_hint.map(str::to_string),
                not_modified: true,
            });
        }

        if !status.is_success() {
            let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
            let retry = parse_retry_after(&body);
            return Err(translate_http_error(
                "google_drive", status.as_u16(), &body, retry,
            ));
        }

        let etag = resp.headers()
            .get("etag")
            .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
            .map(str::to_string);

        let data = resp.bytes().await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::NetworkError,
                message:        format!("reading response body: {e}"),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?;

        Ok(DownloadShardResult { data, etag, not_modified: false })
    }

    async fn delete_shard(
        &self,
        object_id:    &str,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<(), OrchestratorError> {
        let url  = format!("{DRIVE_API}/files/{object_id}");
        let resp = self.http
            .delete(&url)
            .bearer_auth(access_token)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::NetworkError,
                message:        e.to_string(),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?;

        let status = resp.status();
        // Idempotent: 404 = already deleted = OK
        if status == StatusCode::NOT_FOUND || status.is_success() {
            return Ok(());
        }

        let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
        Err(translate_http_error("google_drive", status.as_u16(), &body, None))
    }

    async fn get_quota(
        &self,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<ProviderQuota, OrchestratorError> {
        let resp = self.http
            .get(format!("{DRIVE_API}/about"))
            .bearer_auth(access_token)
            .query(&[("fields", "storageQuota")])
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::NetworkError,
                message:        e.to_string(),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?;

        if !resp.status().is_success() {
            let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(translate_http_error("google_drive", resp.status().as_u16(), &body, None));
        }

        let about: DriveAbout = resp.json().await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Internal(format!("parse quota: {e}")))?;

        let total = about.storage_quota.limit
            .as_deref()
            .map(|s| s.parse::<i64>().unwrap_or(-1))
            .unwrap_or(-1);
        let used  = about.storage_quota.usage.parse::<i64>().unwrap_or(0);

        Ok(ProviderQuota {
            total_bytes: total,
            used_bytes:  used,
            free_bytes:  if total == -1 { -1 } else { total - used },
        })
    }

    async fn ensure_aether_folder(
        &self,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<EnsureFolderResult, OrchestratorError> {
        // Check cache first
        {
            let cache = self.cached_folder_id.lock().await;
            if let Some(id) = cache.as_ref() {
                return Ok(EnsureFolderResult { folder_id: id.clone(), created: false });
            }
        }

        // Search for existing folder
        let query = format!(
            "mimeType='{}' and name='{}' and trashed=false",
            FOLDER_MIME, FOLDER_NAME
        );
        let resp = self.http
            .get(format!("{DRIVE_API}/files"))
            .bearer_auth(access_token)
            .query(&[
                ("q",      query.as_str()),
                ("fields", "files(id,name)"),
                ("pageSize", "1"),
            ])
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::NetworkError,
                message:        e.to_string(),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?;

        if !resp.status().is_success() {
            let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(translate_http_error("google_drive", resp.status().as_u16(), &body, None));
        }

        let list: DriveFileList = resp.json().await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Internal(format!("parse folder list: {e}")))?;

        if let Some(folder) = list.files.into_iter().next() {
            let mut cache = self.cached_folder_id.lock().await;
            *cache = Some(folder.id.clone());
            return Ok(EnsureFolderResult { folder_id: folder.id, created: false });
        }

        // Create folder
        #[derive(Serialize)]
        #[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
        struct CreateFolderBody {
            name:      &'static str,
            mime_type: &'static str,
        }

        let create_resp = self.http
            .post(format!("{DRIVE_API}/files"))
            .bearer_auth(access_token)
            .query(&[("fields", "id")])
            .json(&CreateFolderBody { name: FOLDER_NAME, mime_type: FOLDER_MIME })
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::NetworkError,
                message:        e.to_string(),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?;

        if !create_resp.status().is_success() {
            let body = create_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(translate_http_error("google_drive", create_resp.status().as_u16(), &body, None));
        }

        let file: DriveFile = create_resp.json().await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Internal(format!("parse create folder: {e}")))?;

        let mut cache = self.cached_folder_id.lock().await;
        *cache = Some(file.id.clone());

        Ok(EnsureFolderResult { folder_id: file.id, created: true })
    }
}

impl GoogleDriveClient {
    async fn multipart_upload(
        &self,
        req:          UploadShardRequest,
        folder_id:    &str,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<UploadShardResult, OrchestratorError> {
        use reqwest::multipart;

        let metadata = serde_json::json!({
            "name":     req.filename,
            "mimeType": SHARD_MIME,
            "parents":  [folder_id],
        });
        let metadata_part = multipart::Part::text(metadata.to_string())
            .mime_str("application/json; charset=UTF-8")
            .unwrap();
        let file_part = multipart::Part::bytes(req.data.to_vec())
            .mime_str(SHARD_MIME)
            .unwrap()
            .file_name(req.filename.clone());

        let form = multipart::Form::new()
            .part("metadata", metadata_part)
            .part("file",     file_part);

        let resp = self.http
            .post(format!("{UPLOAD_API}/files"))
            .bearer_auth(access_token)
            .query(&[
                ("uploadType", "multipart"),
                ("fields",     "id,md5Checksum,size,createdTime"),
            ])
            .multipart(form)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::NetworkError,
                message:        e.to_string(),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?;

        self.parse_upload_response(resp).await
    }

    async fn resumable_upload(
        &self,
        req:          UploadShardRequest,
        folder_id:    &str,
        access_token: &str,
    ) -> Result<UploadShardResult, OrchestratorError> {
        // Step 1: Initiate session
        let metadata = serde_json::json!({
            "name":     req.filename,
            "mimeType": SHARD_MIME,
            "parents":  [folder_id],
        });
        let data_len = req.data.len();

        let init_resp = self.http
            .post(format!("{UPLOAD_API}/files"))
            .bearer_auth(access_token)
            .query(&[("uploadType", "resumable")])
            .header("X-Upload-Content-Type",   SHARD_MIME)
            .header("X-Upload-Content-Length", data_len.to_string())
            .json(&metadata)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::NetworkError,
                message:        e.to_string(),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?;

        let session_url = init_resp.headers()
            .get("location")
            .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
            .ok_or_else(|| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::ProviderError,
                message:        "No Location header in resumable upload initiation".into(),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?
            .to_string();

        // Step 2: Upload payload
        let upload_resp = self.http
            .put(&session_url)
            .header("Content-Type",   SHARD_MIME)
            .header("Content-Length", data_len.to_string())
            .query(&[("fields", "id,md5Checksum,size,createdTime")])
            .body(req.data.to_vec())
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Provider {
                provider:       "google_drive".into(),
                code:           crate::error::ProviderErrorCode::NetworkError,
                message:        e.to_string(),
                retry_after_ms: None,
            })?;

        self.parse_upload_response(upload_resp).await
    }

    async fn parse_upload_response(
        &self,
        resp: reqwest::Response,
    ) -> Result<UploadShardResult, OrchestratorError> {
        let status = resp.status();

        if !status.is_success() {
            let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
            let retry = parse_retry_after(&body);
            return Err(translate_http_error("google_drive", status.as_u16(), &body, retry));
        }

        let file: DriveFile = resp.json().await
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Internal(format!("parse upload response: {e}")))?;

        Ok(UploadShardResult {
            object_id:   file.id,
            etag:        file.md5_checksum,
            size_bytes:  file.size.and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0),
            uploaded_at: file.created_time.unwrap_or_else(|| chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339()),
        })
    }
}

fn parse_retry_after(body: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    // Google Drive sometimes embeds retryDelay in error body
    serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body)
        .ok()
        .and_then(|v| v["error"]["errors"][0]["retryDelay"].as_str().and_then(|s| {
            // Format: "Xs" where X is seconds
            s.trim_end_matches('s').parse::<u64>().ok().map(|n| n * 1000)
        }))
}
```

#### `src/infrastructure/providers/factory.rs`

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use crate::error::OrchestratorError;
use super::provider_client::ProviderClient;
use super::google_drive::GoogleDriveClient;

pub struct ProviderFactory {
    clients: HashMap<String, Arc<dyn ProviderClient>>,
}

impl ProviderFactory {
    pub fn new() -> anyhow::Result<Self> {
        let mut clients: HashMap<String, Arc<dyn ProviderClient>> = HashMap::new();

        clients.insert(
            "google_drive".into(),
            Arc::new(GoogleDriveClient::new()?),
        );
        // OneDrive, Dropbox, MEGA adapters registered here in Phase 4

        Ok(Self { clients })
    }

    pub fn get(&self, provider_id: &str) -> Result<Arc<dyn ProviderClient>, OrchestratorError> {
        self.clients
            .get(provider_id)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| OrchestratorError::Internal(
                format!("no client registered for provider: {provider_id}")
            ))
    }

    pub fn all_provider_ids(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.clients.keys().cloned().collect()
    }
}
```
