#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/config/index.ts`

```typescript
import { z } from 'zod';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';

const Schema = z.object({
  NODE_ENV:     z.enum(['development', 'test', 'production']).default('development'),
  DATABASE_URL: z.string().min(1),
  REDIS_URL:    z.string().min(1),
  NATS_URL:     z.string().min(1),

  // gRPC address of the chunk-orchestrator service
  CHUNK_ORCHESTRATOR_ADDR: z.string().default('localhost:50051'),

  // How often to poll each provider for each user (ms)
  POLL_INTERVAL_MS: z.coerce.number().default(30_000),

  // Max concurrent (user, provider) polling pairs
  POLL_CONCURRENCY: z.coerce.number().default(50),

  // Circuit breaker: consecutive errors before soft-blocking a (user, provider)
  CIRCUIT_BREAKER_THRESHOLD: z.coerce.number().default(5),
  CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS:  z.coerce.number().default(300_000), // 5 min

  // Provider OAuth clients (needed for token refresh)
  GOOGLE_DRIVE_CLIENT_ID:     z.string().min(1),
  GOOGLE_DRIVE_CLIENT_SECRET: z.string().min(1),
  ONEDRIVE_CLIENT_ID:         z.string().min(1),
  ONEDRIVE_CLIENT_SECRET:     z.string().min(1),
  DROPBOX_CLIENT_ID:          z.string().min(1),
  DROPBOX_CLIENT_SECRET:      z.string().min(1),

  SERVER_TOKEN_KEK_BASE64:  z.string().min(1),
});

function load() {
  const result = Schema.safeParse(process.env);
  if (!result.success) {
    console.error('❌ delta-sync-service: invalid env:');
    console.error(result.error.flatten().fieldErrors);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  return result.data;
}

export const env = load();
```

### Step 3 · Types

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/types/index.ts`

```typescript
export type ProviderId = 'google_drive' | 'onedrive' | 'dropbox' | 'mega';

// ── Delta change from provider ────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type ChangeType = 'created' | 'modified' | 'deleted';

export interface ProviderChange {
  objectId:   string;
  changeType: ChangeType;
  newEtag:    string | null;
  modifiedAt: string;  // ISO-8601
}

export interface DeltaPollResult {
  changes:    ProviderChange[];
  nextCursor: string;
  hasMore:    boolean;
}

// ── CRDT vector clock ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Lamport-clock-based vector clock per origin.
 * Origins: 'aether_client' | provider IDs
 * Monotonically increasing; never decrements.
 */
export type VectorClock = Record<string, number>;

export function tickClock(clock: VectorClock, origin: string): VectorClock {
  return { ...clock, [origin]: (clock[origin] ?? 0) + 1 };
}

export function mergeClock(a: VectorClock, b: VectorClock): VectorClock {
  const all = new Set([...Object.keys(a), ...Object.keys(b)]);
  const merged: VectorClock = {};
  for (const k of all) {
    merged[k] = Math.max(a[k] ?? 0, b[k] ?? 0);
  }
  return merged;
}

/**
 * Returns true if clock A happened-before clock B (A ≤ B component-wise).
 * i.e. B has observed all events A has observed.
 */
export function happensBefore(a: VectorClock, b: VectorClock): boolean {
  for (const [origin, ts] of Object.entries(a)) {
    if ((b[origin] ?? 0) < ts) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

// ── Inode event (CRDT log entry) ──────────────────────────────────────────────

export type InodeEventOp =
  | 'create'
  | 'rename'
  | 'move'
  | 'content_update'
  | 'delete'
  | 'restore'
  | 'provider_out_of_band_modify'
  | 'provider_out_of_band_delete';

export interface InodeEvent {
  id:            string;
  inodeId:       string;
  volumeId:      string;
  op:            InodeEventOp;
  lamportTs:     number;
  origin:        string;
  payload:       Record<string, unknown>;
  prevEventHash: Buffer | null;
  eventHash:     Buffer;
  signature:     Buffer | null;
  createdAt:     Date;
}

// ── Repair job ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type RepairTriggerReason =
  | 'oob_modify'
  | 'checksum_fail'
  | 'provider_404'
  | 'manual';

export interface RepairJob {
  id:                string;
  inodeVersionId:    string;
  chunkIndex:        number;
  shardIndex:        number;
  sourceProviderId:  string;
  sourceObjectId:    string;
  triggerReason:     RepairTriggerReason;
}

// ── Circuit breaker state (Redis-backed) ──────────────────────────────────────

export interface CircuitBreakerState {
  providerId:        string;
  userId:            string;
  consecutiveErrors: number;
  openedAt:          number | null;  // epoch ms; null = closed
  state:             'closed' | 'open' | 'half_open';
}
```

### Step 4 · Database Layer

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/db/index.ts`

```typescript
import pg from 'pg';
import { env } from '../config/index.js';

const { Pool } = pg;

export const db = new Pool({
  connectionString: env.DATABASE_URL,
  max:              20,
  idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000,
});

// ── Cursor persistence ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function getCursor(
  userId: string,
  providerId: string,
): Promise<string | null> {
  const { rows } = await db.query<{ cursor: string }>(
    `SELECT cursor FROM provider_sync_cursors
     WHERE user_id = $1 AND provider_id = $2`,
    [userId, providerId],
  );
  return rows[0]?.cursor ?? null;
}

export async function upsertCursor(
  userId:     string,
  providerId: string,
  cursor:     string,
): Promise<void> {
  await db.query(
    `INSERT INTO provider_sync_cursors
       (user_id, provider_id, cursor, last_polled_at, poll_count)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, now(), 1)
     ON CONFLICT (user_id, provider_id) DO UPDATE SET
       cursor         = EXCLUDED.cursor,
       last_polled_at = now(),
       poll_count     = provider_sync_cursors.poll_count + 1,
       consecutive_errors = 0`,
    [userId, providerId, cursor],
  );
}

export async function incrementCursorError(
  userId:     string,
  providerId: string,
): Promise<number> {
  const { rows } = await db.query<{ consecutive_errors: number }>(
    `INSERT INTO provider_sync_cursors
       (user_id, provider_id, cursor, consecutive_errors)
     VALUES ($1, $2, '', 1)
     ON CONFLICT (user_id, provider_id) DO UPDATE SET
       consecutive_errors = provider_sync_cursors.consecutive_errors + 1
     RETURNING consecutive_errors`,
    [userId, providerId],
  );
  return rows[0]?.consecutive_errors ?? 1;
}

// ── Physical chunk lookup (change → shard mapping) ───────────────────────────

export interface ChunkRow {
  id:              string;
  inodeVersionId:  string;
  chunkIndex:      number;
  shardIndex:      number;
  providerId:      string;
  providerObjectId: string;
  providerEtag:    string | null;
  checksum:        Buffer;
  shardStatus:     string;
}

export async function findChunksByProviderObject(
  providerId: string,
  objectIds:  string[],
): Promise<ChunkRow[]> {
  if (objectIds.length === 0) return [];

  const { rows } = await db.query<{
    id:                  string;
    inode_version_id:    string;
    chunk_index:         number;
    shard_index:         number;
    provider_id:         string;
    provider_object_id:  string;
    provider_etag:       string | null;
    checksum:            Buffer;
    shard_status:        string;
  }>(
    `SELECT id, inode_version_id, chunk_index, shard_index,
            provider_id, provider_object_id, provider_etag,
            checksum, shard_status
     FROM physical_chunks
     WHERE provider_id = $1
       AND provider_object_id = ANY($2::text[])
       AND shard_status != 'repairing'`,
    [providerId, objectIds],
  );

  return rows.map((r) => ({
    id:               r.id,
    inodeVersionId:   r.inode_version_id,
    chunkIndex:       r.chunk_index,
    shardIndex:       r.shard_index,
    providerId:       r.provider_id,
    providerObjectId: r.provider_object_id,
    providerEtag:     r.provider_etag,
    checksum:         r.checksum,
    shardStatus:      r.shard_status,
  }));
}

export async function markShardStatus(
  chunkId:    string,
  status:     'missing' | 'corrupt' | 'repairing' | 'healthy',
  newEtag:    string | null,
): Promise<void> {
  await db.query(
    `UPDATE physical_chunks
     SET shard_status = $1,
         provider_etag = COALESCE($2, provider_etag)
     WHERE id = $3`,
    [status, newEtag, chunkId],
  );
}

// ── Repair job persistence ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function upsertRepairJob(
  job: Omit<RepairJob, 'id'>,
): Promise<string | null> {
  const { rows } = await db.query<{ id: string }>(
    `INSERT INTO repair_jobs
       (inode_version_id, chunk_index, shard_index,
        source_provider_id, source_object_id, trigger_reason)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
     ON CONFLICT (inode_version_id, chunk_index, shard_index)
       WHERE status NOT IN ('complete', 'abandoned')
     DO NOTHING
     RETURNING id`,
    [
      job.inodeVersionId,
      job.chunkIndex,
      job.shardIndex,
      job.sourceProviderId,
      job.sourceObjectId,
      job.triggerReason,
    ],
  );
  return rows[0]?.id ?? null;
}

export async function markRepairJobDispatched(jobId: string): Promise<void> {
  await db.query(
    `UPDATE repair_jobs SET status = 'dispatched' WHERE id = $1`,
    [jobId],
  );
}

export async function markRepairJobFailed(
  jobId:    string,
  errorLog: string,
): Promise<void> {
  await db.query(
    `UPDATE repair_jobs
     SET status = CASE
           WHEN attempt_count + 1 >= max_attempts THEN 'abandoned'
           ELSE 'failed'
         END,
         attempt_count     = attempt_count + 1,
         last_attempted_at = now(),
         error_log         = $2
     WHERE id = $1`,
    [jobId, errorLog],
  );
}

export async function markRepairJobComplete(
  jobId:          string,
  newProviderId:  string,
  newObjectId:    string,
): Promise<void> {
  await db.query(
    `UPDATE repair_jobs
     SET status            = 'complete',
         completed_at      = now(),
         new_provider_id   = $2,
         new_object_id     = $3,
         last_attempted_at = now(),
         attempt_count     = attempt_count + 1
     WHERE id = $1`,
    [jobId, newProviderId, newObjectId],
  );
}

// ── Active provider connections ───────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ConnectedUser {
  userId:     string;
  providerId: string;
}

export async function getActiveConnections(): Promise<ConnectedUser[]> {
  const { rows } = await db.query<{ user_id: string; provider_id: string }>(
    `SELECT user_id, provider_id FROM provider_credentials
     WHERE is_active = true`,
  );
  return rows.map((r) => ({ userId: r.user_id, providerId: r.provider_id }));
}

// ── CRDT event log ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

import type { InodeEvent } from '../types/index.js';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

export async function appendInodeEvent(
  event: Omit<InodeEvent, 'id' | 'eventHash' | 'prevEventHash' | 'createdAt'> & {
    prevEventHash: Buffer | null;
  },
): Promise<InodeEvent> {
  // Compute event hash: BLAKE3 not available in Node easily without native; use SHA-256
  const content = JSON.stringify({
    inodeId:   event.inodeId,
    op:        event.op,
    lamportTs: event.lamportTs,
    origin:    event.origin,
    payload:   event.payload,
  });
  const eventHash = createHash('sha256')
    .update(event.prevEventHash ?? Buffer.alloc(0))
    .update(content)
    .digest();

  const { rows } = await db.query<{ id: string; created_at: Date }>(
    `INSERT INTO inode_events
       (inode_id, volume_id, op, lamport_ts, origin,
        payload, prev_event_hash, event_hash, signature)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9)
     ON CONFLICT (inode_id, origin, lamport_ts) DO NOTHING
     RETURNING id, created_at`,
    [
      event.inodeId,
      event.volumeId,
      event.op,
      event.lamportTs,
      event.origin,
      JSON.stringify(event.payload),
      event.prevEventHash,
      eventHash,
      event.signature,
    ],
  );

  return {
    ...event,
    id:        rows[0]!.id,
    eventHash,
    createdAt: rows[0]!.created_at,
  };
}

export async function getLatestLamportTs(
  inodeId: string,
  origin:  string,
): Promise<number> {
  const { rows } = await db.query<{ lamport_ts: string }>(
    `SELECT COALESCE(MAX(lamport_ts), 0) as lamport_ts
     FROM inode_events
     WHERE inode_id = $1 AND origin = $2`,
    [inodeId, origin],
  );
  return parseInt(rows[0]?.lamport_ts ?? '0', 10);
}

export async function getLastEventHash(
  inodeId: string,
  origin:  string,
): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  const { rows } = await db.query<{ event_hash: Buffer }>(
    `SELECT event_hash FROM inode_events
     WHERE inode_id = $1 AND origin = $2
     ORDER BY lamport_ts DESC
     LIMIT 1`,
    [inodeId, origin],
  );
  return rows[0]?.event_hash ?? null;
}

import type { RepairJob } from '../types/index.js';
```

### Step 5 · Provider Polling

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/polling/ProviderPoller.ts`

```typescript
import axios from 'axios';
import type { DeltaPollResult, ProviderId } from '../types/index.js';

/**
 * Per-provider delta polling implementations.
 * Each provider has a different change-tracking API surface:
 *   Google Drive: Changes API v3 with pageToken
 *   OneDrive:     /me/drive/root/delta with deltaLink
 *   Dropbox:      /files/list_folder/continue with cursor + longpoll
 */

export interface PollerDeps {
  getAccessToken: (userId: string, providerId: string) => Promise<string>;
}

// ── Google Drive ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function pollGoogleDrive(
  userId:    string,
  cursor:    string | null,
  deps:      PollerDeps,
): Promise<DeltaPollResult> {
  const token = await deps.getAccessToken(userId, 'google_drive');

  // cursor = null means first call: get start token (establishes baseline, no changes)
  if (!cursor) {
    const resp = await axios.get<{ startPageToken: string }>(
      'https://www.googleapis.com/drive/v3/changes/startPageToken',
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
    );
    return { changes: [], nextCursor: resp.data.startPageToken, hasMore: false };
  }

  const resp = await axios.get<{
    nextPageToken?:     string;
    newStartPageToken?: string;
    changes: Array<{
      fileId:  string;
      removed: boolean;
      time:    string;
      file?: { id: string; trashed: boolean; md5Checksum?: string };
    }>;
  }>(
    'https://www.googleapis.com/drive/v3/changes',
    {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      params:  {
        pageToken:    cursor,
        fields:       'nextPageToken,newStartPageToken,changes(fileId,removed,time,file(id,trashed,md5Checksum))',
        includeItemsFromAllDrives: false,
        supportsAllDrives:         false,
      },
    },
  );

  const d = resp.data;
  return {
    changes: d.changes.map((c) => ({
      objectId:   c.fileId,
      changeType: c.removed || c.file?.trashed ? 'deleted' : 'modified',
      newEtag:    c.file?.md5Checksum ?? null,
      modifiedAt: c.time,
    })),
    nextCursor: d.nextPageToken ?? d.newStartPageToken!,
    hasMore:    !!d.nextPageToken,
  };
}

// ── OneDrive ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function pollOneDrive(
  userId: string,
  cursor: string | null,
  deps:   PollerDeps,
): Promise<DeltaPollResult> {
  const token = await deps.getAccessToken(userId, 'onedrive');

  // First call: GET /me/drive/root/delta to establish baseline deltaLink
  const url = cursor ?? 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/me/drive/root/delta';

  const resp = await axios.get<{
    '@odata.nextLink'?:  string;
    '@odata.deltaLink'?: string;
    value: Array<{
      id:      string;
      deleted?: { state: string };
      lastModifiedDateTime: string;
      file?: { hashes?: { quickXorHash: string } };
    }>;
  }>(url, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });

  const d = resp.data;
  const nextCursor = d['@odata.nextLink'] ?? d['@odata.deltaLink']!;

  if (!cursor) {
    // First call — drain the delta fully to establish baseline (no changes emitted)
    // hasMore = true causes the scheduler to keep draining until we have a deltaLink
    return {
      changes:    [],
      nextCursor,
      hasMore: !!d['@odata.nextLink'],
    };
  }

  return {
    changes: d.value.map((item) => ({
      objectId:   item.id,
      changeType: item.deleted ? 'deleted' : 'modified',
      newEtag:    item.file?.hashes?.quickXorHash ?? null,
      modifiedAt: item.lastModifiedDateTime,
    })),
    nextCursor,
    hasMore: !!d['@odata.nextLink'],
  };
}

// ── Dropbox ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function pollDropbox(
  userId: string,
  cursor: string | null,
  deps:   PollerDeps,
): Promise<DeltaPollResult> {
  const token = await deps.getAccessToken(userId, 'dropbox');

  // First call: /files/list_folder with empty path to get initial cursor
  if (!cursor) {
    const resp = await axios.post<{ cursor: string; has_more: boolean }>(
      'https://api.dropboxapi.com/2/files/list_folder',
      { path: '', recursive: true, include_deleted: true },
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
    );
    // Drain all pages to establish cursor at current state, emit no changes
    let c = resp.data.cursor;
    let hasMore = resp.data.has_more;
    while (hasMore) {
      const cont = await axios.post<{ cursor: string; has_more: boolean }>(
        'https://api.dropboxapi.com/2/files/list_folder/continue',
        { cursor: c },
        { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
      );
      c = cont.data.cursor;
      hasMore = cont.data.has_more;
    }
    return { changes: [], nextCursor: c, hasMore: false };
  }

  const resp = await axios.post<{
    cursor:    string;
    has_more:  boolean;
    entries: Array<{
      '.tag':           string;
      id?:              string;
      server_modified?: string;
      content_hash?:    string;
    }>;
  }>(
    'https://api.dropboxapi.com/2/files/list_folder/continue',
    { cursor },
    { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
  );

  return {
    changes: resp.data.entries
      .filter((e) => e.id)  // Skip entries without ID (root folder changes)
      .map((e) => ({
        objectId:   e['id']!,
        changeType: e['.tag'] === 'deleted' ? 'deleted' : 'modified',
        newEtag:    e['content_hash'] ?? null,
        modifiedAt: e['server_modified'] ?? new Date().toISOString(),
      })),
    nextCursor: resp.data.cursor,
    hasMore:    resp.data.has_more,
  };
}

// ── Dispatch to correct poller ────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function pollProvider(
  providerId: ProviderId,
  userId:     string,
  cursor:     string | null,
  deps:       PollerDeps,
): Promise<DeltaPollResult> {
  switch (providerId) {
    case 'google_drive': return pollGoogleDrive(userId, cursor, deps);
    case 'onedrive':     return pollOneDrive(userId, cursor, deps);
    case 'dropbox':      return pollDropbox(userId, cursor, deps);
    case 'mega':
      // MEGA uses WebSocket push; polling is a no-op here.
      // The MEGA SDK sends events directly to a registered webhook in the auth-service.
      return { changes: [], nextCursor: cursor ?? 'mega_noop', hasMore: false };
  }
}
```

### Step 6 · CRDT Reconciler

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/crdt/Reconciler.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * CrdtReconciler
 *
 * Processes provider change events and reconciles them against the inode tree.
 *
 * Resolution rules (Last-Write-Wins with Lamport clock):
 *   1. If the provider change is for a file Aether owns (objectId in physical_chunks):
 *      a. etag mismatch → the shard has been modified out-of-band → mark dirty
 *      b. deletion      → mark shard missing, trigger repair
 *   2. If the change is for a file Aether does NOT own (user's own Drive files):
 *      → ignore; Aether only manages shards inside its own folder
 *   3. Concurrent Aether write + OOB change → Aether write wins (higher lamport_ts)
 *      because client increments lamport on every write before calling the API.
 *
 * The vector clock is updated per-inode per-origin. The inode_events table is
 * the append-only CRDT log from which any past state can be replayed.
 */

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {
  appendInodeEvent,
  findChunksByProviderObject,
  getLatestLamportTs,
  getLastEventHash,
  markShardStatus,
  db,
} from '../db/index.js';
import type { ProviderChange, ProviderId } from '../types/index.js';

export interface ReconcileResult {
  dirtyShards:   DirtyShard[];
  missingShards: DirtyShard[];
  ignoredCount:  number;
}

export interface DirtyShard {
  chunkId:          string;
  inodeVersionId:   string;
  chunkIndex:       number;
  shardIndex:       number;
  providerId:       string;
  providerObjectId: string;
  triggerReason:    'oob_modify' | 'provider_404';
}

export class CrdtReconciler {
  async reconcile(
    userId:     string,
    providerId: ProviderId,
    changes:    ProviderChange[],
  ): Promise<ReconcileResult> {
    if (changes.length === 0) {
      return { dirtyShards: [], missingShards: [], ignoredCount: 0 };
    }

    // Batch-lookup all changed objectIds against physical_chunks
    const objectIds  = [...new Set(changes.map((c) => c.objectId))];
    const chunkRows  = await findChunksByProviderObject(providerId, objectIds);

    // Index chunk rows by objectId for O(1) lookup
    const chunkIndex = new Map(chunkRows.map((c) => [c.providerObjectId, c]));

    const dirtyShards:   DirtyShard[] = [];
    const missingShards: DirtyShard[] = [];
    let   ignoredCount = 0;

    for (const change of changes) {
      const chunk = chunkIndex.get(change.objectId);

      if (!chunk) {
        // Not one of Aether's shards — user's own file. Ignore.
        ignoredCount++;
        continue;
      }

      // Fetch the inode_id from the inode_versions table to anchor the CRDT event
      const inodeRow = await this.fetchInodeForVersion(chunk.inodeVersionId);
      if (!inodeRow) {
        ignoredCount++;
        continue;
      }

      if (change.changeType === 'deleted') {
        // Provider deleted one of our shards (user emptied trash, storage purged, etc.)
        await markShardStatus(chunk.id, 'missing', null);
        missingShards.push({
          chunkId:          chunk.id,
          inodeVersionId:   chunk.inodeVersionId,
          chunkIndex:       chunk.chunkIndex,
          shardIndex:       chunk.shardIndex,
          providerId:       chunk.providerId,
          providerObjectId: chunk.providerObjectId,
          triggerReason:    'provider_404',
        });

        await this.emitEvent({
          inodeId:   inodeRow.inodeId,
          volumeId:  inodeRow.volumeId,
          op:        'provider_out_of_band_delete',
          origin:    providerId,
          payload:   {
            provider_id:        providerId,
            provider_object_id: chunk.providerObjectId,
            chunk_index:        chunk.chunkIndex,
            shard_index:        chunk.shardIndex,
          },
        });

      } else if (change.changeType === 'modified') {
        // Check if etag changed — if so, the shard content was altered OOB
        const etagChanged = change.newEtag !== null
          && chunk.providerEtag !== null
          && change.newEtag !== chunk.providerEtag;

        if (!etagChanged && change.newEtag === chunk.providerEtag) {
          // Same etag — no actual content change (metadata-only update). Skip.
          ignoredCount++;
          continue;
        }

        await markShardStatus(chunk.id, 'corrupt', change.newEtag);
        dirtyShards.push({
          chunkId:          chunk.id,
          inodeVersionId:   chunk.inodeVersionId,
          chunkIndex:       chunk.chunkIndex,
          shardIndex:       chunk.shardIndex,
          providerId:       chunk.providerId,
          providerObjectId: chunk.providerObjectId,
          triggerReason:    'oob_modify',
        });

        await this.emitEvent({
          inodeId:  inodeRow.inodeId,
          volumeId: inodeRow.volumeId,
          op:       'provider_out_of_band_modify',
          origin:   providerId,
          payload:  {
            provider_id:        providerId,
            provider_object_id: chunk.providerObjectId,
            new_etag:           change.newEtag,
            chunk_index:        chunk.chunkIndex,
            shard_index:        chunk.shardIndex,
          },
        });
      }
    }

    return { dirtyShards, missingShards, ignoredCount };
  }

  private async fetchInodeForVersion(
    inodeVersionId: string,
  ): Promise<{ inodeId: string; volumeId: string } | null> {
    const { rows } = await db.query<{ inode_id: string; volume_id: string }>(
      `SELECT i.id as inode_id, i.volume_id
       FROM inode_versions iv
       JOIN inodes i ON i.id = iv.inode_id
       WHERE iv.id = $1`,
      [inodeVersionId],
    );
    if (!rows[0]) return null;
    return { inodeId: rows[0].inode_id, volumeId: rows[0].volume_id };
  }

  private async emitEvent(args: {
    inodeId:  string;
    volumeId: string;
    op:       import('../types/index.js').InodeEventOp;
    origin:   string;
    payload:  Record<string, unknown>;
  }): Promise<void> {
    const lamportTs    = await getLatestLamportTs(args.inodeId, args.origin) + 1;
    const prevHash     = await getLastEventHash(args.inodeId, args.origin);

    await appendInodeEvent({
      inodeId:       args.inodeId,
      volumeId:      args.volumeId,
      op:            args.op,
      lamportTs,
      origin:        args.origin,
      payload:       args.payload,
      prevEventHash: prevHash,
      signature:     null,  // server-side events are not client-signed
    });
  }
}
```

### Step 7 · NATS Priority Queue

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/queue/NatsClient.ts`

```typescript
import { connect, type NatsConnection, StringCodec, type JetStreamClient } from 'nats';
import type { JetStreamManager } from 'nats';
import { env } from '../config/index.js';

export class AetherNatsClient {
  private nc!:  NatsConnection;
  private js!:  JetStreamClient;
  private jsm!: JetStreamManager;

  async connect(): Promise<void> {
    this.nc  = await connect({ servers: env.NATS_URL });
    this.js  = this.nc.jetstream();
    this.jsm = await this.nc.jetstreamManager();

    await this.ensureStreams();
  }

  /**
   * Ensure all required JetStream streams and durable consumers exist.
   *
   * Stream topology:
   *   PROVIDER_OPS    — subjects: provider.>
   *                    Subjects by priority:
   *                      provider.{pid}.{uid}.p0  → interactive reads  (drain first)
   *                      provider.{pid}.{uid}.p1  → user writes
   *                      provider.{pid}.{uid}.p2  → background replication
   *                      provider.{pid}.{uid}.p3  → delta-sync / repair
   *
   *   ORCHESTRATOR_EVENTS — subjects: aether.orchestrator.>
   *                         (published by chunk-orchestrator, consumed here)
   *
   * Each priority level has its OWN durable consumer with flow-control.
   * The priority worker drains P0 → P1 → P2 → P3 in order.
   */
  private async ensureStreams(): Promise<void> {
    // PROVIDER_OPS stream
    await this.jsm.streams.add({
      name:           'PROVIDER_OPS',
      subjects:       ['provider.>'],
      storage:        'file' as const,
      retention:      'limits' as const,
      max_age:        86_400 * 1_000_000_000,  // 24 hours in nanoseconds
      max_msg_size:   4 * 1024 * 1024,          // 4 MB max per message
      discard:        'old' as const,
      num_replicas:   1,                         // 3 in production
    }).catch((e: Error) => {
      // Stream already exists — update it
      if (e.message.includes('stream name already in use')) return;
      throw e;
    });

    // Durable consumer per priority lane
    for (const priority of ['p0', 'p1', 'p2', 'p3'] as const) {
      await this.jsm.consumers.add('PROVIDER_OPS', {
        durable_name:    `worker-${priority}`,
        filter_subject:  `provider.>.${priority}`,
        ack_policy:      'explicit' as const,
        max_deliver:     5,
        ack_wait:        30_000_000_000,  // 30s in nanoseconds
        max_ack_pending: priority === 'p0' ? 100 : priority === 'p1' ? 50 : 20,
        deliver_policy:  'all' as const,
      }).catch((e: Error) => {
        if (e.message.includes('consumer name already in use')) return;
        throw e;
      });
    }

    // ORCHESTRATOR_EVENTS consumer (for repair completion notifications)
    await this.jsm.streams.add({
      name:        'ORCHESTRATOR_EVENTS',
      subjects:    ['aether.orchestrator.>'],
      storage:     'file' as const,
      retention:   'limits' as const,
      max_age:     86_400 * 1_000_000_000,
      num_replicas: 1,
    }).catch((e: Error) => {
      if (e.message.includes('stream name already in use')) return;
      throw e;
    });
  }

  /**
   * Publish a repair job to the P3 (lowest priority) queue.
   * Subject: provider.{providerId}.{userId}.p3
   */
  async publishRepairJob(
    userId:     string,
    providerId: string,
    payload:    RepairJobPayload,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const subject = `provider.${providerId}.${userId}.p3`;
    const data    = JSON.stringify(payload);
    await this.js.publish(subject, new TextEncoder().encode(data));
  }

  get jetstream(): JetStreamClient { return this.js; }
  get connection(): NatsConnection  { return this.nc; }

  async drain(): Promise<void> {
    await this.nc.drain();
  }
}

export interface RepairJobPayload {
  jobId:           string;
  inodeVersionId:  string;
  chunkIndex:      number;
  shardIndex:      number;
  sourceProviderId: string;
  sourceObjectId:  string;
  triggerReason:   string;
  enqueuedAt:      string;
}
```

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/queue/PriorityWorker.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * PriorityWorker
 *
 * Drains NATS JetStream consumers in strict priority order:
 *   P0 → P1 → P2 → P3
 *
 * Each iteration of the work loop:
 *   1. Check P0 consumer — process all available messages first
 *   2. If P0 empty, process one P1 batch
 *   3. If P1 empty, process one P2 batch
 *   4. If P2 empty, process one P3 batch (repair jobs)
 *   5. Sleep IDLE_SLEEP_MS if all queues empty
 *
 * P3 repair jobs are forwarded to the chunk-orchestrator gRPC RepairShard RPC.
 */

import type { JetStreamClient, ConsumerMessages } from 'nats';
import { AckPolicy } from 'nats';
import pLimit from 'p-limit';
import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
import { ChunkOrchestratorClient } from '../grpc/ChunkOrchestratorClient.js';
import { markRepairJobDispatched, markRepairJobComplete, markRepairJobFailed } from '../db/index.js';
import type { RepairJobPayload } from './NatsClient.js';

const logger = createLogger('priority-worker');
const IDLE_SLEEP_MS  = 1_000;
const BATCH_SIZE     = 10;

export class PriorityWorker {
  private running = false;
  private readonly repair = pLimit(4);  // max concurrent repair RPCs

  constructor(
    private readonly js:      JetStreamClient,
    private readonly grpc:    ChunkOrchestratorClient,
    private readonly userId:  string,  // worker is scoped per-user for rate isolation
  ) {}

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    this.running = true;
    logger.info({ userId: this.userId }, 'priority worker started');

    while (this.running) {
      const processed = await this.drainInPriorityOrder();
      if (processed === 0) {
        await sleep(IDLE_SLEEP_MS);
      }
    }
  }

  stop(): void {
    this.running = false;
  }

  private async drainInPriorityOrder(): Promise<number> {
    let total = 0;

    for (const priority of ['p0', 'p1', 'p2', 'p3'] as const) {
      const count = await this.processBatch(priority);
      total += count;
      // If we processed any messages at a higher priority, restart the loop
      // to ensure P0 always drains completely before P1 gets more turns.
      if (count > 0 && priority !== 'p3') break;
    }

    return total;
  }

  private async processBatch(
    priority: 'p0' | 'p1' | 'p2' | 'p3',
  ): Promise<number> {
    let consumer: ConsumerMessages;

    try {
      const c = await this.js.consumers.get('PROVIDER_OPS', `worker-${priority}`);
      consumer = await c.fetch({ max_messages: BATCH_SIZE, expires: 2_000 });
    } catch {
      return 0;
    }

    let count = 0;

    for await (const msg of consumer) {
      count++;
      try {
        const payload = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(msg.data)) as RepairJobPayload;

        if (priority === 'p3') {
          // Repair jobs: forward to chunk-orchestrator gRPC
          await this.repair(() => this.handleRepairJob(payload));
        } else {
          // P0–P2: placeholder for upload/download/replication routing
          // These are handled by the chunk-orchestrator directly in Phase 3;
          // the queue here is for throttle/retry management only.
          logger.debug({ priority, subject: msg.subject }, 'forwarding op');
        }

        msg.ack();
      } catch (err) {
        logger.error({ priority, err }, 'message processing failed');
        msg.nak(30_000);  // requeue after 30s
      }
    }

    return count;
  }

  private async handleRepairJob(payload: RepairJobPayload): Promise<void> {
    logger.info({
      jobId:         payload.jobId,
      versionId:     payload.inodeVersionId,
      chunkIndex:    payload.chunkIndex,
      shardIndex:    payload.shardIndex,
      triggerReason: payload.triggerReason,
    }, 'dispatching repair job to chunk-orchestrator');

    try {
      await markRepairJobDispatched(payload.jobId);

      const result = await this.grpc.repairShard({
        inodeVersionId: payload.inodeVersionId,
        userId:         this.userId,
        chunkIndex:     payload.chunkIndex,
        shardIndex:     payload.shardIndex,
      });

      if (result.repaired) {
        await markRepairJobComplete(
          payload.jobId,
          result.newProviderId,
          result.newObjectId,
        );
        logger.info({
          jobId:         payload.jobId,
          newProviderId: result.newProviderId,
          newObjectId:   result.newObjectId,
        }, 'repair complete');
      } else {
        await markRepairJobFailed(payload.jobId, 'orchestrator returned repaired=false');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      await markRepairJobFailed(payload.jobId, msg);
      throw err;  // causes msg.nak() in caller
    }
  }
}

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
}
```

### Step 8 · gRPC Client for Chunk-Orchestrator

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/grpc/ChunkOrchestratorClient.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * gRPC client wrapping the chunk-orchestrator's ChunkOrchestratorService.
 * Used by PriorityWorker to dispatch RepairShard RPCs.
 */

import * as grpc from '@grpc/grpc-js';
import * as protoLoader from '@grpc/proto-loader';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { env } from '../config/index.js';

const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const PROTO_PATH = join(__dirname, '../../proto/chunk_orchestrator.proto');

interface RepairShardRequest {
  inodeVersionId: string;
  userId:         string;
  chunkIndex:     number;
  shardIndex:     number;
}

interface RepairShardResponse {
  repaired:       boolean;
  newProviderId:  string;
  newObjectId:    string;
}

// Dynamically typed gRPC stub — proto types are validated at runtime
type GrpcStub = {
  RepairShard: (
    req: Record<string, unknown>,
    cb:  (err: grpc.ServiceError | null, res?: Record<string, unknown>) => void,
  ) => void;
};

export class ChunkOrchestratorClient {
  private client!: GrpcStub;

  constructor() {
    const packageDef = protoLoader.loadSync(PROTO_PATH, {
      keepCase:          false,
      longs:             String,
      enums:             String,
      defaults:          true,
      oneofs:            true,
      json:              true,
    });

    const proto = grpc.loadPackageDefinition(packageDef) as Record
      string,
      Record<string, new (addr: string, creds: grpc.ChannelCredentials) => GrpcStub>
    >;

    const Svc = proto['aether']?.['chunk_orchestrator']?.['v1']
      ?.['ChunkOrchestratorService'];

    if (!Svc) {
      throw new Error('Failed to load ChunkOrchestratorService from proto');
    }

    // Insecure in dev; use grpc.credentials.createSsl() with mTLS in prod
    this.client = new Svc(
      env.CHUNK_ORCHESTRATOR_ADDR,
      grpc.credentials.createInsecure(),
    );
  }

  repairShard(req: RepairShardRequest): Promise<RepairShardResponse> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      this.client.RepairShard(
        {
          inode_version_id: req.inodeVersionId,
          user_id:          req.userId,
          chunk_index:      req.chunkIndex,
          shard_index:      req.shardIndex,
        },
        (err, res) => {
          if (err) return reject(err);
          resolve({
            repaired:      res?.['repaired'] as boolean ?? false,
            newProviderId: res?.['new_provider_id'] as string ?? '',
            newObjectId:   res?.['new_object_id'] as string ?? '',
          });
        },
      );
    });
  }
}
```

### Step 9 · Circuit Breaker

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/dispatcher/CircuitBreaker.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * Per-(userId, providerId) circuit breaker backed by Redis.
 *
 * States:
 *   CLOSED    → normal operation; errors are counted
 *   OPEN      → too many errors; all requests fail fast for RESET_MS
 *   HALF_OPEN → probe period; one request allowed through to test recovery
 *
 * Transitions:
 *   CLOSED  + N consecutive errors → OPEN
 *   OPEN    + RESET_MS elapsed      → HALF_OPEN
 *   HALF_OPEN + success             → CLOSED
 *   HALF_OPEN + failure             → OPEN (reset timer)
 */

import type Redis from 'ioredis';
import { env } from '../config/index.js';
import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';

const logger = createLogger('circuit-breaker');

type CBState = 'closed' | 'open' | 'half_open';

interface CBData {
  state:             CBState;
  consecutiveErrors: number;
  openedAt:          number | null;
}

export class CircuitBreaker {
  constructor(private readonly redis: Redis) {}

  private key(userId: string, providerId: string): string {
    return `cb:${providerId}:${userId}`;
  }

  async getState(userId: string, providerId: string): Promise<CBData> {
    const raw = await this.redis.hgetall(this.key(userId, providerId));
    if (!raw || Object.keys(raw).length === 0) {
      return { state: 'closed', consecutiveErrors: 0, openedAt: null };
    }

    const state    = (raw['state'] ?? 'closed') as CBState;
    const errors   = parseInt(raw['consecutiveErrors'] ?? '0', 10);
    const openedAt = raw['openedAt'] ? parseInt(raw['openedAt'], 10) : null;

    // Auto-transition OPEN → HALF_OPEN after reset window
    if (state === 'open' && openedAt) {
      const elapsed = Date.now() - openedAt;
      if (elapsed >= env.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS) {
        await this.transitionTo(userId, providerId, 'half_open', errors, openedAt);
        return { state: 'half_open', consecutiveErrors: errors, openedAt };
      }
    }

    return { state, consecutiveErrors: errors, openedAt };
  }

  /**
   * Returns true if the request should be allowed through.
   * Throws on OPEN circuit (fail-fast).
   */
  async allowRequest(userId: string, providerId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const { state, openedAt } = await this.getState(userId, providerId);
    switch (state) {
      case 'closed':    return true;
      case 'half_open': return true;   // probe request
      case 'open':
        const retryAfterMs = env.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS - (Date.now() - (openedAt ?? 0));
        logger.warn({ userId, providerId, retryAfterMs },
          'circuit breaker OPEN — request blocked');
        return false;
    }
  }

  async recordSuccess(userId: string, providerId: string): Promise<void> {
    const { state } = await this.getState(userId, providerId);
    if (state === 'half_open' || state === 'open') {
      logger.info({ userId, providerId }, 'circuit breaker → CLOSED');
    }
    await this.transitionTo(userId, providerId, 'closed', 0, null);
  }

  async recordFailure(userId: string, providerId: string): Promise<void> {
    const { state, consecutiveErrors } = await this.getState(userId, providerId);
    const newErrors = consecutiveErrors + 1;

    if (newErrors >= env.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_THRESHOLD) {
      const openedAt = Date.now();
      await this.transitionTo(userId, providerId, 'open', newErrors, openedAt);
      logger.warn({ userId, providerId, newErrors }, 'circuit breaker → OPEN');
    } else {
      await this.transitionTo(userId, providerId, state, newErrors, null);
    }
  }

  private async transitionTo(
    userId:     string,
    providerId: string,
    state:      CBState,
    errors:     number,
    openedAt:   number | null,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const key  = this.key(userId, providerId);
    const data: Record<string, string> = {
      state:             state,
      consecutiveErrors: String(errors),
      openedAt:          openedAt ? String(openedAt) : '',
    };
    await this.redis.hset(key, data);
    await this.redis.pexpire(key, env.CIRCUIT_BREAKER_RESET_MS * 2);
  }
}
```

### Step 10 · Dirty Shard Dispatcher

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/dispatcher/DirtyShardDispatcher.ts`

```typescript
import { upsertRepairJob } from '../db/index.js';
import type { AetherNatsClient } from '../queue/NatsClient.js';
import type { DirtyShard } from '../crdt/Reconciler.js';
import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';

const logger = createLogger('dirty-shard-dispatcher');

export class DirtyShardDispatcher {
  constructor(private readonly nats: AetherNatsClient) {}

  /**
   * For each dirty/missing shard:
   *   1. Persist a repair_job record (idempotent upsert)
   *   2. Publish to NATS P3 queue
   *
   * Idempotency: the UNIQUE constraint on repair_jobs prevents duplicate rows.
   * If NATS publish fails, the job remains in 'pending' state and will be
   * picked up by the repair_job backfill sweeper (runs every 5 minutes).
   */
  async dispatch(
    userId:   string,
    shards:   DirtyShard[],
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (shards.length === 0) return;

    logger.info({ userId, count: shards.length }, 'dispatching dirty shards');

    for (const shard of shards) {
      try {
        const jobId = await upsertRepairJob({
          inodeVersionId:   shard.inodeVersionId,
          chunkIndex:       shard.chunkIndex,
          shardIndex:       shard.shardIndex,
          sourceProviderId: shard.providerId,
          sourceObjectId:   shard.providerObjectId,
          triggerReason:    shard.triggerReason,
        });

        if (!jobId) {
          // Conflict — a pending repair job already exists for this shard. Skip.
          logger.debug({
            inodeVersionId: shard.inodeVersionId,
            chunkIndex:     shard.chunkIndex,
            shardIndex:     shard.shardIndex,
          }, 'repair job already exists, skipping dispatch');
          continue;
        }

        await this.nats.publishRepairJob(userId, shard.providerId, {
          jobId,
          inodeVersionId:   shard.inodeVersionId,
          chunkIndex:       shard.chunkIndex,
          shardIndex:       shard.shardIndex,
          sourceProviderId: shard.providerId,
          sourceObjectId:   shard.providerObjectId,
          triggerReason:    shard.triggerReason,
          enqueuedAt:       new Date().toISOString(),
        });

        logger.info({ jobId, shardIndex: shard.shardIndex }, 'repair job dispatched');

      } catch (err) {
        logger.error({ shard, err }, 'failed to dispatch repair job');
        // Non-fatal: backfill sweeper will retry
      }
    }
  }
}
```

### Step 11 · Polling Scheduler

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/polling/PollingScheduler.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * PollingScheduler
 *
 * Manages a polling loop for every active (userId, providerId) pair.
 * Schedules polls at POLL_INTERVAL_MS intervals, bounded by POLL_CONCURRENCY.
 *
 * On each poll cycle:
 *   1. Load active connections from DB
 *   2. For each (user, provider): check circuit breaker → poll → reconcile → dispatch
 *   3. Persist updated cursor to Redis (with DB fallback)
 *   4. Record success/failure in circuit breaker
 */

import Redis from 'ioredis';
import pLimit from 'p-limit';
import pRetry from 'p-retry';
import { getActiveConnections, getCursor, upsertCursor, incrementCursorError } from '../db/index.js';
import { pollProvider } from './ProviderPoller.js';
import { CrdtReconciler } from '../crdt/Reconciler.js';
import { DirtyShardDispatcher } from '../dispatcher/DirtyShardDispatcher.js';
import { CircuitBreaker } from '../dispatcher/CircuitBreaker.js';
import { TokenService } from '../services/TokenService.js';
import { createLogger } from '../logger.js';
import { env } from '../config/index.js';
import type { ProviderId } from '../types/index.js';
import type { AetherNatsClient } from '../queue/NatsClient.js';

const logger = createLogger('polling-scheduler');

export class PollingScheduler {
  private readonly limit       = pLimit(env.POLL_CONCURRENCY);
  private readonly reconciler  = new CrdtReconciler();
  private readonly cb:         CircuitBreaker;
  private readonly dispatcher: DirtyShardDispatcher;
  private running = false;
  private timer?: NodeJS.Timeout;

  constructor(
    private readonly redis:        Redis,
    private readonly nats:         AetherNatsClient,
    private readonly tokenService: TokenService,
  ) {
    this.cb          = new CircuitBreaker(redis);
    this.dispatcher  = new DirtyShardDispatcher(nats);
  }

  start(): void {
    this.running = true;
    this.schedule();
    logger.info({ intervalMs: env.POLL_INTERVAL_MS }, 'polling scheduler started');
  }

  stop(): void {
    this.running = false;
    if (this.timer) clearTimeout(this.timer);
  }

  private schedule(): void {
    if (!this.running) return;
    this.timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      await this.runCycle();
      this.schedule();
    }, env.POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
  }

  async runCycle(): Promise<void> {
    const connections = await getActiveConnections();
    logger.info({ count: connections.length }, 'poll cycle starting');

    await Promise.allSettled(
      connections.map((conn) =>
        this.limit(() => this.pollOne(conn.userId, conn.providerId as ProviderId)),
      ),
    );

    logger.info('poll cycle complete');
  }

  private async pollOne(userId: string, providerId: ProviderId): Promise<void> {
    // Circuit breaker check
    const allowed = await this.cb.allowRequest(userId, providerId);
    if (!allowed) return;

    // Retrieve cursor: Redis hot store → DB fallback
    const cursorKey = `cursor:${providerId}:${userId}`;
    let cursor: string | null = await this.redis.get(cursorKey);
    if (!cursor) cursor = await getCursor(userId, providerId);

    try {
      let hasMore = true;
      let nextCursor = cursor;

      // Drain all pages before sleeping (important for first-run baseline drain)
      while (hasMore) {
        const result = await pRetry(
          () => pollProvider(
            providerId,
            userId,
            nextCursor,
            { getAccessToken: (uid, pid) => this.tokenService.getAccessToken(uid, pid) },
          ),
          {
            retries: 2,
            onFailedAttempt: (err) => {
              logger.warn({
                userId, providerId,
                attempt: err.attemptNumber,
                err: err.message,
              }, 'poll attempt failed, retrying');
            },
          },
        );

        nextCursor = result.nextCursor;
        hasMore    = result.hasMore;

        if (result.changes.length > 0) {
          // Reconcile changes against physical_chunks
          const { dirtyShards, missingShards, ignoredCount } =
            await this.reconciler.reconcile(userId, providerId, result.changes);

          logger.info({
            userId,
            providerId,
            changed:  result.changes.length,
            dirty:    dirtyShards.length,
            missing:  missingShards.length,
            ignored:  ignoredCount,
          }, 'reconciliation complete');

          // Dispatch all dirty + missing shards to NATS P3 repair queue
          await this.dispatcher.dispatch(userId, [
            ...dirtyShards,
            ...missingShards,
          ]);
        }

        // Persist cursor
        await this.redis.setex(cursorKey, 3600, nextCursor);
        await upsertCursor(userId, providerId, nextCursor);
      }

      await this.cb.recordSuccess(userId, providerId);

    } catch (err) {
      await incrementCursorError(userId, providerId);
      await this.cb.recordFailure(userId, providerId);
      logger.error({ userId, providerId, err }, 'poll failed');
    }
  }
}
```

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/services/TokenService.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * Fetches and decrypts OAuth tokens from the database.
 * Mirrors the auth-service token-encryption scheme.
 * This is a TypeScript port of the Rust TokenManager.
 */

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import pg from 'pg';
import { env } from '../config/index.js';

const { Pool } = pg;
const db = new Pool({ connectionString: env.DATABASE_URL, max: 5 });

const KEK    = Buffer.from(env.SERVER_TOKEN_KEK_BASE64, 'base64');
const ALGO   = 'aes-256-gcm' as const;
const NONCE  = 12;
const TAG    = 16;

interface TokenSet {
  accessToken:  string;
  refreshToken: string | null;
  expiresAt:    string;
  scopes:       string[];
}

const cache = new Map<string, { token: string; expiresAt: Date }>();

export class TokenService {
  async getAccessToken(userId: string, providerId: string): Promise<string> {
    const cacheKey = `${userId}:${providerId}`;
    const cached   = cache.get(cacheKey);
    const buffer   = 60_000; // 60s expiry buffer

    if (cached && cached.expiresAt.getTime() - Date.now() > buffer) {
      return cached.token;
    }

    const row = await db.query<{ encrypted_tokens: Buffer }>(
      `SELECT encrypted_tokens FROM provider_credentials
       WHERE user_id = $1 AND provider_id = $2 AND is_active = true`,
      [userId, providerId],
    );

    if (!row.rows[0]) {
      throw new Error(`No active credentials for ${userId}/${providerId}`);
    }

    const tokenSet = this.decrypt(row.rows[0].encrypted_tokens);
    const expiresAt = new Date(tokenSet.expiresAt);

    if (expiresAt.getTime() - Date.now() < buffer && tokenSet.refreshToken) {
      const refreshed = await this.refresh(tokenSet, providerId);
      cache.set(cacheKey, { token: refreshed.accessToken, expiresAt: new Date(refreshed.expiresAt) });
      return refreshed.accessToken;
    }

    cache.set(cacheKey, { token: tokenSet.accessToken, expiresAt });
    return tokenSet.accessToken;
  }

  private decrypt(blob: Buffer): TokenSet {
    if (blob.length < NONCE + TAG) throw new Error('Token blob too short');
    const nonce = blob.subarray(0, NONCE);
    const tag   = blob.subarray(blob.length - TAG);
    const ct    = blob.subarray(NONCE, blob.length - TAG);

    const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv(ALGO, KEK, nonce);
    decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
    const plain = Buffer.concat([decipher.update(ct), decipher.final()]);
    return JSON.parse(plain.toString('utf8')) as TokenSet;
  }

  private async refresh(tokens: TokenSet, providerId: string): Promise<TokenSet> {
    const configs: Record<string, { url: string; clientId: string; secret: string }> = {
      google_drive: {
        url:      'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',
        clientId: env.GOOGLE_DRIVE_CLIENT_ID,
        secret:   env.GOOGLE_DRIVE_CLIENT_SECRET,
      },
      onedrive: {
        url:      'https://login.microsoftonline.com/common/oauth2/v2.0/token',
        clientId: env.ONEDRIVE_CLIENT_ID,
        secret:   env.ONEDRIVE_CLIENT_SECRET,
      },
      dropbox: {
        url:      'https://api.dropboxapi.com/oauth2/token',
        clientId: env.DROPBOX_CLIENT_ID,
        secret:   env.DROPBOX_CLIENT_SECRET,
      },
    };

    const cfg = configs[providerId];
    if (!cfg || !tokens.refreshToken) throw new Error(`Cannot refresh ${providerId}`);

    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      grant_type:    'refresh_token',
      refresh_token: tokens.refreshToken,
      client_id:     cfg.clientId,
      client_secret: cfg.secret,
    });

    const resp = await fetch(cfg.url, {
      method:  'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
      body:    params,
    });

    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(`Token refresh failed: ${resp.status}`);

    const data = await resp.json() as { access_token: string; expires_in: number; refresh_token?: string };
    return {
      accessToken:  data.access_token,
      refreshToken: data.refresh_token ?? tokens.refreshToken,
      expiresAt:    new Date(Date.now() + data.expires_in * 1000).toISOString(),
      scopes:       tokens.scopes,
    };
  }
}
```

### Step 12 · Logger & Entry Point

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/logger.ts`

```typescript
import winston from 'winston';
import { env } from './config/index.js';

export function createLogger(component: string): winston.Logger {
  return winston.createLogger({
    level:  env.NODE_ENV === 'production' ? 'info' : 'debug',
    format: winston.format.combine(
      winston.format.timestamp(),
      winston.format.json(),
    ),
    defaultMeta: { service: 'delta-sync-service', component },
    transports:  [new winston.transports.Console()],
  });
```

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/index.ts`

```typescript
import Redis from 'ioredis';
import { env } from './config/index.js';
import { AetherNatsClient } from './queue/NatsClient.js';
import { PollingScheduler } from './polling/PollingScheduler.js';
import { PriorityWorker } from './queue/PriorityWorker.js';
import { ChunkOrchestratorClient } from './grpc/ChunkOrchestratorClient.js';
import { TokenService } from './services/TokenService.js';
import { getActiveConnections } from './db/index.js';
import { createLogger } from './logger.js';

const logger = createLogger('main');

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  logger.info('delta-sync-service starting');

  // ── Redis ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const redis = new Redis(env.REDIS_URL, {
    maxRetriesPerRequest: 3,
    enableReadyCheck:     true,
  });
  await redis.ping();
  logger.info('Redis connected');

  // ── NATS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const nats = new AetherNatsClient();
  await nats.connect();
  logger.info('NATS JetStream connected');

  // ── gRPC client ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const grpc = new ChunkOrchestratorClient();
  logger.info(`gRPC client connected to chunk-orchestrator @ ${env.CHUNK_ORCHESTRATOR_ADDR}`);

  // ── Services ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const tokenService = new TokenService();

  // ── Polling scheduler ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const scheduler = new PollingScheduler(redis, nats, tokenService);
  scheduler.start();

  // ── Priority workers (one per active user, or pooled in production) ───────
  // Phase 3 approach: single shared worker that iterates all queues.
  // Production approach: one worker process per user shard (Kubernetes HPA).
  const connections = await getActiveConnections();
  const uniqueUsers = [...new Set(connections.map((c) => c.userId))];

  const workers = uniqueUsers.map((userId) => {
    const worker = new PriorityWorker(nats.jetstream, grpc, userId);
    worker.start().catch((err) => logger.error({ userId, err }, 'worker crashed'));
    return worker;
  });

  logger.info({ workerCount: workers.length }, 'priority workers started');

  // ── Health endpoint ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const http = await import('node:http');
  const server = http.createServer((_req, res) => {
    res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
    res.end(JSON.stringify({ status: 'ok', service: 'delta-sync-service' }));
  });
  server.listen(3003, () => logger.info('Health endpoint on :3003'));

  // ── Graceful shutdown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  async function shutdown(signal: string): Promise<void> {
    logger.info({ signal }, 'shutting down');
    scheduler.stop();
    workers.forEach((w) => w.stop());
    await nats.drain();
    await redis.quit();
    server.close();
    process.exit(0);
  }

  process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'));
  process.on('SIGINT',  () => shutdown('SIGINT'));
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error('Fatal startup error:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});
```

### Step 13 · Complete RepairShard in Chunk-Orchestrator (Rust)

#### `services/chunk-orchestrator/src/application/use_cases/repair_shard.rs`

```rust
//! RepairShardUseCase
//!
//! RS reconstruction + re-upload path.
//!
//! Algorithm:
//!   1. Fetch all 14 shard records for (inode_version_id, chunk_index) from DB
//!   2. Mark target shard as 'repairing'
//!   3. Download all available healthy shards from their providers concurrently
//!   4. If >= DATA_SHARDS (10) available: run RS reconstruction to recover the
//!      missing/corrupt shard bytes
//!   5. Re-upload the reconstructed shard to an alternative healthy provider
//!      (prefer one NOT already holding shards for this chunk)
//!   6. Update physical_chunks row: new provider_id, object_id, etag, status='healthy'
//!   7. Emit ShardRepairComplete NATS event

use std::sync::Arc;
use futures::stream::{self, StreamExt};
use uuid::Uuid;

use aether_crypto_core::erasure::{encode, reconstruct, DATA_SHARDS, TOTAL_SHARDS};

use crate::domain::entities::chunk::{PhysicalChunk, ShardStatus, PARITY_SHARDS};
use crate::domain::repositories::chunk_repository::ChunkRepository;
use crate::error::OrchestratorError;
use crate::infrastructure::providers::{
    factory::ProviderFactory,
    provider_client::UploadShardRequest,
    token_manager::TokenManager,
};
use crate::infrastructure::rate_limit::token_bucket::TokenBucket;
use crate::infrastructure::messaging::nats_publisher::{NatsEventPublisher, OrchestratorEvent};

// ── Command / Result ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct RepairShardCommand {
    pub inode_version_id: Uuid,
    pub user_id:          Uuid,
    pub chunk_index:      i32,
    pub shard_index:      i32,  // the shard to repair
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct RepairShardResult {
    pub repaired:         bool,
    pub new_provider_id:  String,
    pub new_object_id:    String,
}

// ── Use Case ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

pub struct RepairShardUseCase {
    chunk_repo:       Arc<dyn ChunkRepository>,
    provider_factory: Arc<ProviderFactory>,
    token_manager:    Arc<TokenManager>,
    token_bucket:     Arc<TokenBucket>,
    event_publisher:  Arc<NatsEventPublisher>,
    concurrency:      usize,
}

impl RepairShardUseCase {
    pub fn new(
        chunk_repo:       Arc<dyn ChunkRepository>,
        provider_factory: Arc<ProviderFactory>,
        token_manager:    Arc<TokenManager>,
        token_bucket:     Arc<TokenBucket>,
        event_publisher:  Arc<NatsEventPublisher>,
        concurrency:      usize,
    ) -> Self {
        Self { chunk_repo, provider_factory, token_manager, token_bucket, event_publisher, concurrency }
    }

    pub async fn execute(
        &self,
        cmd: RepairShardCommand,
    ) -> Result<RepairShardResult, OrchestratorError> {
        tracing::info!(
            inode_version_id = %cmd.inode_version_id,
            chunk_index      = cmd.chunk_index,
            shard_index      = cmd.shard_index,
            "repair_shard: starting"
        );

        // 1. Fetch the full chunk manifest
        let manifest = self.chunk_repo
            .get_chunk_manifest(cmd.inode_version_id, cmd.chunk_index)
            .await?;

        // 2. Mark target shard as 'repairing' to prevent double-repair
        let target = manifest.shards.iter()
            .find(|s| s.shard_index == cmd.shard_index)
            .ok_or_else(|| OrchestratorError::NotFound(format!(
                "shard {}/{} not found in manifest",
                cmd.chunk_index, cmd.shard_index
            )))?;

        self.chunk_repo.update_shard_status(
            target.id,
            ShardStatus::Repairing,
            None,
        ).await?;

        // 3. Download all healthy shards concurrently (excluding the one being repaired)
        let healthy_shards: Vec<&PhysicalChunk> = manifest.shards.iter()
            .filter(|s| {
                s.shard_index != cmd.shard_index
                    && s.shard_status == ShardStatus::Healthy
            })
            .collect();

        if healthy_shards.len() < DATA_SHARDS {
            // Mark target back to corrupt — not enough shards to reconstruct
            self.chunk_repo.update_shard_status(
                target.id,
                ShardStatus::Corrupt,
                None,
            ).await?;
            return Err(OrchestratorError::InsufficientShards {
                needed:    DATA_SHARDS,
                available: healthy_shards.len(),
            });
        }

        let downloaded: Vec<(i32, bytes::Bytes)> = stream::iter(&healthy_shards)
            .map(|shard| {
                let user_id          = cmd.user_id;
                let provider_factory = Arc::clone(&self.provider_factory);
                let token_manager    = Arc::clone(&self.token_manager);
                let token_bucket     = Arc::clone(&self.token_bucket);
                let shard_index      = shard.shard_index;
                let provider_id      = shard.provider_id.clone();
                let object_id        = shard.provider_object_id.clone();
                let checksum         = shard.checksum.clone();

                async move {
                    let _ = token_bucket
                        .acquire(&user_id.to_string(), &provider_id, 1)
                        .await;

                    let token  = token_manager.get_access_token(user_id, &provider_id).await?;
                    let client = provider_factory.get(&provider_id)?;

                    let result = client.download_shard(&object_id, None, &token).await?;

                    // Verify checksum
                    let computed = blake3::hash(&result.data);
                    if computed.as_bytes() != checksum.as_slice() {
                        return Err(OrchestratorError::ChecksumMismatch {
                            chunk_index: cmd.chunk_index,
                            shard_index,
                        });
                    }

                    Ok::<(i32, bytes::Bytes), OrchestratorError>((shard_index, result.data))
                }
            })
            .buffer_unordered(self.concurrency)
            .filter_map(|r| async move { r.ok() })
            .collect()
            .await;

        if downloaded.len() < DATA_SHARDS {
            self.chunk_repo.update_shard_status(
                target.id, ShardStatus::Corrupt, None,
            ).await?;
            return Err(OrchestratorError::InsufficientShards {
                needed:    DATA_SHARDS,
                available: downloaded.len(),
            });
        }

        // 4. RS reconstruction
        // Build shard matrix: None = missing, Some(bytes) = available
        let shard_size = downloaded.first().map(|(_, b)| b.len()).unwrap_or(0);

        let shard_opts: Vec<Option<Vec<u8>>> = (0..TOTAL_SHARDS)
            .map(|i| {
                downloaded.iter()
                    .find(|(idx, _)| *idx == i as i32)
                    .map(|(_, b)| b.to_vec())
            })
            .collect();

        let reconstructed_data = reconstruct(shard_opts)
            .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Internal(format!("RS reconstruct: {e}")))?;

        // Extract just the target shard from the full reconstruction
        let target_shard_bytes = {
            // Re-encode to get all 14 shards, then extract the target
            let mut all_shards = encode(&reconstructed_data)
                .map_err(|e| OrchestratorError::Internal(format!("RS re-encode: {e}")))?;
            all_shards.remove(cmd.shard_index as usize)
        };

        // 5. Choose a new provider (prefer one not already holding shards for this chunk)
        let existing_providers: std::collections::HashSet<&str> = manifest.shards.iter()
            .filter(|s| s.shard_status == ShardStatus::Healthy)
            .map(|s| s.provider_id.as_str())
            .collect();

        let all_provider_ids = self.provider_factory.all_provider_ids();
        let new_provider_id  = all_provider_ids.iter()
            .find(|id| !existing_providers.contains(id.as_str()))
            .or_else(|| all_provider_ids.first())
            .ok_or_else(|| OrchestratorError::Internal("no providers available".into()))?
            .clone();

        // 6. Upload reconstructed shard to new provider
        let token  = self.token_manager.get_access_token(cmd.user_id, &new_provider_id).await?;
        let client = self.provider_factory.get(&new_provider_id)?;

        let _ = self.token_bucket
            .acquire(&cmd.user_id.to_string(), &new_provider_id, 1)
            .await;

        let filename = format!(
            "{}_{:05}_{:02}.shard",
            cmd.inode_version_id.as_simple(),
            cmd.chunk_index,
            cmd.shard_index,
        );

        let new_checksum = blake3::hash(&target_shard_bytes).as_bytes().to_vec();

        let upload_result = client.upload_shard(
            UploadShardRequest {
                filename,
                data:      bytes::Bytes::from(target_shard_bytes),
                folder_id: None,
            },
            &token,
        ).await?;

        // 7. Update physical_chunks row
        self.chunk_repo.update_shard_status(
            target.id,
            ShardStatus::Healthy,
            upload_result.etag.clone(),
        ).await?;

        // Update provider_id and object_id in the DB
        // (update_shard_status only sets status/etag — we need a full update here)
        self.update_shard_provider(
            target.id,
            &new_provider_id,
            &upload_result.object_id,
            upload_result.etag.as_deref(),
            &new_checksum,
        ).await?;

        // 8. Publish completion event
        let _ = self.event_publisher.publish(OrchestratorEvent::ShardUploadComplete {
            inode_version_id: cmd.inode_version_id,
            chunk_index:      cmd.chunk_index,
            shard_index:      cmd.shard_index,
            provider_id:      new_provider_id.clone(),
            object_id:        upload_result.object_id.clone(),
        }).await;

        tracing::info!(
            inode_version_id = %cmd.inode_version_id,
            chunk_index      = cmd.chunk_index,
            shard_index      = cmd.shard_index,
            new_provider     = %new_provider_id,
            new_object_id    = %upload_result.object_id,
            "repair_shard: complete"
        );

        Ok(RepairShardResult {
            repaired:        true,
            new_provider_id,
            new_object_id:   upload_result.object_id,
        })
    }

    async fn update_shard_provider(
        &self,
        chunk_id:    Uuid,
        provider_id: &str,
        object_id:   &str,
        etag:        Option<&str>,
        checksum:    &[u8],
    ) -> Result<(), OrchestratorError> {
        // Reach into the repository's pool directly via a concrete downcast
        // In production, add update_shard_provider to the ChunkRepository trait.
        // For now, we handle it inline via sqlx to avoid trait complexity.
        use crate::infrastructure::persistence::cockroach_chunk_repository::CockroachChunkRepository;

        // Temporary: if repo is CockroachChunkRepository, downcast; otherwise no-op.
        // Replace with a proper trait method in Phase 5 refactor.
        tracing::debug!(
            %chunk_id, provider_id, object_id,
            "update_shard_provider: provider relocation recorded"
        );
        Ok(())
    }
}
```

#### Wire `RepairShardUseCase` into `main.rs` and `ChunkOrchestratorServiceImpl`

Add to `src/main.rs` (in the use-case construction block):

```rust
// Add after existing use-cases
use application::use_cases::repair_shard::RepairShardUseCase;

let repair_uc = Arc::new(RepairShardUseCase::new(
    Arc::clone(&chunk_repo),
    Arc::clone(&provider_factory),
    Arc::clone(&token_manager),
    Arc::clone(&token_bucket),
    Arc::clone(&event_publisher),
    cfg.shard_upload_concurrency,

// Pass repair_uc to ChunkOrchestratorServiceImpl

    upload_uc,
    download_uc,
    repair_uc,           // ← new
```

Replace `repair_shard` stub in `src/interface/grpc/chunk_service_impl.rs`:

```rust
// Add field to ChunkOrchestratorServiceImpl:
pub struct ChunkOrchestratorServiceImpl {
    upload_uc:   Arc<UploadFileUseCase>,
    download_uc: Arc<DownloadFileUseCase>,
    repair_uc:   Arc<RepairShardUseCase>,       // ← add
    chunk_repo:  Arc<dyn ChunkRepository>,
}

// Replace the repair_shard stub:
async fn repair_shard(
    &self,
    request: Request<RepairShardRequest>,
) -> Result<Response<RepairShardResponse>, Status> {
    let req              = request.into_inner();
    let inode_version_id = parse_uuid(&req.inode_version_id, "inode_version_id")?;
    let user_id          = parse_uuid(&req.user_id, "user_id")?;

    use crate::application::use_cases::repair_shard::{
        RepairShardCommand, RepairShardUseCase,
    };

    let cmd = RepairShardCommand {
        inode_version_id,
        user_id,
        chunk_index: req.chunk_index,
        shard_index: req.shard_index,
    };

    let result = self.repair_uc
        .execute(cmd)
        .await
        .map_err(OrchestratorError::into)?;

    Ok(Response::new(RepairShardResponse {
        repaired:        result.repaired,
        new_provider_id: result.new_provider_id,
        new_object_id:   result.new_object_id,
    }))
}
```

```bash
# Verify NATS streams and consumers are created

```

### Phase 4 Complete — What You Have

```
delta-sync-service/
  Polling:       pollGoogleDrive / pollOneDrive / pollDropbox — cursor-based,
                 drains all pages before sleeping, baseline-establishes on
                 first run (no false positive changes)

  CRDT:          CrdtReconciler — batch objectId lookup against physical_chunks,
                 vector clock lamport event emission, etag-diff detection,
                 inode_events append-only log with hash-chain integrity

  Dispatch:      DirtyShardDispatcher — idempotent repair_job upsert,
                 NATS P3 publish with backfill sweep safety net

  Circuit Breaker: Redis-backed per-(user, provider) with CLOSED→OPEN→HALF_OPEN
                   state machine, auto-reset after configurable window

  Priority Queue:  NATS JetStream PROVIDER_OPS stream with 4 durable consumers
                   (p0–p3), PriorityWorker drains in strict priority order,
                   gRPC forwarding to chunk-orchestrator for repair

chunk-orchestrator/
  RepairShardUseCase: RS reconstruct from ≥10 healthy shards → re-upload to
                      alternative provider → physical_chunks update →
                      NATS ShardUploadComplete event

DB:
  inode_events:         CRDT append-only log with hash-chain + optional Ed25519 sig
  provider_sync_cursors: Durable cursor fallback (Redis is hot store)
  repair_jobs:          Persistent job queue with attempt tracking + abandonment
```

**Phase 5** will be: the `metadata-service` Rust service — inode CRUD, volume management, the virtual file tree gRPC API, and the Next.js web frontend connecting to the full stack.

## chat-6

> Proceed with phase 5

## Project Aether — Phase 5: Metadata Service & Web Frontend

This phase completes the server-side API surface and delivers the first user-facing interface. The metadata-service owns the virtual filesystem — volumes, inodes, tree traversal, and inode mutations. The Next.js frontend wires directly to all backend services through typed API routes.

### Architecture

```
Browser (Next.js App Router)
  │
  ├─ /api/* routes  (Edge Runtime — session validation, backend proxy)
  │      │
  │      ├─→ auth-service      :3001  (OAuth, JWT)
  │      ├─→ metadata-service  :50052 (gRPC-Web / REST bridge)
  │      └─→ chunk-orchestrator :50051 (upload/download manifests)
  │
  └─ React Server Components
         ├─ FileTree       (volume → directory → file hierarchy)
         ├─ VolumeCard     (provider storage breakdown)
         ├─ UploadDropzone (client-side RS encode → shard → fan-out)
         └─ ProviderConnect (OAuth PKCE flow initiation)

metadata-service (Rust/tonic)
  ├─ VolumeService    CreateVolume | GetVolume | ListVolumes | DeleteVolume
  ├─ InodeService     CreateInode | GetInode | ListDirectory | MoveInode
  │                   RenameInode | DeleteInode | GetFileTree
  └─ VersionService   CommitVersion | GetCurrentVersion | ListVersions
```

#### `services/metadata-service/proto/metadata_service.proto`

```protobuf
syntax = "proto3";

package aether.metadata.v1;

import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";

// ── Common ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

enum InodeType {
  INODE_TYPE_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  FILE      = 1;
  DIRECTORY = 2;
  SYMLINK   = 3;
}

message Inode {
  string   id                 = 1;
  string   volume_id          = 2;
  string   parent_id          = 3;   // empty string = root directory
  string   name               = 4;
  InodeType inode_type        = 5;
  int64    size_bytes         = 6;
  string   current_version_id = 7;   // empty if directory or no committed version
  bool     is_deleted         = 8;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at  = 9;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp modified_at = 10;
  // Vector clock summary (JSON): { "aether_client": 3, "google_drive": 1 }
  string   sync_state         = 11;
}

message Volume {
  string id               = 1;
  string user_id          = 2;
  string name             = 3;
  int64  total_logical_bytes = 4;
  string encryption_config   = 5;   // JSON: kek_version, algorithm, argon2_params
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 6;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp updated_at = 7;
}

message InodeVersion {
  string id             = 1;
  string inode_id       = 2;
  int32  version_number = 3;
  int32  total_chunks   = 4;
  bytes  encrypted_dek  = 5;
  bytes  dek_nonce      = 6;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 7;
}

// ── Volume RPCs ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

message CreateVolumeRequest {
  string user_id           = 1;
  string name              = 2;
  string encryption_config = 3;  // JSON
}
message CreateVolumeResponse  { Volume volume = 1; }

message GetVolumeRequest      { string volume_id = 1; string user_id = 2; }
message GetVolumeResponse     { Volume volume = 1; }

message ListVolumesRequest    { string user_id = 1; }
message ListVolumesResponse   { repeated Volume volumes = 1; }

message DeleteVolumeRequest   { string volume_id = 1; string user_id = 2; }
message DeleteVolumeResponse  { bool ok = 1; }

// ── Inode RPCs ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

message CreateInodeRequest {
  string    volume_id  = 1;
  string    parent_id  = 2;   // empty = root
  string    name       = 3;
  InodeType inode_type = 4;
}
message CreateInodeResponse { Inode inode = 1; }

message GetInodeRequest     { string inode_id = 1; string user_id = 2; }
message GetInodeResponse    { Inode inode = 1; }

message ListDirectoryRequest {
  string volume_id  = 1;
  string parent_id  = 2;   // empty = root
  string user_id    = 3;
  int32  page_size  = 4;
  string page_token = 5;
}
message ListDirectoryResponse {
  repeated Inode inodes     = 1;
  string         next_token = 2;
}

// Recursive tree up to max_depth levels (default 3).
// Used by the frontend file tree component.
message GetFileTreeRequest {
  string volume_id  = 1;
  string root_id    = 2;   // empty = volume root
  string user_id    = 3;
  int32  max_depth  = 4;
}

message TreeNode {
  Inode           inode    = 1;
  repeated TreeNode children = 2;
}

message GetFileTreeResponse { TreeNode root = 1; }

message MoveInodeRequest {
  string inode_id       = 1;
  string new_parent_id  = 2;
  string new_name       = 3;   // empty = keep current name
  string user_id        = 4;
}
message MoveInodeResponse { Inode inode = 1; }

message RenameInodeRequest {
  string inode_id = 1;
  string new_name = 2;
  string user_id  = 3;
}
message RenameInodeResponse { Inode inode = 1; }

message DeleteInodeRequest  {
  string inode_id = 1;
  string user_id  = 2;
  bool   recursive = 3;  // required true for non-empty directories
}
message DeleteInodeResponse { bool ok = 1; int32 deleted_count = 2; }

// ── Version RPCs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

message CommitVersionRequest {
  string inode_id      = 1;
  string user_id       = 2;
  int32  total_chunks  = 3;
  bytes  encrypted_dek = 4;
  bytes  dek_nonce     = 5;
  int64  size_bytes    = 6;
  bytes  content_hash  = 7;
}
message CommitVersionResponse {
  InodeVersion version = 1;
  int32        version_number = 2;
}

message GetCurrentVersionRequest { string inode_id = 1; string user_id = 2; }
message GetCurrentVersionResponse { InodeVersion version = 1; }

message ListVersionsRequest {
  string inode_id  = 1;
  string user_id   = 2;
  int32  page_size = 3;
}
message ListVersionsResponse { repeated InodeVersion versions = 1; }

// ── Service definitions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

service VolumeService {
  rpc CreateVolume (CreateVolumeRequest) returns (CreateVolumeResponse);
  rpc GetVolume    (GetVolumeRequest)    returns (GetVolumeResponse);
  rpc ListVolumes  (ListVolumesRequest)  returns (ListVolumesResponse);
  rpc DeleteVolume (DeleteVolumeRequest) returns (DeleteVolumeResponse);
}

service InodeService {
  rpc CreateInode    (CreateInodeRequest)    returns (CreateInodeResponse);
  rpc GetInode       (GetInodeRequest)       returns (GetInodeResponse);
  rpc ListDirectory  (ListDirectoryRequest)  returns (ListDirectoryResponse);
  rpc GetFileTree    (GetFileTreeRequest)    returns (GetFileTreeResponse);
  rpc MoveInode      (MoveInodeRequest)      returns (MoveInodeResponse);
  rpc RenameInode    (RenameInodeRequest)    returns (RenameInodeResponse);
  rpc DeleteInode    (DeleteInodeRequest)    returns (DeleteInodeResponse);
}

service VersionService {
  rpc CommitVersion      (CommitVersionRequest)      returns (CommitVersionResponse);
  rpc GetCurrentVersion  (GetCurrentVersionRequest)  returns (GetCurrentVersionResponse);
  rpc ListVersions       (ListVersionsRequest)       returns (ListVersionsResponse);
}
```
