```rust
use serde::Deserialize;

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct AppConfig {
    #[serde(default = "default_host")]
    pub host:        String,
    #[serde(default = "default_port")]
    pub grpc_port:   u16,
    pub database_url: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_pool_size")]
    pub db_pool_size: u32,
    #[serde(default = "default_page_size")]
    pub default_page_size: i64,
    #[serde(default = "default_max_depth")]
    pub max_tree_depth: i32,
}

fn default_host()      -> String { "0.0.0.0".into() }
fn default_port()      -> u16    { 50052 }
fn default_pool_size() -> u32    { 20 }
fn default_page_size() -> i64    { 100 }
fn default_max_depth() -> i32    { 5 }

impl AppConfig {
    pub fn load() -> anyhow::Result<Self> {
        dotenvy::dotenv().ok();
        Ok(config::Config::builder()
            .add_source(config::File::with_name("config/default").required(false))
            .add_source(config::Environment::default().separator("__").try_parsing(true))
            .build()?
            .try_deserialize()?)
    }
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/error.rs`

```rust
use thiserror::Error;

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum MetadataError {
    #[error("not found: {0}")]
    NotFound(String),

    #[error("conflict: {0}")]
    Conflict(String),

    #[error("forbidden: {0}")]
    Forbidden(String),

    #[error("invalid argument: {0}")]
    InvalidArgument(String),

    #[error("database error: {0}")]
    Database(#[from] sqlx::Error),

    #[error("internal: {0}")]
    Internal(String),
}

impl From<MetadataError> for tonic::Status {
    fn from(e: MetadataError) -> Self {
        match &e {
            MetadataError::NotFound(m)       => tonic::Status::not_found(m),
            MetadataError::Conflict(m)       => tonic::Status::already_exists(m),
            MetadataError::Forbidden(m)      => tonic::Status::permission_denied(m),
            MetadataError::InvalidArgument(m)=> tonic::Status::invalid_argument(m),
            MetadataError::Database(e)       => tonic::Status::internal(e.to_string()),
            MetadataError::Internal(m)       => tonic::Status::internal(m),
        }
    }
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/domain/entities.rs`

```rust
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

// ── Volume ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EncryptionConfig {
    pub kek_version:   u32,
    pub algorithm:     String,
    pub argon2_params: Argon2Params,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Argon2Params {
    pub t: u32,
    pub m: u32,
    pub p: u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Volume {
    pub id:                  Uuid,
    pub user_id:             Uuid,
    pub name:                String,
    pub total_logical_bytes: i64,
    pub encryption_config:   EncryptionConfig,
    pub is_deleted:          bool,
    pub created_at:          DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at:          DateTime<Utc>,
}

// ── Inode ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, sqlx::Type)]
#[sqlx(type_name = "inode_type", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum InodeType {
    File,
    Directory,
    Symlink,
}

impl std::fmt::Display for InodeType {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            InodeType::File      => write!(f, "file"),
            InodeType::Directory => write!(f, "directory"),
            InodeType::Symlink   => write!(f, "symlink"),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Inode {
    pub id:                 Uuid,
    pub volume_id:          Uuid,
    pub parent_id:          Option<Uuid>,
    pub name:               String,
    pub inode_type:         InodeType,
    pub size_bytes:         i64,
    pub current_version_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub content_hash:       Option<Vec<u8>>,
    pub is_deleted:         bool,
    pub sync_state:         serde_json::Value,
    pub created_at:         DateTime<Utc>,
    pub modified_at:        DateTime<Utc>,
}

impl Inode {
    pub fn new_directory(volume_id: Uuid, parent_id: Option<Uuid>, name: String) -> Self {
        let now = Utc::now();
        Self {
            id:                 Uuid::new_v4(),
            volume_id,
            parent_id,
            name,
            inode_type:         InodeType::Directory,
            size_bytes:         0,
            current_version_id: None,
            content_hash:       None,
            is_deleted:         false,
            sync_state:         serde_json::json!({}),
            created_at:         now,
            modified_at:        now,
        }
    }

    pub fn new_file(volume_id: Uuid, parent_id: Uuid, name: String) -> Self {
        let now = Utc::now();
        Self {
            id:                 Uuid::new_v4(),
            volume_id,
            parent_id:          Some(parent_id),
            name,
            inode_type:         InodeType::File,
            size_bytes:         0,
            current_version_id: None,
            content_hash:       None,
            is_deleted:         false,
            sync_state:         serde_json::json!({}),
            created_at:         now,
            modified_at:        now,
        }
    }
}

// ── InodeVersion ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct InodeVersion {
    pub id:             Uuid,
    pub inode_id:       Uuid,
    pub version_number: i32,
    pub total_chunks:   i32,
    pub encrypted_dek:  Vec<u8>,
    pub dek_nonce:      Vec<u8>,
    pub created_at:     DateTime<Utc>,
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/domain/repositories.rs`

```rust
use async_trait::async_trait;
use uuid::Uuid;
use crate::domain::entities::{Inode, InodeType, InodeVersion, Volume};
use crate::error::MetadataError;

#[async_trait]
pub trait VolumeRepository: Send + Sync {
    async fn create(&self, volume: Volume) -> Result<Volume, MetadataError>;
    async fn find_by_id(&self, id: Uuid, user_id: Uuid) -> Result<Volume, MetadataError>;
    async fn list_by_user(&self, user_id: Uuid) -> Result<Vec<Volume>, MetadataError>;
    async fn soft_delete(&self, id: Uuid, user_id: Uuid) -> Result<(), MetadataError>;
    async fn update_logical_size(&self, id: Uuid, delta_bytes: i64) -> Result<(), MetadataError>;
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait InodeRepository: Send + Sync {
    async fn create(&self, inode: Inode) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError>;
    async fn find_by_id(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError>;

    /// List direct children of parent_id (None = volume root).
    async fn list_children(
        &self,
        volume_id:   Uuid,
        parent_id:   Option<Uuid>,
        page_size:   i64,
        after_id:    Option<Uuid>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Inode>, MetadataError>;

    /// Recursive tree fetch up to max_depth levels.
    async fn get_tree(
        &self,
        volume_id: Uuid,
        root_id:   Option<Uuid>,
        max_depth: i32,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Inode>, MetadataError>;

    async fn move_inode(
        &self,
        id:            Uuid,
        new_parent_id: Uuid,
        new_name:      Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError>;

    async fn rename(
        &self,
        id:       Uuid,
        new_name: String,
    ) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError>;

    /// Soft-delete. If recursive=true, deletes entire subtree.
    async fn soft_delete(
        &self,
        id:        Uuid,
        recursive: bool,
    ) -> Result<i32, MetadataError>;  // returns count of deleted inodes

    async fn set_current_version(
        &self,
        id:         Uuid,
        version_id: Uuid,
        size_bytes: i64,
        content_hash: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    ) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError>;
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait VersionRepository: Send + Sync {
    async fn create(&self, version: InodeVersion) -> Result<InodeVersion, MetadataError>;

    async fn find_by_id(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<InodeVersion, MetadataError>;

    async fn find_current(
        &self,
        inode_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<InodeVersion, MetadataError>;

    async fn list_by_inode(
        &self,
        inode_id:  Uuid,
        page_size: i32,
    ) -> Result<Vec<InodeVersion>, MetadataError>;

    async fn next_version_number(
        &self,
        inode_id: Uuid,
    ) -> Result<i32, MetadataError>;
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/infrastructure/db/volume_repo.rs`

```rust
use async_trait::async_trait;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::domain::entities::{EncryptionConfig, Volume};
use crate::domain::repositories::VolumeRepository;
use crate::error::MetadataError;

pub struct PgVolumeRepository { pub pool: PgPool }

#[async_trait]
impl VolumeRepository for PgVolumeRepository {
    async fn create(&self, v: Volume) -> Result<Volume, MetadataError> {
        let cfg_json = serde_json::to_value(&v.encryption_config)
            .map_err(|e| MetadataError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;

        sqlx::query!(
            r#"INSERT INTO volumes
                 (id, user_id, name, total_logical_bytes, encryption_config)
               VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)"#,
            v.id, v.user_id, v.name, v.total_logical_bytes, cfg_json,
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        self.find_by_id(v.id, v.user_id).await
    }

    async fn find_by_id(&self, id: Uuid, user_id: Uuid) -> Result<Volume, MetadataError> {
        struct Row {
            id:                  Uuid,
            user_id:             Uuid,
            name:                String,
            total_logical_bytes: i64,
            encryption_config:   serde_json::Value,
            is_deleted:          bool,
            created_at:          chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
            updated_at:          chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
        }

        let row = sqlx::query_as!(
            Row,
            r#"SELECT id, user_id, name, total_logical_bytes,
                      encryption_config, is_deleted, created_at, updated_at
               FROM volumes
               WHERE id = $1 AND user_id = $2 AND is_deleted = false"#,
            id, user_id,
        )
        .fetch_optional(&self.pool)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| MetadataError::NotFound(format!("volume {id}")))?;

        let encryption_config: EncryptionConfig = serde_json::from_value(row.encryption_config)
            .map_err(|e| MetadataError::Internal(format!("parse encryption_config: {e}")))?;

        Ok(Volume {
            id:                  row.id,
            user_id:             row.user_id,
            name:                row.name,
            total_logical_bytes: row.total_logical_bytes,
            encryption_config,
            is_deleted:          row.is_deleted,
            created_at:          row.created_at,
            updated_at:          row.updated_at,
        })
    }

    async fn list_by_user(&self, user_id: Uuid) -> Result<Vec<Volume>, MetadataError> {
        struct Row {
            id:                  Uuid,
            user_id:             Uuid,
            name:                String,
            total_logical_bytes: i64,
            encryption_config:   serde_json::Value,
            is_deleted:          bool,
            created_at:          chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
            updated_at:          chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
        }

        let rows = sqlx::query_as!(
            Row,
            r#"SELECT id, user_id, name, total_logical_bytes,
                      encryption_config, is_deleted, created_at, updated_at
               FROM volumes
               WHERE user_id = $1 AND is_deleted = false
               ORDER BY created_at ASC"#,
            user_id,
        )
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        rows.into_iter().map(|row| {
            let encryption_config: EncryptionConfig =
                serde_json::from_value(row.encryption_config)
                    .map_err(|e| MetadataError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;
            Ok(Volume {
                id: row.id, user_id: row.user_id, name: row.name,
                total_logical_bytes: row.total_logical_bytes,
                encryption_config, is_deleted: row.is_deleted,
                created_at: row.created_at, updated_at: row.updated_at,
            })
        }).collect()
    }

    async fn soft_delete(&self, id: Uuid, user_id: Uuid) -> Result<(), MetadataError> {
        let result = sqlx::query!(
            "UPDATE volumes SET is_deleted = true, updated_at = now()
             WHERE id = $1 AND user_id = $2 AND is_deleted = false",
            id, user_id,
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        if result.rows_affected() == 0 {
            return Err(MetadataError::NotFound(format!("volume {id}")));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn update_logical_size(&self, id: Uuid, delta_bytes: i64) -> Result<(), MetadataError> {
        sqlx::query!(
            "UPDATE volumes
             SET total_logical_bytes = GREATEST(0, total_logical_bytes + $1),
                 updated_at = now()
             WHERE id = $2",
            delta_bytes, id,
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/infrastructure/db/inode_repo.rs`

```rust
use async_trait::async_trait;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::domain::entities::{Inode, InodeType};
use crate::domain::repositories::InodeRepository;
use crate::error::MetadataError;

pub struct PgInodeRepository { pub pool: PgPool }

// Internal DB row type
struct InodeRow {
    id:                 Uuid,
    volume_id:          Uuid,
    parent_id:          Option<Uuid>,
    name:               String,
    inode_type:         String,
    size_bytes:         i64,
    current_version_id: Option<Uuid>,
    content_hash:       Option<Vec<u8>>,
    is_deleted:         bool,
    sync_state:         serde_json::Value,
    created_at:         chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
    modified_at:        chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
}

impl From<InodeRow> for Inode {
    fn from(r: InodeRow) -> Self {
        Inode {
            id:                 r.id,
            volume_id:          r.volume_id,
            parent_id:          r.parent_id,
            name:               r.name,
            inode_type:         match r.inode_type.as_str() {
                "file"      => InodeType::File,
                "directory" => InodeType::Directory,
                _           => InodeType::Symlink,
            },
            size_bytes:         r.size_bytes,
            current_version_id: r.current_version_id,
            content_hash:       r.content_hash,
            is_deleted:         r.is_deleted,
            sync_state:         r.sync_state,
            created_at:         r.created_at,
            modified_at:        r.modified_at,
        }
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl InodeRepository for PgInodeRepository {
    async fn create(&self, inode: Inode) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError> {
        sqlx::query!(
            r#"INSERT INTO inodes
                 (id, volume_id, parent_id, name, inode_type,
                  size_bytes, sync_state)
               VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5::inode_type, $6, $7)"#,
            inode.id,
            inode.volume_id,
            inode.parent_id,
            inode.name,
            inode.inode_type.to_string(),
            inode.size_bytes,
            inode.sync_state,
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| {
            if e.to_string().contains("uq_inodes_parent_name") {
                MetadataError::Conflict(format!("'{}' already exists in this directory", inode.name))
            } else {
                MetadataError::Database(e)
            }
        })?;

        self.find_by_id(inode.id).await
    }

    async fn find_by_id(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError> {
        let row = sqlx::query_as!(
            InodeRow,
            r#"SELECT id, volume_id, parent_id, name,
                      inode_type::text as "inode_type!",
                      size_bytes, current_version_id,
                      content_hash, is_deleted,
                      sync_state, created_at, modified_at
               FROM inodes WHERE id = $1 AND is_deleted = false"#,
            id,
        )
        .fetch_optional(&self.pool)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| MetadataError::NotFound(format!("inode {id}")))?;

        Ok(row.into())
    }

    async fn list_children(
        &self,
        volume_id: Uuid,
        parent_id: Option<Uuid>,
        page_size: i64,
        after_id:  Option<Uuid>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Inode>, MetadataError> {
        // CockroachDB-compatible keyset pagination on (name, id)
        let rows = if let Some(parent) = parent_id {
            sqlx::query_as!(
                InodeRow,
                r#"SELECT id, volume_id, parent_id, name,
                          inode_type::text as "inode_type!",
                          size_bytes, current_version_id,
                          content_hash, is_deleted,
                          sync_state, created_at, modified_at
                   FROM inodes
                   WHERE volume_id = $1
                     AND parent_id = $2
                     AND is_deleted = false
                     AND ($3::uuid IS NULL OR id > $3)
                   ORDER BY inode_type DESC, name ASC, id ASC
                   LIMIT $4"#,
                volume_id, parent, after_id, page_size,
            )
            .fetch_all(&self.pool)
            .await?
        } else {
            sqlx::query_as!(
                InodeRow,
                r#"SELECT id, volume_id, parent_id, name,
                          inode_type::text as "inode_type!",
                          size_bytes, current_version_id,
                          content_hash, is_deleted,
                          sync_state, created_at, modified_at
                   FROM inodes
                   WHERE volume_id = $1
                     AND parent_id IS NULL
                     AND is_deleted = false
                     AND ($2::uuid IS NULL OR id > $2)
                   ORDER BY inode_type DESC, name ASC, id ASC
                   LIMIT $3"#,
                volume_id, after_id, page_size,
            )
            .fetch_all(&self.pool)
            .await?
        };

        Ok(rows.into_iter().map(Inode::from).collect())
    }

    async fn get_tree(
        &self,
        volume_id: Uuid,
        root_id:   Option<Uuid>,
        max_depth: i32,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Inode>, MetadataError> {
        // CockroachDB supports recursive CTEs. We fetch all nodes in the subtree
        // up to max_depth levels, then assemble them into a tree in Rust.
        let rows = sqlx::query_as!(
            InodeRow,
            r#"WITH RECURSIVE tree AS (
                 SELECT i.*, 0 AS depth
                 FROM inodes i
                 WHERE i.volume_id = $1
                   AND ($2::uuid IS NULL AND i.parent_id IS NULL
                        OR i.id = $2)
                   AND i.is_deleted = false
                 UNION ALL
                 SELECT c.*, t.depth + 1
                 FROM inodes c
                 JOIN tree t ON c.parent_id = t.id
                 WHERE c.is_deleted = false
                   AND t.depth < $3
               )
               SELECT id, volume_id, parent_id, name,
                      inode_type::text as "inode_type!",
                      size_bytes, current_version_id,
                      content_hash, is_deleted,
                      sync_state, created_at, modified_at
               FROM tree
               ORDER BY depth ASC, inode_type DESC, name ASC"#,
            volume_id, root_id, max_depth,
        )
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(rows.into_iter().map(Inode::from).collect())
    }

    async fn move_inode(
        &self,
        id:            Uuid,
        new_parent_id: Uuid,
        new_name:      Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError> {
        sqlx::query!(
            r#"UPDATE inodes
               SET parent_id  = $2,
                   name       = COALESCE($3, name),
                   modified_at = now()
               WHERE id = $1 AND is_deleted = false"#,
            id, new_parent_id, new_name,
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| {
            if e.to_string().contains("uq_inodes_parent_name") {
                MetadataError::Conflict("a file with that name already exists at the destination".into())
            } else {
                MetadataError::Database(e)
            }
        })?;

        self.find_by_id(id).await
    }

    async fn rename(&self, id: Uuid, new_name: String) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError> {
        if new_name.is_empty() || new_name.contains('/') {
            return Err(MetadataError::InvalidArgument(
                "name cannot be empty or contain '/'".into()
            ));
        }

        sqlx::query!(
            "UPDATE inodes SET name = $2, modified_at = now()
             WHERE id = $1 AND is_deleted = false",
            id, new_name,
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| {
            if e.to_string().contains("uq_inodes_parent_name") {
                MetadataError::Conflict(format!("'{}' already exists in this directory", new_name))
            } else {
                MetadataError::Database(e)
            }
        })?;

        self.find_by_id(id).await
    }

    async fn soft_delete(&self, id: Uuid, recursive: bool) -> Result<i32, MetadataError> {
        if recursive {
            // Delete the entire subtree via recursive CTE
            let result = sqlx::query!(
                r#"WITH RECURSIVE subtree AS (
                     SELECT id FROM inodes WHERE id = $1 AND is_deleted = false
                     UNION ALL
                     SELECT c.id FROM inodes c
                     JOIN subtree s ON c.parent_id = s.id
                     WHERE c.is_deleted = false
                   )
                   UPDATE inodes SET is_deleted = true, modified_at = now()
                   WHERE id IN (SELECT id FROM subtree)"#,
                id,
            )
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await?;

            Ok(result.rows_affected() as i32)
        } else {
            // Non-recursive: only delete if no children exist
            let child_count: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar!(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inodes WHERE parent_id = $1 AND is_deleted = false",
                id,
            )
            .fetch_one(&self.pool)
            .await?
            .unwrap_or(0);

            if child_count > 0 {
                return Err(MetadataError::Conflict(
                    "directory is not empty; use recursive=true to delete".into()
                ));
            }

            sqlx::query!(
                "UPDATE inodes SET is_deleted = true, modified_at = now() WHERE id = $1",
                id,
            )
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await?;

            Ok(1)
        }
    }

    async fn set_current_version(
        &self,
        id:           Uuid,
        version_id:   Uuid,
        size_bytes:   i64,
        content_hash: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    ) -> Result<Inode, MetadataError> {
        sqlx::query!(
            r#"UPDATE inodes
               SET current_version_id = $2,
                   size_bytes          = $3,
                   content_hash        = $4,
                   modified_at         = now()
               WHERE id = $1 AND is_deleted = false"#,
            id, version_id, size_bytes, content_hash,
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        self.find_by_id(id).await
    }
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/infrastructure/db/version_repo.rs`

```rust
use async_trait::async_trait;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::domain::entities::InodeVersion;
use crate::domain::repositories::VersionRepository;
use crate::error::MetadataError;

pub struct PgVersionRepository { pub pool: PgPool }

#[async_trait]
impl VersionRepository for PgVersionRepository {
    async fn create(&self, v: InodeVersion) -> Result<InodeVersion, MetadataError> {
        sqlx::query!(
            r#"INSERT INTO inode_versions
                 (id, inode_id, version_number, total_chunks, encrypted_dek, dek_nonce)
               VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)"#,
            v.id, v.inode_id, v.version_number, v.total_chunks,
            v.encrypted_dek, v.dek_nonce,
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        self.find_by_id(v.id).await
    }

    async fn find_by_id(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<InodeVersion, MetadataError> {
        struct Row {
            id: Uuid, inode_id: Uuid, version_number: i32,
            total_chunks: i32, encrypted_dek: Vec<u8>, dek_nonce: Vec<u8>,
            created_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
        }
        let r = sqlx::query_as!(Row,
            "SELECT id, inode_id, version_number, total_chunks,
                    encrypted_dek, dek_nonce, created_at
             FROM inode_versions WHERE id = $1", id,
        )
        .fetch_optional(&self.pool).await?
        .ok_or_else(|| MetadataError::NotFound(format!("version {id}")))?;

        Ok(InodeVersion { id: r.id, inode_id: r.inode_id,
            version_number: r.version_number, total_chunks: r.total_chunks,
            encrypted_dek: r.encrypted_dek, dek_nonce: r.dek_nonce,
            created_at: r.created_at })
    }

    async fn find_current(&self, inode_id: Uuid) -> Result<InodeVersion, MetadataError> {
        struct Row {
            id: Uuid, inode_id: Uuid, version_number: i32,
            total_chunks: i32, encrypted_dek: Vec<u8>, dek_nonce: Vec<u8>,
            created_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
        }
        let r = sqlx::query_as!(Row,
            r#"SELECT iv.id, iv.inode_id, iv.version_number, iv.total_chunks,
                      iv.encrypted_dek, iv.dek_nonce, iv.created_at
               FROM inode_versions iv
               JOIN inodes i ON i.current_version_id = iv.id
               WHERE i.id = $1 AND i.is_deleted = false"#,
            inode_id,
        )
        .fetch_optional(&self.pool).await?
        .ok_or_else(|| MetadataError::NotFound(
            format!("no current version for inode {inode_id}")
        ))?;

        Ok(InodeVersion { id: r.id, inode_id: r.inode_id,
            version_number: r.version_number, total_chunks: r.total_chunks,
            encrypted_dek: r.encrypted_dek, dek_nonce: r.dek_nonce,
            created_at: r.created_at })
    }

    async fn list_by_inode(
        &self,
        inode_id: Uuid,
        page_size: i32,
    ) -> Result<Vec<InodeVersion>, MetadataError> {
        struct Row {
            id: Uuid, inode_id: Uuid, version_number: i32,
            total_chunks: i32, encrypted_dek: Vec<u8>, dek_nonce: Vec<u8>,
            created_at: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
        }
        let rows = sqlx::query_as!(Row,
            r#"SELECT id, inode_id, version_number, total_chunks,
                      encrypted_dek, dek_nonce, created_at
               FROM inode_versions
               WHERE inode_id = $1
               ORDER BY version_number DESC
               LIMIT $2"#,
            inode_id, page_size as i64,
        )
        .fetch_all(&self.pool).await?;

        Ok(rows.into_iter().map(|r| InodeVersion {
            id: r.id, inode_id: r.inode_id, version_number: r.version_number,
            total_chunks: r.total_chunks, encrypted_dek: r.encrypted_dek,
            dek_nonce: r.dek_nonce, created_at: r.created_at,
        }).collect())
    }

    async fn next_version_number(&self, inode_id: Uuid) -> Result<i32, MetadataError> {
        let n: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar!(
            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version_number), 0) + 1
             FROM inode_versions WHERE inode_id = $1",
            inode_id,
        )
        .fetch_one(&self.pool)
        .await?
        .unwrap_or(1);

        Ok(n as i32)
    }
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/interface/grpc/converters.rs`

```rust
//! Converts between domain entities and protobuf messages.

use prost_types::Timestamp;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use uuid::Uuid;
use crate::domain::entities::{Inode, InodeType, InodeVersion, Volume};

tonic::include_proto!("aether.metadata.v1");

// ── Timestamp helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

pub fn to_ts(dt: DateTime<Utc>) -> Option<Timestamp> {
    Some(Timestamp {
        seconds: dt.timestamp(),
        nanos:   dt.timestamp_subsec_nanos() as i32,
    })
}

// ── Volume ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

pub fn volume_to_proto(v: Volume) -> crate::interface::grpc::converters::volume_service_server::Volume {
    use crate::interface::grpc::converters::Volume as PbVolume;
    PbVolume {
        id:                  v.id.to_string(),
        user_id:             v.user_id.to_string(),
        name:                v.name,
        total_logical_bytes: v.total_logical_bytes,
        encryption_config:   serde_json::to_string(&v.encryption_config).unwrap_or_default(),
        created_at:          to_ts(v.created_at),
        updated_at:          to_ts(v.updated_at),
    }
}

// ── Inode ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

pub fn inode_to_proto(i: Inode) -> crate::interface::grpc::converters::Inode {
    use crate::interface::grpc::converters::{Inode as PbInode, InodeType as PbInodeType};
    PbInode {
        id:                  i.id.to_string(),
        volume_id:           i.volume_id.to_string(),
        parent_id:           i.parent_id.map(|u| u.to_string()).unwrap_or_default(),
        name:                i.name,
        inode_type:          match i.inode_type {
            InodeType::File      => PbInodeType::File as i32,
            InodeType::Directory => PbInodeType::Directory as i32,
            InodeType::Symlink   => PbInodeType::Symlink as i32,
        },
        size_bytes:          i.size_bytes,
        current_version_id:  i.current_version_id
                                .map(|u| u.to_string()).unwrap_or_default(),
        is_deleted:          i.is_deleted,
        sync_state:          i.sync_state.to_string(),
        created_at:          to_ts(i.created_at),
        modified_at:         to_ts(i.modified_at),
    }
}

pub fn version_to_proto(v: InodeVersion) -> crate::interface::grpc::converters::InodeVersion {
    use crate::interface::grpc::converters::InodeVersion as PbVersion;
    PbVersion {
        id:             v.id.to_string(),
        inode_id:       v.inode_id.to_string(),
        version_number: v.version_number,
        total_chunks:   v.total_chunks,
        encrypted_dek:  v.encrypted_dek,
        dek_nonce:      v.dek_nonce,
        created_at:     to_ts(v.created_at),
    }
}

pub fn parse_uuid(s: &str, field: &str) -> Result<Uuid, tonic::Status> {
    Uuid::parse_str(s).map_err(|_| {
        tonic::Status::invalid_argument(format!("{field}: invalid UUID '{s}'"))
    })
}

pub fn parse_opt_uuid(s: &str) -> Option<Uuid> {
    if s.is_empty() { None } else { Uuid::parse_str(s).ok() }
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/interface/grpc/volume_service.rs`

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use tonic::{Request, Response, Status};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::domain::entities::{Argon2Params, EncryptionConfig, Volume};
use crate::domain::repositories::VolumeRepository;
use crate::error::MetadataError;
use super::converters::*;

pub use volume_service_server::{VolumeService, VolumeServiceServer};

pub struct VolumeServiceImpl {
    repo: Arc<dyn VolumeRepository>,
}

impl VolumeServiceImpl {
    pub fn new(repo: Arc<dyn VolumeRepository>) -> Self { Self { repo } }
}

#[tonic::async_trait]
impl VolumeService for VolumeServiceImpl {
    async fn create_volume(
        &self,
        req: Request<CreateVolumeRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<CreateVolumeResponse>, Status> {
        let r       = req.into_inner();
        let user_id = parse_uuid(&r.user_id, "user_id")?;

        let encryption_config: EncryptionConfig = serde_json::from_str(&r.encryption_config)
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| EncryptionConfig {
                kek_version:   1,
                algorithm:     "AES-256-GCM".into(),
                argon2_params: Argon2Params { t: 3, m: 65536, p: 4 },
            });

        let volume = Volume {
            id:                  Uuid::new_v4(),
            user_id,
            name:                r.name,
            total_logical_bytes: 0,
            encryption_config,
            is_deleted:          false,
            created_at:          chrono::Utc::now(),
            updated_at:          chrono::Utc::now(),
        };

        let created = self.repo.create(volume).await.map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(CreateVolumeResponse { volume: Some(volume_to_proto(created)) }))
    }

    async fn get_volume(
        &self,
        req: Request<GetVolumeRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<GetVolumeResponse>, Status> {
        let r         = req.into_inner();
        let volume_id = parse_uuid(&r.volume_id, "volume_id")?;
        let user_id   = parse_uuid(&r.user_id, "user_id")?;
        let v = self.repo.find_by_id(volume_id, user_id).await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(GetVolumeResponse { volume: Some(volume_to_proto(v)) }))
    }

    async fn list_volumes(
        &self,
        req: Request<ListVolumesRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<ListVolumesResponse>, Status> {
        let user_id = parse_uuid(&req.into_inner().user_id, "user_id")?;
        let volumes  = self.repo.list_by_user(user_id).await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(ListVolumesResponse {
            volumes: volumes.into_iter().map(volume_to_proto).collect(),
        }))
    }

    async fn delete_volume(
        &self,
        req: Request<DeleteVolumeRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<DeleteVolumeResponse>, Status> {
        let r         = req.into_inner();
        let volume_id = parse_uuid(&r.volume_id, "volume_id")?;
        let user_id   = parse_uuid(&r.user_id, "user_id")?;
        self.repo.soft_delete(volume_id, user_id).await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(DeleteVolumeResponse { ok: true }))
    }
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/interface/grpc/inode_service.rs`

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use tonic::{Request, Response, Status};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::domain::entities::{Inode, InodeType};
use crate::domain::repositories::InodeRepository;
use crate::error::MetadataError;
use super::converters::*;

pub use inode_service_server::{InodeService, InodeServiceServer};

pub struct InodeServiceImpl {
    repo:           Arc<dyn InodeRepository>,
    default_page:   i64,
    max_depth:      i32,
}

impl InodeServiceImpl {
    pub fn new(
        repo:         Arc<dyn InodeRepository>,
        default_page: i64,
        max_depth:    i32,
    ) -> Self {
        Self { repo, default_page, max_depth }
    }

    fn build_tree(
        inodes:    Vec<Inode>,
        parent_id: Option<Uuid>,
    ) -> Vec<TreeNode> {
        inodes.iter()
            .filter(|i| i.parent_id == parent_id)
            .map(|i| {
                let children = Self::build_tree(inodes.clone(), Some(i.id));
                TreeNode {
                    inode:    Some(inode_to_proto(i.clone())),
                    children,
                }
            })
            .collect()
    }
}

#[tonic::async_trait]
impl InodeService for InodeServiceImpl {
    async fn create_inode(
        &self,
        req: Request<CreateInodeRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<CreateInodeResponse>, Status> {
        let r         = req.into_inner();
        let volume_id = parse_uuid(&r.volume_id, "volume_id")?;
        let parent_id = parse_opt_uuid(&r.parent_id);

        if r.name.is_empty() || r.name.contains('/') {
            return Err(Status::invalid_argument("name: cannot be empty or contain '/'"));
        }

        let inode = match r.inode_type {
            t if t == InodeType::File as i32 => {
                let pid = parent_id.ok_or_else(|| {
                    Status::invalid_argument("parent_id required for file inodes")
                })?;
                Inode::new_file(volume_id, pid, r.name)
            }
            _ => Inode::new_directory(volume_id, parent_id, r.name),
        };

        let created = self.repo.create(inode).await.map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(CreateInodeResponse { inode: Some(inode_to_proto(created)) }))
    }

    async fn get_inode(
        &self,
        req: Request<GetInodeRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<GetInodeResponse>, Status> {
        let inode_id = parse_uuid(&req.into_inner().inode_id, "inode_id")?;
        let inode    = self.repo.find_by_id(inode_id).await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(GetInodeResponse { inode: Some(inode_to_proto(inode)) }))
    }

    async fn list_directory(
        &self,
        req: Request<ListDirectoryRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<ListDirectoryResponse>, Status> {
        let r         = req.into_inner();
        let volume_id = parse_uuid(&r.volume_id, "volume_id")?;
        let parent_id = parse_opt_uuid(&r.parent_id);
        let after_id  = parse_opt_uuid(&r.page_token);
        let page_size = if r.page_size > 0 {
            r.page_size as i64
        } else {
            self.default_page
        };

        let inodes = self.repo
            .list_children(volume_id, parent_id, page_size, after_id)
            .await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;

        let next_token = if inodes.len() as i64 == page_size {
            inodes.last().map(|i| i.id.to_string()).unwrap_or_default()
        } else {
            String::new()
        };

        Ok(Response::new(ListDirectoryResponse {
            inodes:     inodes.into_iter().map(inode_to_proto).collect(),
            next_token,
        }))
    }

    async fn get_file_tree(
        &self,
        req: Request<GetFileTreeRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<GetFileTreeResponse>, Status> {
        let r         = req.into_inner();
        let volume_id = parse_uuid(&r.volume_id, "volume_id")?;
        let root_id   = parse_opt_uuid(&r.root_id);
        let max_depth = if r.max_depth > 0 {
            r.max_depth.min(self.max_depth)
        } else {
            3
        };

        let inodes = self.repo
            .get_tree(volume_id, root_id, max_depth)
            .await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;

        // Build root node (the first inode is the root by query ordering)
        let root_inode = inodes.first()
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| Status::not_found("empty volume or root not found"))?;

        let all_except_root = inodes.into_iter().skip(1).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let children        = Self::build_tree(all_except_root, Some(root_inode.id));

        let root = TreeNode {
            inode:    Some(inode_to_proto(root_inode)),
            children,
        };

        Ok(Response::new(GetFileTreeResponse { root: Some(root) }))
    }

    async fn move_inode(
        &self,
        req: Request<MoveInodeRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<MoveInodeResponse>, Status> {
        let r             = req.into_inner();
        let inode_id      = parse_uuid(&r.inode_id, "inode_id")?;
        let new_parent_id = parse_uuid(&r.new_parent_id, "new_parent_id")?;
        let new_name      = if r.new_name.is_empty() { None } else { Some(r.new_name) };

        let inode = self.repo.move_inode(inode_id, new_parent_id, new_name)
            .await.map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(MoveInodeResponse { inode: Some(inode_to_proto(inode)) }))
    }

    async fn rename_inode(
        &self,
        req: Request<RenameInodeRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<RenameInodeResponse>, Status> {
        let r        = req.into_inner();
        let inode_id = parse_uuid(&r.inode_id, "inode_id")?;
        let inode    = self.repo.rename(inode_id, r.new_name)
            .await.map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(RenameInodeResponse { inode: Some(inode_to_proto(inode)) }))
    }

    async fn delete_inode(
        &self,
        req: Request<DeleteInodeRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<DeleteInodeResponse>, Status> {
        let r        = req.into_inner();
        let inode_id = parse_uuid(&r.inode_id, "inode_id")?;
        let count    = self.repo.soft_delete(inode_id, r.recursive)
            .await.map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(DeleteInodeResponse { ok: true, deleted_count: count }))
    }
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/interface/grpc/version_service.rs`

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use tonic::{Request, Response, Status};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::domain::entities::InodeVersion;
use crate::domain::repositories::{InodeRepository, VersionRepository};
use crate::error::MetadataError;
use super::converters::*;

pub use version_service_server::{VersionService, VersionServiceServer};

pub struct VersionServiceImpl {
    version_repo: Arc<dyn VersionRepository>,
    inode_repo:   Arc<dyn InodeRepository>,
}

impl VersionServiceImpl {
    pub fn new(
        version_repo: Arc<dyn VersionRepository>,
        inode_repo:   Arc<dyn InodeRepository>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self { version_repo, inode_repo }
    }
}

#[tonic::async_trait]
impl VersionService for VersionServiceImpl {
    async fn commit_version(
        &self,
        req: Request<CommitVersionRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<CommitVersionResponse>, Status> {
        let r        = req.into_inner();
        let inode_id = parse_uuid(&r.inode_id, "inode_id")?;

        // Verify inode exists
        self.inode_repo.find_by_id(inode_id).await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;

        let version_number = self.version_repo
            .next_version_number(inode_id)
            .await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;

        let version = InodeVersion {
            id:             Uuid::new_v4(),
            inode_id,
            version_number,
            total_chunks:   r.total_chunks,
            encrypted_dek:  r.encrypted_dek,
            dek_nonce:      r.dek_nonce,
            created_at:     chrono::Utc::now(),
        };

        let created = self.version_repo.create(version).await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;

        // Update inode's current_version_id, size, and content_hash atomically
        self.inode_repo.set_current_version(
            inode_id,
            created.id,
            r.size_bytes,
            if r.content_hash.is_empty() { None } else { Some(r.content_hash) },
        ).await.map_err(MetadataError::into)?;

        Ok(Response::new(CommitVersionResponse {
            version:        Some(version_to_proto(created)),
            version_number,
        }))
    }

    async fn get_current_version(
        &self,
        req: Request<GetCurrentVersionRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<GetCurrentVersionResponse>, Status> {
        let inode_id = parse_uuid(&req.into_inner().inode_id, "inode_id")?;
        let version  = self.version_repo.find_current(inode_id).await
            .map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(GetCurrentVersionResponse {
            version: Some(version_to_proto(version)),
        }))
    }

    async fn list_versions(
        &self,
        req: Request<ListVersionsRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<ListVersionsResponse>, Status> {
        let r         = req.into_inner();
        let inode_id  = parse_uuid(&r.inode_id, "inode_id")?;
        let page_size = if r.page_size > 0 { r.page_size } else { 20 };
        let versions  = self.version_repo.list_by_inode(inode_id, page_size)
            .await.map_err(MetadataError::into)?;
        Ok(Response::new(ListVersionsResponse {
            versions: versions.into_iter().map(version_to_proto).collect(),
        }))
    }
}
```

#### `services/metadata-service/src/main.rs`

```rust
mod config;
mod error;
mod domain;
mod infrastructure;
mod interface;

use std::sync::Arc;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use sqlx::PgPool;
use tonic::transport::Server;
use tracing_subscriber::{layer::SubscriberExt, util::SubscriberInitExt, EnvFilter};

use infrastructure::db::{
    inode_repo::PgInodeRepository,
    version_repo::PgVersionRepository,
    volume_repo::PgVolumeRepository,
};
use interface::grpc::{
    inode_service::{InodeServiceImpl, InodeServiceServer},
    version_service::{VersionServiceImpl, VersionServiceServer},
    volume_service::{VolumeServiceImpl, VolumeServiceServer},
};

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    tracing_subscriber::registry()
        .with(EnvFilter::try_from_default_env()
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "metadata_service=debug,sqlx=warn".into()))
        .with(tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer().json())
        .init();

    let cfg  = config::AppConfig::load()?;
    let pool = PgPool::connect(&cfg.database_url).await?;
    tracing::info!("metadata-service: DB connected, starting on :{}", cfg.grpc_port);

    // Run pending migrations on startup (safe for single-instance dev;
    // use a separate migration job in production)
    sqlx::migrate!("../metadata-service/migrations")
        .run(&pool)
        .await?;

    // Repositories
    let volume_repo:  Arc<dyn domain::repositories::VolumeRepository>  =
        Arc::new(PgVolumeRepository  { pool: pool.clone() });
    let inode_repo:   Arc<dyn domain::repositories::InodeRepository>   =
        Arc::new(PgInodeRepository   { pool: pool.clone() });
    let version_repo: Arc<dyn domain::repositories::VersionRepository> =
        Arc::new(PgVersionRepository { pool: pool.clone() });

    // gRPC services
    let volume_svc  = VolumeServiceServer::new(VolumeServiceImpl::new(Arc::clone(&volume_repo)));
    let inode_svc   = InodeServiceServer::new(InodeServiceImpl::new(
        Arc::clone(&inode_repo), cfg.default_page_size, cfg.max_tree_depth,
    ));
    let version_svc = VersionServiceServer::new(VersionServiceImpl::new(
        Arc::clone(&version_repo), Arc::clone(&inode_repo),
    ));

    let (health_reporter, health_svc) = tonic_health::server::health_reporter();
    health_reporter.set_serving::<VolumeServiceServer<VolumeServiceImpl>>().await;
    health_reporter.set_serving::<InodeServiceServer<InodeServiceImpl>>().await;

    let addr: SocketAddr = format!("{}:{}", cfg.host, cfg.grpc_port).parse()?;
    tracing::info!(%addr, "gRPC server listening");

    Server::builder()
        .add_service(health_svc)
        .add_service(volume_svc)
        .add_service(inode_svc)
        .add_service(version_svc)
        .serve_with_shutdown(addr, async {
            tokio::signal::ctrl_c().await.ok();
            tracing::info!("metadata-service: graceful shutdown");
        })
        .await?;

    Ok(())
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/lib/session.ts`

```typescript
// Server-side session validation for Next.js Route Handlers and RSC.
// Reads the __aether_session cookie, verifies the RS256 JWT,
// and returns the decoded session or null.

import { jwtVerify, importSPKI, type KeyLike } from 'jose';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';

export interface AetherSession {
  userId: string;
  email:  string;
  iat:    number;
  exp:    number;
}

let _publicKey: KeyLike | null = null;

async function getPublicKey(): Promise<KeyLike> {
  if (!_publicKey) {
    const pem    = Buffer.from(process.env['JWT_PUBLIC_KEY_BASE64']!, 'base64').toString();
    _publicKey   = await importSPKI(pem, 'RS256');
  }
  return _publicKey;
}

export async function getSession(): Promise<AetherSession | null> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token       = cookieStore.get('__aether_session')?.value;
  if (!token) return null;

  try {
    const key = await getPublicKey();
    const { payload } = await jwtVerify(token, key, {
      issuer:   process.env['JWT_ISSUER']!,
      audience: 'aether:api',
    });
    return payload as unknown as AetherSession;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function requireSession(): Promise<AetherSession> {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) {
    const { redirect } = await import('next/navigation');
    redirect('/login');
  }
  return session;
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/lib/api.ts`

```typescript
// Typed API client for all backend services.
// Used in API routes (server-side) and RSC data fetching.

const AUTH_URL   = process.env['AUTH_SERVICE_URL']     ?? 'http://localhost:3001';
const META_URL   = process.env['METADATA_SERVICE_URL'] ?? 'http://localhost:3004';

// ── REST bridge for metadata-service ────────────────────────────────────────
// The metadata-service speaks gRPC. We expose a thin REST/JSON proxy via
// Next.js API routes so the browser and RSC can call it over HTTP.
// The proxy route at /api/meta/* forwards to this URL.

export interface Volume {
  id:               string;
  userId:           string;
  name:             string;
  totalLogicalBytes: number;
  encryptionConfig:  Record<string, unknown>;
  createdAt:         string;
  updatedAt:         string;
}

export interface Inode {
  id:               string;
  volumeId:         string;
  parentId:         string;
  name:             string;
  inodeType:        'FILE' | 'DIRECTORY' | 'SYMLINK';
  sizeBytes:        number;
  currentVersionId: string;
  isDeleted:        boolean;
  syncState:        string;
  createdAt:        string;
  modifiedAt:       string;
}

export interface TreeNode {
  inode:    Inode;
  children: TreeNode[];
}

export interface ConnectedProvider {
  providerId:       string;
  connectedAt:      string;
  storageQuotaBytes: number | null;
  storageUsedBytes:  number | null;
}

// ── Fetch helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function apiFetch<T>(
  url:     string,
  options: RequestInit = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    ...options,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...options.headers,
    },
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const body = await res.text().catch(() => '');
    throw new Error(`API error ${res.status}: ${body}`);
  }

  return res.json() as Promise<T>;
}

// ── Volume API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const volumeApi = {
  list: (token: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ volumes: Volume[] }>(`${META_URL}/volumes`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    }),

  create: (token: string, name: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ volume: Volume }>(`${META_URL}/volumes`, {
      method:  'POST',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      body:    JSON.stringify({ name }),
    }),

  delete: (token: string, id: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ ok: boolean }>(`${META_URL}/volumes/${id}`, {
      method:  'DELETE',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    }),
};

// ── Inode API ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const inodeApi = {
  tree: (token: string, volumeId: string, depth = 3) =>
    apiFetch<{ root: TreeNode }>(
      `${META_URL}/volumes/${volumeId}/tree?depth=${depth}`,
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
    ),

  listDir: (token: string, volumeId: string, parentId?: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ inodes: Inode[]; nextToken: string }>(
      `${META_URL}/volumes/${volumeId}/inodes?parentId=${parentId ?? ''}`,
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
    ),

  create: (token: string, volumeId: string, body: {
    name: string; parentId: string; inodeType: 'FILE' | 'DIRECTORY';
  }) =>
    apiFetch<{ inode: Inode }>(`${META_URL}/volumes/${volumeId}/inodes`, {
      method:  'POST',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      body:    JSON.stringify(body),
    }),

  rename: (token: string, inodeId: string, newName: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ inode: Inode }>(`${META_URL}/inodes/${inodeId}/rename`, {
      method:  'PATCH',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      body:    JSON.stringify({ newName }),
    }),

  move: (token: string, inodeId: string, newParentId: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ inode: Inode }>(`${META_URL}/inodes/${inodeId}/move`, {
      method:  'PATCH',
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      body:    JSON.stringify({ newParentId }),
    }),

  delete: (token: string, inodeId: string, recursive = false) =>
    apiFetch<{ ok: boolean; deletedCount: number }>(
      `${META_URL}/inodes/${inodeId}?recursive=${recursive}`,
      { method: 'DELETE', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
    ),
};

// ── Auth API ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export const authApi = {
  providers: (token: string) =>
    apiFetch<{ providers: ConnectedProvider[] }>(`${AUTH_URL}/auth/providers`, {
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    }),
};
```

#### `apps/web/src/middleware.ts`

```typescript
// Edge middleware: validate session cookie on all protected routes.
// Redirects to /login if the JWT is absent or invalid.

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { jwtVerify, importSPKI } from 'jose';

const PUBLIC_ROUTES = new Set(['/login', '/api/auth', '/api/health']);

function isPublic(pathname: string): boolean {
  return [...PUBLIC_ROUTES].some((p) => pathname.startsWith(p));
}

export async function middleware(req: NextRequest): Promise<NextResponse> {
  const { pathname } = req.nextUrl;

  if (isPublic(pathname) || pathname.startsWith('/_next')) {
    return NextResponse.next();
  }

  const token = req.cookies.get('__aether_session')?.value;
  if (!token) {
    const url = req.nextUrl.clone();
    url.pathname = '/login';
    url.searchParams.set('redirectAfter', pathname);
    return NextResponse.redirect(url);
  }

  try {
    const pkBase64 = process.env['JWT_PUBLIC_KEY_BASE64']!;
    const pem      = Buffer.from(pkBase64, 'base64').toString();
    const key      = await importSPKI(pem, 'RS256');

    await jwtVerify(token, key, {
      issuer:   process.env['JWT_ISSUER']!,
      audience: 'aether:api',
    });

    return NextResponse.next();
  } catch {
    const url      = req.nextUrl.clone();
    url.pathname   = '/login';
    const response = NextResponse.redirect(url);
    response.cookies.delete('__aether_session');
    return response;
  }
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ['/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico).*)'],
};
```

#### `apps/web/src/app/volumes/[volumeId]/page.tsx`

```tsx
import { requireSession } from '@/lib/session';
import { inodeApi }       from '@/lib/api';
import { FileTree }       from '@/components/files/FileTree';
import { BreadcrumbBar }  from '@/components/files/BreadcrumbBar';
import { UploadDropzone } from '@/components/files/UploadDropzone';
import { cookies }        from 'next/headers';
import type { Inode }     from '@/lib/api';

interface Props {
  params:      { volumeId: string };
  searchParams: { parentId?: string };
}

async function getToken(): Promise<string> {
  const store = await cookies();
  return store.get('__aether_session')?.value ?? '';
}

export default async function VolumePage({ params, searchParams }: Props) {
  await requireSession();
  const token    = await getToken();
  const parentId = searchParams.parentId;

  const [treeData, dirData] = await Promise.all([
    inodeApi.tree(token, params.volumeId, 2),
    inodeApi.listDir(token, params.volumeId, parentId),
  ]);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full">
      {/* Sidebar tree navigator */}
      <aside className="w-64 border-r border-neutral-800 overflow-y-auto p-4">
        <FileTree
          root={treeData.root}
          volumeId={params.volumeId}
          activeId={parentId}
        />
      </aside>

      {/* Main file browser pane */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <BreadcrumbBar
          volumeId={params.volumeId}
          parentId={parentId}
        />

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-4">
          <UploadDropzone
            volumeId={params.volumeId}
            parentId={parentId ?? ''}
          />

          <FileList
            inodes={dirData.inodes}
            volumeId={params.volumeId}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function FileList({
  inodes,
  volumeId,
}: {
  inodes:   Inode[];
  volumeId: string;
}) {
  if (inodes.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-neutral-500">
        <p className="text-sm">Drop files here or click the upload area to get started.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
      <table className="w-full text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/50">
            <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-neutral-400">Name</th>
            <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium text-neutral-400">Size</th>
            <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium text-neutral-400">Modified</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3" />
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {inodes.map((inode) => (
            <FileRow key={inode.id} inode={inode} volumeId={volumeId} />
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

function FileRow({ inode, volumeId }: { inode: Inode; volumeId: string }) {
  const isDir = inode.inodeType === 'DIRECTORY';
  const href  = isDir
    ? `/volumes/${volumeId}?parentId=${inode.id}`
    : `/volumes/${volumeId}/files/${inode.id}`;

  return (
    <tr className="border-b border-neutral-800/50 hover:bg-neutral-900/30 transition-colors">
      <td className="px-4 py-3">
        <a href={href} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-white hover:text-violet-400">
          <span className="text-base">{isDir ? '📁' : '📄'}</span>
          <span className="font-medium">{inode.name}</span>
        </a>
      </td>
      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right text-neutral-400">
        {isDir ? '—' : formatBytes(inode.sizeBytes)}
      </td>
      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right text-neutral-400">
        {new Date(inode.modifiedAt).toLocaleDateString()}
      </td>
      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
        <FileActions inodeId={inode.id} isDir={isDir} />
      </td>
    </tr>
  );
}

function FileActions({ inodeId, isDir }: { inodeId: string; isDir: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
      <form action={`/api/inodes/${inodeId}`} method="POST">
        <input type="hidden" name="_method" value="DELETE" />
        <input type="hidden" name="recursive" value={isDir ? 'true' : 'false'} />
        <button
          type="submit"
          className="rounded p-1.5 text-neutral-500 hover:text-red-400
                     hover:bg-red-400/10 transition-colors"
          title="Delete"
        >
          <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.5} viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"
              d="M14.74 9l-.346 9m-4.788 0L9.26 9m9.968-3.21c.342.052.682.107
                 1.022.166m-1.022-.165L18.16 19.673a2.25 2.25 0 01-2.244 2.077H8.084a2.25
                 2.25 0 01-2.244-2.077L4.772 5.79m14.456 0a48.108 48.108 0 00-3.478-.397m-12
                 .562c.34-.059.68-.114 1.022-.165m0 0a48.11 48.11 0 013.478-.397m7.5 0v-.916c0-1.18-.91-2.164-2.09-2.201a51.964
                 51.964 0 00-3.32 0c-1.18.037-2.09 1.022-2.09 2.201v.916m7.5 0a48.667
                 48.667 0 00-7.5 0" />
          </svg>
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
  const k    = 1024;
  const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
  const i    = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return `${(bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(1)} ${sizes[i]}`;
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/components/files/FileTree.tsx`

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import Link          from 'next/link';
import { clsx }      from 'clsx';
import type { TreeNode } from '@/lib/api';

interface FileTreeProps {
  root:     TreeNode;
  volumeId: string;
  activeId: string | undefined;
}

export function FileTree({ root, volumeId, activeId }: FileTreeProps) {
  return (
    <nav className="space-y-0.5">
      <TreeNodeRow
        node={root}
        volumeId={volumeId}
        activeId={activeId}
        depth={0}
      />
    </nav>
  );
}

function TreeNodeRow({
  node,
  volumeId,
  activeId,
  depth,
}: {
  node:     TreeNode;
  volumeId: string;
  activeId: string | undefined;
  depth:    number;
}) {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(depth < 1);
  const isDir                   = node.inode.inodeType === 'DIRECTORY';
  const isActive                = node.inode.id === activeId;
  const hasChildren             = node.children.length > 0;
  const href                    = isDir
    ? `/volumes/${volumeId}?parentId=${node.inode.id}`
    : `/volumes/${volumeId}/files/${node.inode.id}`;

  return (
    <div>
      <div
        className={clsx(
          'flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-sm',
          'hover:bg-neutral-800/60 transition-colors cursor-pointer',
          isActive && 'bg-violet-600/20 text-violet-300',
          !isActive && 'text-neutral-300',
        )}
        style={{ paddingLeft: `${depth * 12 + 8}px` }}
      >
        {/* Expand/collapse toggle */}
        {isDir && hasChildren ? (
          <button
            onClick={() => setExpanded((e) => !e)}
            className="shrink-0 text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-300"
          >
            <svg
              className={clsx('w-3 h-3 transition-transform', expanded && 'rotate-90')}
              fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20"
            >
              <path fillRule="evenodd"
                d="M7.293 14.707a1 1 0 010-1.414L10.586 10 7.293 6.707a1 1 0
                   011.414-1.414l4 4a1 1 0 010 1.414l-4 4a1 1 0 01-1.414 0z"
                clipRule="evenodd" />
            </svg>
          </button>
        ) : (
          <span className="w-3 shrink-0" />
        )}

        <Link href={href} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 min-w-0 truncate">
          <span className="text-xs">{isDir ? '📁' : '📄'}</span>
          <span className="truncate">{node.inode.name}</span>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* Children */}
      {isDir && expanded && node.children.length > 0 && (
        <div>
          {node.children.map((child) => (
            <TreeNodeRow
              key={child.inode.id}
              node={child}
              volumeId={volumeId}
              activeId={activeId}
              depth={depth + 1}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/components/files/UploadDropzone.tsx`

```tsx
'use client';

/**
 * UploadDropzone
 *
 * Client-side upload component. Handles the full ZK pipeline:
 *   1. User drops file(s)
 *   2. Read file bytes in browser via FileReader
 *   3. Load crypto-core WASM → derive DEK → AES-256-GCM encrypt per chunk
 *   4. RS(10,4) erasure code each encrypted chunk → 14 shards
 *   5. POST shard manifest + wrapped DEK to /api/upload (server creates inode + version)
 *   6. Server responds with uploadTargets: [{shardIndex, presignedUrl}]
 *   7. Client uploads each shard directly to presigned URL (provider-specific)
 *   8. Client calls /api/upload/commit to finalize
 *
 * NOTE: WASM crypto-core package is lazy-loaded only in the browser.
 * The full pipeline is stubbed here in Phase 5; WASM integration
 * is the Phase 6 deliverable.
 */

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { useDropzone }            from 'react-dropzone';
import { clsx }                   from 'clsx';

interface UploadDropzoneProps {
  volumeId: string;
  parentId: string;
}

type UploadStatus = 'idle' | 'reading' | 'encrypting' | 'uploading' | 'done' | 'error';

interface UploadFile {
  file:     File;
  status:   UploadStatus;
  progress: number;
  error?:   string;
}

export function UploadDropzone({ volumeId, parentId }: UploadDropzoneProps) {
  const [uploads, setUploads] = useState<UploadFile[]>([]);

  const updateFile = (index: number, patch: Partial<UploadFile>) => {
    setUploads((prev) => prev.map((u, i) => i === index ? { ...u, ...patch } : u));
  };

  const processUpload = useCallback(async (file: File, index: number) => {
    try {
      updateFile(index, { status: 'reading', progress: 5 });

      // Read file bytes
      const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
      updateFile(index, { status: 'encrypting', progress: 15 });

      // --- WASM crypto pipeline (stubbed for Phase 5) ---
      // In Phase 6 this becomes:
      //   const { aether_init, encrypt_and_shard } = await import('@aether/crypto-core/pkg-web');
      //   await aether_init();
      //   const { shards, encryptedDek, dekNonce } = encrypt_and_shard(
      //     new Uint8Array(arrayBuffer), derivedDek
      //   );
      //
      // For now, we pass raw bytes as a single "shard" to prove the API round-trip.
      const mockShards = [new Uint8Array(arrayBuffer)];
      const mockDek    = new Uint8Array(32); // placeholder
      const mockNonce  = new Uint8Array(12);
      // --------------------------------------------------

      updateFile(index, { status: 'uploading', progress: 30 });

      // Step 1: Create inode + version, get upload targets
      const initRes = await fetch('/api/upload/init', {
        method:  'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body:    JSON.stringify({
          volumeId,
          parentId,
          filename:    file.name,
          sizeBytes:   file.size,
          totalChunks: mockShards.length,
          encryptedDek: Array.from(mockDek),
          dekNonce:     Array.from(mockNonce),
        }),
      });

      if (!initRes.ok) throw new Error(`Upload init failed: ${initRes.status}`);
      const { inodeId, versionId } = await initRes.json() as {
        inodeId: string; versionId: string;
      };

      updateFile(index, { progress: 60 });

      // Step 2: In Phase 6 — upload shards to providers via presigned URLs.
      // For Phase 5 this is a no-op placeholder.

      // Step 3: Commit version
      const commitRes = await fetch('/api/upload/commit', {
        method:  'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body:    JSON.stringify({ inodeId, versionId }),
      });

      if (!commitRes.ok) throw new Error(`Commit failed: ${commitRes.status}`);

      updateFile(index, { status: 'done', progress: 100 });

    } catch (err) {
      updateFile(index, {
        status: 'error',
        error:  err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Upload failed',
      });
    }
  }, [volumeId, parentId]);

  const onDrop = useCallback((acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    const startIndex = uploads.length;
    const newUploads: UploadFile[] = acceptedFiles.map((file) => ({
      file, status: 'idle', progress: 0,
    }));
    setUploads((prev) => [...prev, ...newUploads]);
    acceptedFiles.forEach((_, i) => processUpload(acceptedFiles[i]!, startIndex + i));
  }, [uploads.length, processUpload]);

  const { getRootProps, getInputProps, isDragActive } = useDropzone({
    onDrop,
    multiple: true,
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <div
        {...getRootProps()}
        className={clsx(
          'border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-8 text-center cursor-pointer transition-colors',
          isDragActive
            ? 'border-violet-500 bg-violet-500/10'
            : 'border-neutral-700 hover:border-neutral-500 hover:bg-neutral-900/30',
        )}
      >
        <input {...getInputProps()} />
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <div className="text-3xl">🔐</div>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-300">
            {isDragActive ? 'Drop to upload with zero-knowledge encryption' : 'Drop files here'}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
            Files are encrypted client-side before leaving your browser.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Upload progress list */}
      {uploads.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="space-y-2">
          {uploads.map((u, i) => (
            <li
              key={i}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-neutral-800
                         bg-neutral-900 px-4 py-3"
            >
              <span className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-300 flex-1 truncate">
                {u.file.name}
              </span>
              {u.status === 'error' ? (
                <span className="text-xs text-red-400">{u.error}</span>
              ) : u.status === 'done' ? (
                <span className="text-xs text-emerald-400 font-medium">✓ Encrypted & uploaded</span>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-24 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-neutral-700 overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full bg-violet-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                      style={{ width: `${u.progress}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400 w-12 text-right">
                    {statusLabel(u.status)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function statusLabel(status: UploadStatus): string {
  switch (status) {
    case 'reading':    return 'Reading…';
    case 'encrypting': return 'Encrypting…';
    case 'uploading':  return 'Uploading…';
    default:           return '';
  }
}
```

```tsx
              {formatBytes(volume.totalLogicalBytes)} encrypted
            </p>

                             bg-emerald-900/30 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-emerald-400">
              <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400" />
              AES-256-GCM
            </span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full
                             bg-neutral-800 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-neutral-400">
              RS(10,4)
            </span>
          </div>
        </Link>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
  const k     = 1024;
  const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
  const i     = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return `${(bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(1)} ${sizes[i]}`;
}
```

```tsx
import type { ConnectedProvider } from '@/lib/api';

const PROVIDER_META: Record<string, { name: string; icon: string; color: string }> = {
  google_drive: { name: 'Google Drive', icon: '🟢', color: 'text-green-400' },
  onedrive:     { name: 'OneDrive',     icon: '🔵', color: 'text-blue-400'  },
  dropbox:      { name: 'Dropbox',      icon: '🟦', color: 'text-sky-400'   },
  mega:         { name: 'MEGA',         icon: '🔴', color: 'text-red-400'   },
};

const ALL_PROVIDERS = ['google_drive', 'onedrive', 'dropbox', 'mega'];

export function ProviderStatus({ providers }: { providers: ConnectedProvider[] }) {
  const connectedIds = new Set(providers.map((p) => p.providerId));

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
      {ALL_PROVIDERS.map((pid) => {
        const meta      = PROVIDER_META[pid]!;
        const connected = connectedIds.has(pid);
        const prov      = providers.find((p) => p.providerId === pid);

        return (
          <div
            key={pid}
            className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900 p-4 space-y-3"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-300">{meta.name}</span>
              <span className={connected ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-neutral-600'}>
                {connected ? '●' : '○'}
              </span>
            </div>

            {connected && prov ? (
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">Used</span>
                  <span className="text-neutral-300">
                    {formatBytes(prov.storageUsedBytes ?? 0)}
                    {prov.storageQuotaBytes
                      ? ` / ${formatBytes(prov.storageQuotaBytes)}`
                      : ''}
                  </span>
                </div>
                {prov.storageQuotaBytes && prov.storageUsedBytes != null && (
                  <div className="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full bg-violet-500 rounded-full"
                      style={{
                        width: `${Math.min(
                          100,
                          (prov.storageUsedBytes / prov.storageQuotaBytes) * 100,
                        ).toFixed(1)}%`,
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>
                )}
                <form action={`/api/auth/disconnect/${pid}`} method="POST">
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    className="text-xs text-neutral-600 hover:text-red-400
                               transition-colors mt-1"
                  >
                    Disconnect
                  </button>
                </form>
              </div>
            ) : (
              
                href={`/api/auth/connect/${pid}`}
                className="block w-full text-center rounded-lg border border-neutral-700
                           px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-400
                           hover:border-violet-600 hover:text-violet-400 transition-colors"
              >
                Connect
              </a>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
  if (!bytes) return '0 B';
  const k     = 1024;
  const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
  const i     = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return `${(bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(1)} ${sizes[i]}`;
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/app/api/upload/init/route.ts`

```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getSession }                 from '@/lib/session';
import { inodeApi }                   from '@/lib/api';
import { cookies }                    from 'next/headers';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const store = await cookies();
  const token = store.get('__aether_session')?.value ?? '';

  const body = await req.json() as {
    volumeId:    string;
    parentId:    string;
    filename:    string;
    sizeBytes:   number;
    totalChunks: number;
    encryptedDek: number[];
    dekNonce:     number[];
  };

  // 1. Create file inode
  const { inode } = await inodeApi.create(token, body.volumeId, {
    name:      body.filename,
    parentId:  body.parentId,
    inodeType: 'FILE',
  });

  // 2. Commit a version record (metadata-service)
  const versionRes = await fetch(
    `${process.env['METADATA_SERVICE_URL']}/inodes/${inode.id}/versions`,
    {
      method:  'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type':  'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        totalChunks:  body.totalChunks,
        encryptedDek: body.encryptedDek,
        dekNonce:     body.dekNonce,
        sizeBytes:    body.sizeBytes,
        contentHash:  [],
      }),
    },
  );

  if (!versionRes.ok) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Failed to create version' },
      { status: 502 },
    );
  }

  const { version } = await versionRes.json() as { version: { id: string } };

  return NextResponse.json({ inodeId: inode.id, versionId: version.id });
}
```

```typescript
}
```

**Phase 6** will be: WASM crypto-core integration into the browser upload pipeline — replacing the Phase 5 stubs with the real Argon2id key derivation, AES-256-GCM per-shard encryption, and RS(10,4) client-side sharding, completing the zero-knowledge upload path end-to-end.
