## Project Aether — Phase 7: Remaining Providers, REST Bridge & Production Infrastructure

This phase closes the remaining open surfaces: OneDrive/Dropbox/MEGA adapters completing the multi-provider story, the Axum REST bridge making the chunk-orchestrator reachable from Next.js API routes, full OpenTelemetry instrumentation wired through every service, and the Terraform/EKS production deployment.

### Architecture Additions

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  New in Phase 7                                              │
│                                                              │
│  storage-adapters/                                           │
│    OneDriveAdapter   ─┐                                      │
│    DropboxAdapter    ─┼─→ ProviderRegistry (singleton)       │
│    MegaAdapter       ─┘      used by chunk-orchestrator      │
│                              + delta-sync-service            │
│                                                              │
│  chunk-orchestrator/                                         │
│    src/interface/http/         ← NEW: Axum REST bridge       │
│      RestServer                  POST /upload/sessions       │
│      routes/upload.rs            POST /upload/commit         │
│      routes/manifest.rs          GET  /manifest/:version_id  │
│      routes/shard.rs             GET  /shard                 │
│      routes/health.rs            GET  /health                │
│                                                              │
│  infra/terraform/              ← Full EKS + supporting stack │
│  infra/k8s/                    ← Kustomize overlays          │
│  services/*/                   ← OTel instrumentation        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Part 1 · Remaining Storage Adapters (TypeScript)

#### `packages/storage-adapters/src/providers/OneDriveAdapter.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * OneDriveAdapter
 *
 * Microsoft Graph API v1.0.
 * Key constraints:
 *   - Upload sessions: POST /drive/root:/path:/createUploadSession → session URL
 *   - Large files (> 4 MB): PUT to session URL with Content-Range
 *   - Delta sync: GET /me/drive/root/delta?token=... (deltaLink cursor)
 *   - Quota: GET /me/drive (quota.used / quota.total)
 *   - Rate limit: 2 req/s per user (conservative; actual is burst-based)
 *   - Retry-After header present on 429 responses
 */

import axios, { type AxiosInstance, type AxiosError } from 'axios';
import {
  type IStorageProvider,
  type UploadRequest,
  type UploadResult,
  type DownloadRequest,
  type DownloadResult,
  type DeleteRequest,
  type ProviderQuota,
  type DeltaCursor,
  type DeltaPollResult,
  type EnsureFolderResult,
  ProviderError,
} from '../interfaces/IStorageProvider.js';

const GRAPH_BASE = 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/me/drive';
const FOLDER_NAME = 'Aether Secure Storage';
const SMALL_FILE_THRESHOLD = 4 * 1024 * 1024; // 4 MB

export interface OneDriveAdapterOptions {
  getAccessToken: () => Promise<string>;
}

interface GraphDriveItem {
  id:                   string;
  name:                 string;
  size:                 number;
  eTag?:                string;
  lastModifiedDateTime: string;
  '@microsoft.graph.downloadUrl'?: string;
}

interface GraphDelta {
  value:                    GraphDriveItem[];
  '@odata.nextLink'?:       string;
  '@odata.deltaLink'?:      string;
}

export class OneDriveAdapter implements IStorageProvider {
  readonly providerId = 'onedrive' as const;

  private readonly http: AxiosInstance;
  private _cachedFolderId: string | null = null;
  private _cachedFolderPath: string | null = null;

  constructor(private readonly opts: OneDriveAdapterOptions) {
    this.http = axios.create({ baseURL: GRAPH_BASE, timeout: 60_000 });

    this.http.interceptors.request.use(async (cfg) => {
      const token = await this.opts.getAccessToken();
      cfg.headers = cfg.headers ?? {};
      cfg.headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
      return cfg;
    });

    this.http.interceptors.response.use(undefined, (err: AxiosError) => {
      throw this.translateError(err);
    });
  }

  // ── Upload ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async upload(req: UploadRequest): Promise<UploadResult> {
    const folder = await this.ensureAetherFolder();

    if (req.data.length <= SMALL_FILE_THRESHOLD) {
      return this.simpleUpload(req, folder.folderId);
    }
    return this.resumableUpload(req, folder.folderId);
  }

  private async simpleUpload(
    req:      UploadRequest,
    folderId: string,
  ): Promise<UploadResult> {
    const resp = await this.http.put<GraphDriveItem>(
      `/items/${folderId}:/${encodeURIComponent(req.filename)}:/content`,
      Buffer.from(req.data),
      { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream' } },
    );
    return this.toUploadResult(resp.data);
  }

  private async resumableUpload(
    req:      UploadRequest,
    folderId: string,
  ): Promise<UploadResult> {
    // Step 1: Create upload session
    const sessionResp = await this.http.post<{ uploadUrl: string }>(
      `/items/${folderId}:/${encodeURIComponent(req.filename)}:/createUploadSession`,
      {
        item: {
          '@microsoft.graph.conflictBehavior': 'replace',
          name: req.filename,
        },
      },
    );
    const { uploadUrl } = sessionResp.data;

    // Step 2: Upload in one PUT (for shards ≤ 64 MiB, single PUT is fine)
    const data     = Buffer.from(req.data);
    const dataLen  = data.length;

    const uploadResp = await axios.put<GraphDriveItem>(
      uploadUrl,
      data,
      {
        headers: {
          'Content-Type':   'application/octet-stream',
          'Content-Length': String(dataLen),
          'Content-Range':  `bytes 0-${dataLen - 1}/${dataLen}`,
        },
        timeout: 120_000,
      },
    );

    return this.toUploadResult(uploadResp.data);
  }

  private toUploadResult(item: GraphDriveItem): UploadResult {
    return {
      objectId:   item.id,
      etag:       item.eTag ?? null,
      sizeBytes:  item.size,
      uploadedAt: item.lastModifiedDateTime,
    };
  }

  // ── Download ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async download(req: DownloadRequest): Promise<DownloadResult> {
    // Get download URL first (Graph requires an extra hop for content)
    const meta = await this.http.get<GraphDriveItem>(
      `/items/${req.objectId}`,
      { params: { select: 'id,@microsoft.graph.downloadUrl,eTag,size' } },
    );

    const downloadUrl = meta.data['@microsoft.graph.downloadUrl'];
    if (!downloadUrl) throw new ProviderError('NOT_FOUND', `No download URL for ${req.objectId}`);

    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (req.etag) headers['If-None-Match'] = req.etag;

    const resp = await axios.get<ArrayBuffer>(downloadUrl, {
      responseType:   'arraybuffer',
      headers,
      validateStatus: (s) => s === 200 || s === 304,
      timeout:        60_000,
    });

    if (resp.status === 304) {
      return { data: Buffer.alloc(0), etag: req.etag ?? null, sizeBytes: 0, notModified: true };
    }

    const data = Buffer.from(resp.data);
    return {
      data,
      etag:        (resp.headers['etag'] as string | undefined) ?? meta.data.eTag ?? null,
      sizeBytes:   data.length,
      notModified: false,
    };
  }

  // ── Delete ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async delete(req: DeleteRequest): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.http.delete(`/items/${req.objectId}`);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ProviderError && err.code === 'NOT_FOUND') return;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  // ── Quota ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async getQuota(): Promise<ProviderQuota> {
    const resp = await this.http.get<{
      quota: { total: number; used: number; remaining: number };
    }>('', { params: { select: 'quota' } });

    const q = resp.data.quota;
    return { totalBytes: q.total, usedBytes: q.used, freeBytes: q.remaining };
  }

  // ── Delta ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async pollDelta(cursor: DeltaCursor | null): Promise<DeltaPollResult> {
    const url = cursor ?? `${GRAPH_BASE}/root/delta`;

    const resp = await this.http.get<GraphDelta>(url.replace(GRAPH_BASE, ''));

    const changes = resp.data.value
      .filter((item) => item.id)
      .map((item) => ({
        objectId:   item.id,
        changeType: ('deleted' in item ? 'deleted' : 'modified') as 'deleted' | 'modified',
        newEtag:    item.eTag ?? null,
        modifiedAt: item.lastModifiedDateTime,
      }));

    const nextCursor = resp.data['@odata.nextLink']
      ?? resp.data['@odata.deltaLink']!;

    return {
      changes,
      nextCursor,
      hasMore: !!resp.data['@odata.nextLink'],
    };
  }

  // ── Folder ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async ensureAetherFolder(): Promise<EnsureFolderResult> {
    if (this._cachedFolderId) {
      return { folderId: this._cachedFolderId, created: false };
    }

    try {
      const resp = await this.http.get<GraphDriveItem>(
        `/root:/${encodeURIComponent(FOLDER_NAME)}`,
      );
      this._cachedFolderId   = resp.data.id;
      this._cachedFolderPath = FOLDER_NAME;
      return { folderId: resp.data.id, created: false };
    } catch (err) {
      if (!(err instanceof ProviderError) || err.code !== 'NOT_FOUND') throw err;
    }

    // Create folder
    const resp = await this.http.post<GraphDriveItem>('/root/children', {
      name:                             FOLDER_NAME,
      folder:                           {},
      '@microsoft.graph.conflictBehavior': 'fail',
    });

    this._cachedFolderId = resp.data.id;
    return { folderId: resp.data.id, created: true };
  }

  // ── Error translation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private translateError(err: AxiosError): ProviderError {
    const status = err.response?.status;
    if (status === 404)        return new ProviderError('NOT_FOUND',      'OneDrive item not found');
    if (status === 401 || status === 403) return new ProviderError('AUTH_EXPIRED', `OneDrive auth: ${status}`);
    if (status === 429) {
      const retryAfter = parseInt(
        (err.response?.headers as Record<string, string>)['retry-after'] ?? '5', 10,
      );
      return new ProviderError('RATE_LIMITED', 'OneDrive rate limited', retryAfter * 1000);
    }
    if (status === 507) return new ProviderError('QUOTA_EXCEEDED', 'OneDrive quota exceeded');
    if (!status)        return new ProviderError('NETWORK_ERROR',  `Network: ${err.message}`);
    return new ProviderError('PROVIDER_ERROR', `OneDrive ${status}`);
  }
}
```

#### `packages/storage-adapters/src/providers/DropboxAdapter.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * DropboxAdapter
 *
 * Dropbox API v2 (content + API endpoints).
 * Key differences from Google/OneDrive:
 *   - Upload: /files/upload (< 150 MB) or /upload_session/start → append → finish
 *   - Download: /files/download (Dropbox-API-Arg header carries metadata)
 *   - Object IDs: Dropbox uses path strings as primary IDs; also has stable `id:` IDs
 *     We use the stable id: format returned after upload for provider_object_id.
 *   - Delta: /files/list_folder/continue with cursor
 *   - Rate limit: 100 req/min per user (~ 1.67 req/s)
 */

import axios, { type AxiosError } from 'axios';
import {
  type IStorageProvider,
  type UploadRequest, type UploadResult,
  type DownloadRequest, type DownloadResult,
  type DeleteRequest, type ProviderQuota,
  type DeltaCursor, type DeltaPollResult,
  type EnsureFolderResult,
  ProviderError,
} from '../interfaces/IStorageProvider.js';

const API_BASE     = 'https://api.dropboxapi.com/2';
const CONTENT_BASE = 'https://content.dropboxapi.com/2';
const FOLDER_NAME  = 'Aether Secure Storage';
const SESSION_THRESHOLD = 150 * 1024 * 1024; // 150 MB

export interface DropboxAdapterOptions {
  getAccessToken: () => Promise<string>;
}

interface DropboxMetadata {
  '.tag':           string;
  id:               string;
  name:             string;
  size:             number;
  content_hash?:    string;
  server_modified:  string;
  path_lower:       string;
  path_display:     string;
}

export class DropboxAdapter implements IStorageProvider {
  readonly providerId = 'dropbox' as const;
  private _cachedFolderPath: string | null = null;

  constructor(private readonly opts: DropboxAdapterOptions) {}

  private async token(): Promise<string> {
    return this.opts.getAccessToken();
  }

  // ── Upload ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async upload(req: UploadRequest): Promise<UploadResult> {
    const folder = await this.ensureAetherFolder();

    if (req.data.length < SESSION_THRESHOLD) {
      return this.singleUpload(req, folder.folderId);
    }
    return this.sessionUpload(req, folder.folderId);
  }

  private async singleUpload(
    req:        UploadRequest,
    folderPath: string,
  ): Promise<UploadResult> {
    const path = `${folderPath}/${req.filename}`;
    const arg  = JSON.stringify({
      path,
      mode:          { '.tag': 'overwrite' },
      autorename:    false,
      mute:          true,
      strict_conflict: false,
    });

    const token = await this.token();
    const resp  = await axios.post<DropboxMetadata>(
      `${CONTENT_BASE}/files/upload`,
      Buffer.from(req.data),
      {
        headers: {
          Authorization:          `Bearer ${token}`,
          'Content-Type':         'application/octet-stream',
          'Dropbox-API-Arg':      arg,
        },
        timeout: 120_000,
      },
    );

    return this.toUploadResult(resp.data);
  }

  private async sessionUpload(
    req:        UploadRequest,
    folderPath: string,
  ): Promise<UploadResult> {
    const token = await this.token();

    // Start session
    const startResp = await axios.post<{ session_id: string }>(
      `${CONTENT_BASE}/files/upload_session/start`,
      Buffer.alloc(0),
      {
        headers: {
          Authorization:    `Bearer ${token}`,
          'Content-Type':   'application/octet-stream',
          'Dropbox-API-Arg': JSON.stringify({ close: false }),
        },
      },
    );
    const sessionId = startResp.data.session_id;
    const data      = Buffer.from(req.data);

    // Finish (send all data in one call for shards ≤ 64 MiB)
    const finishArg = JSON.stringify({
      cursor: { session_id: sessionId, offset: 0 },
      commit: {
        path: `${folderPath}/${req.filename}`,
        mode: { '.tag': 'overwrite' },
        mute: true,
      },
    });

    const finishResp = await axios.post<DropboxMetadata>(
      `${CONTENT_BASE}/files/upload_session/finish`,
      data,
      {
        headers: {
          Authorization:    `Bearer ${token}`,
          'Content-Type':   'application/octet-stream',
          'Dropbox-API-Arg': finishArg,
        },
        timeout: 120_000,
      },
    );

    return this.toUploadResult(finishResp.data);
  }

  private toUploadResult(meta: DropboxMetadata): UploadResult {
    return {
      objectId:   meta.id,       // stable id: format — survives renames/moves
      etag:       meta.content_hash ?? null,
      sizeBytes:  meta.size,
      uploadedAt: meta.server_modified,
    };
  }

  // ── Download ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async download(req: DownloadRequest): Promise<DownloadResult> {
    const token = await this.token();
    const arg   = JSON.stringify({ path: `id:${req.objectId.replace(/^id:/, '')}` });

    try {
      const resp = await axios.post<ArrayBuffer>(
        `${CONTENT_BASE}/files/download`,
        undefined,
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization:    `Bearer ${token}`,
            'Dropbox-API-Arg': arg,
          },
          responseType:   'arraybuffer',
          timeout:        60_000,
        },
      );

      const meta: DropboxMetadata = JSON.parse(
        (resp.headers['dropbox-api-result'] as string) ?? '{}'
      );
      const data = Buffer.from(resp.data);

      return {
        data,
        etag:        meta.content_hash ?? null,
        sizeBytes:   data.length,
        notModified: false,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      throw this.translateError(err as AxiosError);
    }
  }

  // ── Delete ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async delete(req: DeleteRequest): Promise<void> {
    const token = await this.token();
    try {
      await axios.post(
        `${API_BASE}/files/delete_v2`,
        { path: `id:${req.objectId.replace(/^id:/, '')}` },
        { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
      );
    } catch (err) {
      const pe = this.translateError(err as AxiosError);
      if (pe.code === 'NOT_FOUND') return; // idempotent
      throw pe;
    }
  }

  // ── Quota ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async getQuota(): Promise<ProviderQuota> {
    const token = await this.token();
    const resp  = await axios.post<{
      allocation: { '.tag': string; allocated?: number };
      used:       number;
    }>(
      `${API_BASE}/users/get_space_usage`,
      null,
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
    );

    const total = resp.data.allocation.allocated ?? -1;
    const used  = resp.data.used;
    return { totalBytes: total, usedBytes: used, freeBytes: total === -1 ? -1 : total - used };
  }

  // ── Delta ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async pollDelta(cursor: DeltaCursor | null): Promise<DeltaPollResult> {
    const token = await this.token();

    if (!cursor) {
      // First call: establish baseline cursor — drain entire listing
      let initCursor: string;
      const initResp = await axios.post<{ cursor: string; has_more: boolean }>(
        `${API_BASE}/files/list_folder`,
        { path: this._cachedFolderPath ?? '', recursive: true, include_deleted: true },
        { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
      );
      let cur     = initResp.data.cursor;
      let hasMore = initResp.data.has_more;

      while (hasMore) {
        const cont = await axios.post<{ cursor: string; has_more: boolean }>(
          `${API_BASE}/files/list_folder/continue`,
          { cursor: cur },
          { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
        );
        cur     = cont.data.cursor;
        hasMore = cont.data.has_more;
      }

      return { changes: [], nextCursor: cur, hasMore: false };
    }

    const resp = await axios.post<{
      cursor:   string;
      has_more: boolean;
      entries:  Array<{ '.tag': string; id?: string; server_modified?: string; content_hash?: string }>;
    }>(
      `${API_BASE}/files/list_folder/continue`,
      { cursor },
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
    );

    return {
      changes: resp.data.entries
        .filter((e) => e['id'])
        .map((e) => ({
          objectId:   e['id']!,
          changeType: e['.tag'] === 'deleted' ? 'deleted' : 'modified',
          newEtag:    e['content_hash'] ?? null,
          modifiedAt: e['server_modified'] ?? new Date().toISOString(),
        })),
      nextCursor: resp.data.cursor,
      hasMore:    resp.data.has_more,
    };
  }

  // ── Folder ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async ensureAetherFolder(): Promise<EnsureFolderResult> {
    if (this._cachedFolderPath) {
      return { folderId: this._cachedFolderPath, created: false };
    }

    const token    = await this.token();
    const path     = `/${FOLDER_NAME}`;

    try {
      await axios.post(
        `${API_BASE}/files/get_metadata`,
        { path },
        { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
      );
      this._cachedFolderPath = path;
      return { folderId: path, created: false };
    } catch {
      // Doesn't exist — create it
    }

    await axios.post(
      `${API_BASE}/files/create_folder_v2`,
      { path, autorename: false },
      { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } },
    );

    this._cachedFolderPath = path;
    return { folderId: path, created: true };
  }

  // ── Error translation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private translateError(err: AxiosError): ProviderError {
    const status = err.response?.status;
    if (status === 409) return new ProviderError('NOT_FOUND',     'Dropbox path not found');
    if (status === 401) return new ProviderError('AUTH_EXPIRED',  'Dropbox auth expired');
    if (status === 429) {
      const retry = parseInt(
        (err.response?.headers as Record<string, string>)['retry-after'] ?? '30', 10,
      );
      return new ProviderError('RATE_LIMITED', 'Dropbox rate limited', retry * 1000);
    }
    if (!status) return new ProviderError('NETWORK_ERROR', err.message);
    return new ProviderError('PROVIDER_ERROR', `Dropbox ${status}`);
  }
}
```

#### `packages/storage-adapters/src/providers/MegaAdapter.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * MegaAdapter
 *
 * MEGA does not provide an official REST API — it exposes a binary WebSocket
 * protocol documented only through reverse engineering and the official SDK.
 * We use the `megajs` npm package which wraps this protocol.
 *
 * Key differences:
 *   - MEGA is already E2EE at rest; Aether adds an additional encryption layer
 *     (our shards are encrypted before hitting MEGA's own encryption).
 *   - There is no standard OAuth2 flow — users provide email + password which
 *     derive the MEGA master key client-side. We store these credentials using
 *     the same server-side AES-256-GCM envelope as other providers.
 *   - Delta sync: MEGA pushes events over WebSocket. We poll the folder listing
 *     and compare checksums (attribute fingerprints) as a fallback.
 *   - Rate limits: unofficial; MEGA throttles aggressively at ~ 5 MB/s sustained.
 *
 * WARNING: megajs is community-maintained. Pin the exact version and audit
 * on every upgrade. MEGA's API can change without notice.
 */

import {
  type IStorageProvider,
  type UploadRequest, type UploadResult,
  type DownloadRequest, type DownloadResult,
  type DeleteRequest, type ProviderQuota,
  type DeltaCursor, type DeltaPollResult,
  type EnsureFolderResult,
  ProviderError,
} from '../interfaces/IStorageProvider.js';

// Dynamically imported to avoid loading megajs in environments that don't need it.
// megajs uses Node.js streams and cannot run in the browser.
type MegaStorage = {
  root: MegaNode;
  login(): Promise<void>;
  getAccountInfo(): Promise<{ spaceUsed: number; spaceTotal: number }>;
};
type MegaNode = {
  children: MegaNode[];
  name:     string;
  nodeId:   string;
  size?:    number;
  timestamp?: number;
  mkdir(name: string): Promise<MegaNode>;
  upload(opts: { name: string; size: number }, data: Buffer): { complete: Promise<MegaNode> };
  download(): Promise<Buffer>;
  delete(): Promise<void>;
};

const FOLDER_NAME = 'Aether Secure Storage';

export interface MegaAdapterOptions {
  email:    string;
  password: string;
}

export class MegaAdapter implements IStorageProvider {
  readonly providerId = 'mega' as const;

  private _storage: MegaStorage | null     = null;
  private _folder:  MegaNode | null        = null;
  private _initPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;

  constructor(private readonly opts: MegaAdapterOptions) {}

  private async init(): Promise<void> {
    if (this._storage) return;
    if (this._initPromise) { await this._initPromise; return; }

    this._initPromise = (async () => {
      const { Storage } = await import('megajs') as { Storage: new (opts: { email: string; password: string; autologin: boolean }) => MegaStorage };
      this._storage = new Storage({
        email:     this.opts.email,
        password:  this.opts.password,
        autologin: false,
      });
      await this._storage.login();
    })();

    await this._initPromise;
  }

  // ── Upload ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async upload(req: UploadRequest): Promise<UploadResult> {
    await this.init();
    const folder = await this.ensureAetherFolder();
    const node   = this._folder!;
    const data   = Buffer.from(req.data);

    const uploadHandle = node.upload(
      { name: req.filename, size: data.length },
      data,
    );

    const uploaded = await uploadHandle.complete;

    return {
      objectId:   uploaded.nodeId,
      etag:       null,  // MEGA doesn't provide content ETags
      sizeBytes:  data.length,
      uploadedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
  }

  // ── Download ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async download(req: DownloadRequest): Promise<DownloadResult> {
    await this.init();

    const folder = this._folder;
    if (!folder) throw new ProviderError('NOT_FOUND', 'MEGA Aether folder not initialised');

    const file = folder.children.find((n) => n.nodeId === req.objectId);
    if (!file)  throw new ProviderError('NOT_FOUND', `MEGA object ${req.objectId} not found`);

    const data = await file.download();
    return { data, etag: null, sizeBytes: data.length, notModified: false };
  }

  // ── Delete ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async delete(req: DeleteRequest): Promise<void> {
    await this.init();
    const file = this._folder?.children.find((n) => n.nodeId === req.objectId);
    if (!file) return; // idempotent

    try { await file.delete(); }
    catch (err) {
      // MEGA SDK throws on already-deleted nodes; treat as success
      if (String(err).includes('ENOENT')) return;
      throw new ProviderError('PROVIDER_ERROR', `MEGA delete: ${err}`);
    }
  }

  // ── Quota ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async getQuota(): Promise<ProviderQuota> {
    await this.init();
    const { spaceUsed, spaceTotal } = await this._storage!.getAccountInfo();
    return {
      totalBytes: spaceTotal,
      usedBytes:  spaceUsed,
      freeBytes:  spaceTotal - spaceUsed,
    };
  }

  // ── Delta ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async pollDelta(cursor: DeltaCursor | null): Promise<DeltaPollResult> {
    // MEGA uses WebSocket push events. As a polling fallback we snapshot
    // the folder's children and compare against the stored cursor (JSON snapshot).
    await this.init();
    await this.ensureAetherFolder();

    const children  = this._folder?.children ?? [];
    const snapshot  = JSON.stringify(
      children.map((n) => ({ id: n.nodeId, size: n.size, ts: n.timestamp }))
    );

    if (!cursor) {
      return { changes: [], nextCursor: snapshot, hasMore: false };
    }

    let previous: Array<{ id: string; size?: number; ts?: number }> = [];
    try { previous = JSON.parse(cursor) as typeof previous; }
    catch { /* corrupt cursor — treat as no prior state */ }

    const prevMap = new Map(previous.map((p) => [p.id, p]));
    const currMap = new Map(children.map((n) => [n.nodeId, n]));

    const changes: DeltaPollResult['changes'] = [];

    // Deletions
    for (const [id] of prevMap) {
      if (!currMap.has(id)) {
        changes.push({ objectId: id, changeType: 'deleted', newEtag: null, modifiedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
      }
    }
    // Modifications / creations
    for (const [id, node] of currMap) {
      const prev = prevMap.get(id);
      if (!prev || prev.size !== node.size || prev.ts !== node.timestamp) {
        changes.push({
          objectId:   id,
          changeType: prev ? 'modified' : 'created',
          newEtag:    null,
          modifiedAt: node.timestamp ? new Date(node.timestamp * 1000).toISOString() : new Date().toISOString(),
        });
      }
    }

    return { changes, nextCursor: snapshot, hasMore: false };
  }

  // ── Folder ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async ensureAetherFolder(): Promise<EnsureFolderResult> {
    await this.init();

    if (this._folder) return { folderId: this._folder.nodeId, created: false };

    const root     = this._storage!.root;
    const existing = root.children.find((n) => n.name === FOLDER_NAME);

    if (existing) {
      this._folder = existing;
      return { folderId: existing.nodeId, created: false };
    }

    const created = await root.mkdir(FOLDER_NAME);
    this._folder  = created;
    return { folderId: created.nodeId, created: true };
  }
}
```

#### `packages/storage-adapters/src/registry/ProviderRegistry.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * ProviderRegistry
 *
 * Singleton registry that creates and caches IStorageProvider instances
 * keyed by (userId, providerId). Credentials are loaded lazily via the
 * injected TokenService.
 *
 * Used by:
 *   - chunk-orchestrator Rust service (via ProviderFactory)
 *   - delta-sync-service TypeScript (directly)
 */

import type { IStorageProvider, ProviderId } from '../interfaces/IStorageProvider.js';
import { GoogleDriveAdapter }               from '../providers/GoogleDriveAdapter.js';
import { OneDriveAdapter }                  from '../providers/OneDriveAdapter.js';
import { DropboxAdapter }                   from '../providers/DropboxAdapter.js';
import { MegaAdapter }                      from '../providers/MegaAdapter.js';

export interface CredentialLoader {
  /** Returns a valid OAuth access token. Handles refresh internally. */
  getAccessToken(userId: string, providerId: string): Promise<string>;
  /** Returns MEGA email + password for the given user (decrypted by server KEK). */
  getMegaCredentials(userId: string): Promise<{ email: string; password: string }>;
}

// Composite key: `${userId}:${providerId}`
type CacheKey = string;

export class ProviderRegistry {
  private readonly cache = new Map<CacheKey, IStorageProvider>();

  constructor(private readonly loader: CredentialLoader) {}

  get(userId: string, providerId: ProviderId): IStorageProvider {
    const key = `${userId}:${providerId}`;

    if (this.cache.has(key)) return this.cache.get(key)!;

    const provider = this.create(userId, providerId);
    this.cache.set(key, provider);
    return provider;
  }

  /** Invalidate cached instance (e.g., after credential revocation). */
  invalidate(userId: string, providerId: ProviderId): void {
    this.cache.delete(`${userId}:${providerId}`);
  }

  private create(userId: string, providerId: ProviderId): IStorageProvider {
    switch (providerId) {
      case 'google_drive':
        return new GoogleDriveAdapter({
          getAccessToken: () => this.loader.getAccessToken(userId, 'google_drive'),
        });

      case 'onedrive':
        return new OneDriveAdapter({
          getAccessToken: () => this.loader.getAccessToken(userId, 'onedrive'),
        });

      case 'dropbox':
        return new DropboxAdapter({
          getAccessToken: () => this.loader.getAccessToken(userId, 'dropbox'),
        });

      case 'mega': {
        // MEGA credentials are loaded synchronously on first API call
        // via a promise-based lazy init inside the adapter.
        let email    = '';
        let password = '';
        let loaded   = false;

        const loadCreds = async () => {
          if (loaded) return;
          const creds = await this.loader.getMegaCredentials(userId);
          email    = creds.email;
          password = creds.password;
          loaded   = true;
        };

        // We construct with placeholder and let the adapter call loadCreds
        // on first use via its init() method.
        const adapter = new MegaAdapter({ email, password });

        // Monkey-patch init to load creds on first call
        const originalInit = (adapter as { init?: () => Promise<void> }).init?.bind(adapter);
        (adapter as { init: () => Promise<void> }).init = async () => {
          await loadCreds();
          (adapter as { opts: { email: string; password: string } }).opts.email    = email;
          (adapter as { opts: { email: string; password: string } }).opts.password = password;
          if (originalInit) await originalInit();
        };

        return adapter;
      }
    }
  }
}
```

### Part 2 · Chunk-Orchestrator REST Bridge (Axum)

The Next.js API routes reference `CHUNK_ORCHESTRATOR_REST_URL`. This Axum HTTP server wraps the existing gRPC use-cases with a thin REST layer — same process, different port.

#### Add to `services/chunk-orchestrator/Cargo.toml`

```toml
# Add to [dependencies]:
axum             = { version = "0.7", features = ["macros"] }
tower-http       = { version = "0.5", features = ["cors", "trace", "timeout"] }
serde_json       = { workspace = true }
```

#### `services/chunk-orchestrator/src/interface/http/extractors.rs`

```rust
use axum::{
    extract::FromRequestParts,
    http::{request::Parts, StatusCode},
    response::{IntoResponse, Response},
    Json,
};
use serde_json::json;
use uuid::Uuid;

/// Extracts validated user ID from the X-User-ID header.
/// Set by the Next.js API route after JWT verification.
pub struct UserId(pub Uuid);

#[axum::async_trait]
impl<S> FromRequestParts<S> for UserId
where
    S: Send + Sync,
{
    type Rejection = Response;

    async fn from_request_parts(parts: &mut Parts, _state: &S) -> Result<Self, Self::Rejection> {
        let header = parts
            .headers
            .get("x-user-id")
            .and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or("");

        Uuid::parse_str(header).map(UserId).map_err(|_| {
            (
                StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,
                Json(json!({ "error": "Missing or invalid X-User-ID header" })),
            )
                .into_response()
        })
    }
}
```

#### `services/chunk-orchestrator/src/interface/http/routes/upload.rs`

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use axum::{
    extract::{Json, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    response::IntoResponse,
};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::{
    application::use_cases::upload_file::{UploadFileCommand, UploadFileUseCase, UploadShardCmd},
    domain::entities::chunk::TOTAL_SHARDS,
    interface::http::extractors::UserId,
};

/// POST /upload/sessions
/// Called by Next.js to get provider session URLs for direct shard upload.
/// In Phase 7 we return pre-signed Google Drive resumable session URLs.
#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct PrepareRequest {
    pub version_id:  String,
    pub chunk_index: i32,
    pub shard_count: usize,
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
pub struct ShardAssignment {
    pub shard_index:  usize,
    pub provider_id:  String,
    pub session_url:  String,
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
pub struct PrepareResponse {
    pub assignments: Vec<ShardAssignment>,
}

pub async fn prepare_upload_sessions(
    UserId(user_id): UserId,
    State(state):    State<Arc<HttpState>>,
    Json(req):       Json<PrepareRequest>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    // In production: call the provider factory to create upload sessions
    // and return the real session URLs. For Phase 7 we return placeholder
    // URLs that the client will replace with actual provider calls.
    //
    // Real implementation calls:
    //   provider.create_upload_session(filename, size) → session_url
    // per shard, distributed across providers using ErasureDistributor.

    let version_id = match Uuid::parse_str(&req.version_id) {
        Ok(id) => id,
        Err(_) => return (
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            axum::Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": "invalid version_id" }))
        ).into_response(),
    };

    let provider_ids = state.provider_factory.all_provider_ids();
    let n = provider_ids.len().max(1);

    let assignments: Vec<ShardAssignment> = (0..req.shard_count)
        .map(|shard_index| {
            let provider_id = provider_ids[shard_index % n].clone();
            // The session URL would be created by the provider adapter.
            // Returning a routed internal URL so the Next.js client can POST
            // the shard back to us (we proxy to provider in Phase 7+).
            let session_url = format!(
                "/upload/shard-relay/{}/{}/{}/{}",
                user_id, version_id.as_simple(), req.chunk_index, shard_index
            );
            ShardAssignment { shard_index, provider_id, session_url }
        })
        .collect();

    (StatusCode::OK, axum::Json(PrepareResponse { assignments })).into_response()
}

/// POST /upload/commit
/// Receives the full shard manifest and writes physical_chunks to CockroachDB.
#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct CommitManifestEntry {
    pub chunk_index:   i32,
    pub shard_index:   i32,
    pub provider_id:   String,
    pub object_id:     String,
    pub etag:          Option<String>,
    pub checksum:      Vec<u8>,
    pub size_bytes:    i64,
    pub wrapped_dek:   Vec<u8>,
    pub dek_nonce:     Vec<u8>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct CommitRequest {
    pub inode_id:   String,
    pub version_id: String,
    pub manifest:   Vec<CommitManifestEntry>,
}

pub async fn commit_upload(
    UserId(user_id): UserId,
    State(state):    State<Arc<HttpState>>,
    Json(req):       Json<CommitRequest>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    use crate::domain::entities::chunk::PhysicalChunk;

    let version_id = match Uuid::parse_str(&req.version_id) {
        Ok(id) => id,
        Err(_) => return (
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            axum::Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": "invalid version_id" }))
        ).into_response(),
    };

    let chunks: Vec<PhysicalChunk> = req.manifest.iter().map(|entry| {
        PhysicalChunk::new(
            version_id,
            entry.chunk_index,
            entry.shard_index,
            entry.provider_id.clone(),
            entry.object_id.clone(),
            entry.etag.clone(),
            entry.size_bytes,
            entry.checksum.clone(),
        )
    }).collect();

    match state.chunk_repo.insert_chunks(chunks).await {
        Ok(()) => (
            StatusCode::OK,
            axum::Json(serde_json::json!({ "ok": true, "versionId": req.version_id }))
        ).into_response(),
        Err(e) => (
            StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            axum::Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))
        ).into_response(),
    }
}

// ── Shared HTTP state ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

use crate::{
    domain::repositories::chunk_repository::ChunkRepository,
    infrastructure::providers::factory::ProviderFactory,
    infrastructure::providers::token_manager::TokenManager,
};

pub struct HttpState {
    pub chunk_repo:       Arc<dyn ChunkRepository>,
    pub provider_factory: Arc<ProviderFactory>,
    pub token_manager:    Arc<TokenManager>,
}
```

#### `services/chunk-orchestrator/src/interface/http/routes/manifest.rs`

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use axum::{
    extract::{Path, State},
    http::StatusCode,
    response::IntoResponse,
    Json,
};
use serde::Serialize;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::{
    domain::entities::chunk::{PhysicalChunk, ShardStatus},
    interface::http::extractors::UserId,
    interface::http::routes::upload::HttpState,
};

#[derive(Serialize)]
pub struct ShardEntry {
    pub chunk_index:       i32,
    pub shard_index:       i32,
    pub provider_id:       String,
    pub provider_object_id: String,
    pub size_bytes:        i64,
    pub checksum:          Vec<u8>,
    pub shard_status:      String,
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
pub struct ManifestResponse {
    pub version_id: String,
    pub shards:     Vec<ShardEntry>,
}

pub async fn get_manifest(
    UserId(_user_id): UserId,
    State(state):     State<Arc<HttpState>>,
    Path(version_id_str): Path<String>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let version_id = match Uuid::parse_str(&version_id_str) {
        Ok(id) => id,
        Err(_) => return (
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": "invalid version_id" }))
        ).into_response(),
    };

    match state.chunk_repo.get_all_chunks(version_id).await {
        Ok(chunks) => {
            let shards: Vec<ShardEntry> = chunks.into_iter().map(|c| ShardEntry {
                chunk_index:        c.chunk_index,
                shard_index:        c.shard_index,
                provider_id:        c.provider_id,
                provider_object_id: c.provider_object_id,
                size_bytes:         c.size_bytes,
                checksum:           c.checksum,
                shard_status:       c.shard_status.to_string(),
            }).collect();

            (StatusCode::OK, Json(ManifestResponse { version_id: version_id_str, shards })).into_response()
        }
        Err(e) => (
            StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
            Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))
        ).into_response(),
    }
}
```

#### `services/chunk-orchestrator/src/interface/http/routes/shard.rs`

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use axum::{
    body::Body,
    extract::{Query, State},
    http::{HeaderMap, StatusCode},
    response::{IntoResponse, Response},
    Json,
};
use serde::Deserialize;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::{
    interface::http::extractors::UserId,
    interface::http::routes::upload::HttpState,
};

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct ShardQuery {
    pub object_id:  String,
    pub provider:   String,
}

/// GET /shard?objectId=...&provider=...
/// Proxies a shard download from the provider (encrypted bytes only).
pub async fn get_shard(
    UserId(user_id): UserId,
    State(state):    State<Arc<HttpState>>,
    Query(q):        Query<ShardQuery>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let client = match state.provider_factory.get(&q.provider) {
        Ok(c)  => c,
        Err(e) => return (
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": format!("unknown provider: {}", q.provider) }))
        ).into_response(),
    };

    let access_token = match state.token_manager.get_access_token(user_id, &q.provider).await {
        Ok(t)  => t,
        Err(e) => return (
            StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,
            Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))
        ).into_response(),
    };

    match client.download_shard(&q.object_id, None, &access_token).await {
        Ok(result) => {
            let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
            headers.insert(
                axum::http::header::CONTENT_TYPE,
                "application/octet-stream".parse().unwrap(),
            );
            if let Some(etag) = result.etag {
                if let Ok(val) = etag.parse() {
                    headers.insert(axum::http::header::ETAG, val);
                }
            }
            (headers, result.data).into_response()
        }
        Err(e) => {
            use crate::error::ProviderErrorCode;
            let status = match e {
                crate::error::OrchestratorError::Provider { code: ProviderErrorCode::NotFound, .. }
                    => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
                crate::error::OrchestratorError::Provider { code: ProviderErrorCode::RateLimited, .. }
                    => StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,
                _ => StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,
            };
            (status, Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response()
        }
    }
}
```

#### `services/chunk-orchestrator/src/interface/http/server.rs`

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::net::SocketAddr;
use axum::{
    routing::{get, post},
    Router,
};
use tower_http::{
    cors::{Any, CorsLayer},
    timeout::TimeoutLayer,
    trace::TraceLayer,
};
use std::time::Duration;

use super::routes::{
    upload::{commit_upload, prepare_upload_sessions, HttpState},
    manifest::get_manifest,
    shard::get_shard,
};

pub async fn start_rest_server(
    state:    Arc<HttpState>,
    http_port: u16,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let cors = CorsLayer::new()
        .allow_origin(Any)
        .allow_methods(Any)
        .allow_headers(Any);

    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/health",            get(health))
        .route("/upload/sessions",   post(prepare_upload_sessions))
        .route("/upload/commit",     post(commit_upload))
        .route("/manifest/:version", get(get_manifest))
        .route("/shard",             get(get_shard))
        .layer(cors)
        .layer(TraceLayer::new_for_http())
        .layer(TimeoutLayer::new(Duration::from_secs(120)))
        .with_state(state);

    let addr: SocketAddr = format!("0.0.0.0:{http_port}").parse()?;
    tracing::info!(addr = %addr, "REST bridge listening");

    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(addr).await?;
    axum::serve(listener, app).await?;
    Ok(())
}

async fn health() -> axum::Json<serde_json::Value> {
    axum::Json(serde_json::json!({
        "status": "ok",
        "service": "chunk-orchestrator"
    }))
}
```

#### Update `services/chunk-orchestrator/src/main.rs` (add REST server spawn)

```rust
// Add after the gRPC server setup, before .serve_with_shutdown:
use interface::http::{
    server::start_rest_server,
    routes::upload::HttpState,
};

let http_state = Arc::new(HttpState {
    chunk_repo:       Arc::clone(&chunk_repo),
    provider_factory: Arc::clone(&provider_factory),
    token_manager:    Arc::clone(&token_manager),
});

// Spawn REST bridge alongside gRPC
tokio::spawn(async move {
    if let Err(e) = start_rest_server(http_state, cfg.http_port).await {
        tracing::error!("REST bridge error: {e}");
    }
});
```

### Part 3 · OpenTelemetry Instrumentation

#### `services/chunk-orchestrator/src/telemetry.rs`

```rust
//! OpenTelemetry setup for all Rust services.
//! Exports traces to the OTel collector (OTLP/gRPC).
//! Metrics exported to Prometheus scrape endpoint.

use opentelemetry::{
    global,
    trace::TracerProvider,
    KeyValue,
};
use opentelemetry_otlp::WithExportConfig;
use opentelemetry_sdk::{
    propagation::TraceContextPropagator,
    runtime,
    trace as sdktrace,
    Resource,
};
use tracing_opentelemetry::OpenTelemetryLayer;
use tracing_subscriber::{layer::SubscriberExt, util::SubscriberInitExt, EnvFilter};

pub fn init_telemetry(service_name: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    // OTLP endpoint — defaults to local collector; override via OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT
    let otlp_endpoint = std::env::var("OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT")
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| "http://localhost:4317".into());

    global::set_text_map_propagator(TraceContextPropagator::new());

    let resource = Resource::new(vec![
        KeyValue::new("service.name",    service_name.to_string()),
        KeyValue::new("service.version", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")),
        KeyValue::new("deployment.environment",
            std::env::var("NODE_ENV").unwrap_or_else(|_| "development".into())),
    ]);

    let tracer_provider = opentelemetry_otlp::new_pipeline()
        .tracing()
        .with_exporter(
            opentelemetry_otlp::new_exporter()
                .tonic()
                .with_endpoint(otlp_endpoint),
        )
        .with_trace_config(
            sdktrace::Config::default().with_resource(resource),
        )
        .install_batch(runtime::Tokio)?;

    let tracer = tracer_provider.tracer(service_name.to_string());

    tracing_subscriber::registry()
        .with(EnvFilter::try_from_default_env()
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| format!("{}=debug,tower_http=info,sqlx=warn", service_name.replace('-', "_")).into()))
        .with(tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer().json())
        .with(OpenTelemetryLayer::new(tracer))
        .init();

    Ok(())
}

pub fn shutdown_telemetry() {
    global::shutdown_tracer_provider();
}
```

#### `services/delta-sync-service/src/telemetry.ts`

```typescript
// OpenTelemetry setup for TypeScript services.
// Must be imported FIRST before any other module.

import { NodeSDK }                            from '@opentelemetry/sdk-node';
import { OTLPTraceExporter }                  from '@opentelemetry/exporter-trace-otlp-grpc';
import { OTLPMetricExporter }                 from '@opentelemetry/exporter-metrics-otlp-grpc';
import { PeriodicExportingMetricReader }       from '@opentelemetry/sdk-metrics';
import { Resource }                           from '@opentelemetry/resources';
import { SemanticResourceAttributes }         from '@opentelemetry/semantic-conventions';
import { HttpInstrumentation }                from '@opentelemetry/instrumentation-http';
import { PgInstrumentation }                  from '@opentelemetry/instrumentation-pg';
import { WinstonInstrumentation }             from '@opentelemetry/instrumentation-winston';

const otlpEndpoint = process.env['OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT'] ?? 'http://localhost:4317';

const sdk = new NodeSDK({
  resource: new Resource({
    [SemanticResourceAttributes.SERVICE_NAME]:    process.env['SERVICE_NAME'] ?? 'delta-sync-service',
    [SemanticResourceAttributes.SERVICE_VERSION]: process.env['npm_package_version'] ?? '0.1.0',
    'deployment.environment': process.env['NODE_ENV'] ?? 'development',
  }),
  traceExporter:  new OTLPTraceExporter({ url: `${otlpEndpoint}` }),
  metricReader:   new PeriodicExportingMetricReader({
    exporter:       new OTLPMetricExporter({ url: `${otlpEndpoint}` }),
    exportIntervalMillis: 15_000,
  }),
  instrumentations: [
    new HttpInstrumentation(),
    new PgInstrumentation(),
    new WinstonInstrumentation(),
  ],
});

sdk.start();

process.on('SIGTERM', async () => {
  await sdk.shutdown();
});

export {};
```

#### `infra/k8s/base/services/chunk-orchestrator-deployment.yaml`

```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: chunk-orchestrator
  namespace: aether
  labels:
    app: chunk-orchestrator
    version: "0.1.0"
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: chunk-orchestrator
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxUnavailable: 1
      maxSurge: 1
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: chunk-orchestrator
      annotations:
        prometheus.io/scrape: "true"
        prometheus.io/port:   "9090"
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: aether-workload
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 30
      containers:
        - name: chunk-orchestrator
          image: ghcr.io/aether-os/chunk-orchestrator:latest
          ports:
            - { name: grpc, containerPort: 50051 }
            - { name: http, containerPort: 3002 }
            - { name: metrics, containerPort: 9090 }
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef: { name: aether-config }
          env:
            - name: DATABASE_URL
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef: { name: aether-secrets, key: DATABASE_URL }
            - name: SERVER_TOKEN_KEK_BASE64
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef: { name: aether-secrets, key: SERVER_TOKEN_KEK_BASE64 }
            - name: GOOGLE_DRIVE__CLIENT_ID
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef: { name: aether-secrets, key: GOOGLE_DRIVE_CLIENT_ID }
            - name: GOOGLE_DRIVE__CLIENT_SECRET
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef: { name: aether-secrets, key: GOOGLE_DRIVE_CLIENT_SECRET }
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu:    "500m"
              memory: "512Mi"
            limits:
              cpu:    "2000m"
              memory: "2Gi"
          livenessProbe:
            grpc:
              port: 50051
            initialDelaySeconds: 10
            periodSeconds:       15
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health
              port: 3002
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds:       10
          lifecycle:
            preStop:
              exec:
                command: ["/bin/sh", "-c", "sleep 5"]
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: chunk-orchestrator
  namespace: aether
spec:
  selector:
    app: chunk-orchestrator
  ports:
    - { name: grpc,    port: 50051, targetPort: 50051 }
    - { name: http,    port: 3002,  targetPort: 3002 }
    - { name: metrics, port: 9090,  targetPort: 9090 }
```

```yaml
    matchLabels:
      app: web
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: web
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: aether-workload
      containers:
        - name: web
          image: ghcr.io/aether-os/web:latest
          ports:
            - { containerPort: 3000 }
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef: { name: aether-config }
          env:
            - name: JWT_PUBLIC_KEY_BASE64
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef: { name: aether-secrets, key: JWT_PUBLIC_KEY_BASE64 }
            - name: JWT_ISSUER
              value: "https://api.aether.app"
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: "200m", memory: "256Mi" }
            limits:   { cpu: "1000m", memory: "1Gi" }
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet: { path: /api/health, port: 3000 }
            initialDelaySeconds: 10
            periodSeconds: 15
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: web
  namespace: aether
spec:
  selector:
    app: web
  ports:
    - { port: 3000, targetPort: 3000 }
---
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Ingress
metadata:
  name: web-ingress
  namespace: aether
  annotations:
    kubernetes.io/ingress.class: "nginx"
    cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer: "letsencrypt-prod"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/ssl-redirect: "true"
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/proxy-body-size: "200m"
spec:
  tls:
    - hosts: ["app.aether.app"]
      secretName: aether-tls
  rules:
    - host: app.aether.app
      http:
        paths:
          - path: /
            pathType: Prefix
            backend:
              service:
                name: web
                port: { number: 3000 }
```

#### `infra/k8s/base/observability/otel-collector.yaml`

```yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: otel-collector-config
  namespace: aether
data:
  config.yaml: |
    receivers:
      otlp:
        protocols:
          grpc: { endpoint: "0.0.0.0:4317" }
          http: { endpoint: "0.0.0.0:4318" }

    processors:
      batch:
        timeout:    10s
        send_batch_size: 512
      memory_limiter:
        check_interval: 1s
        limit_mib:      400
        spike_limit_mib: 100
      resource:
        attributes:
          - key: environment
            value: production
            action: upsert

    exporters:
      otlp/tempo:
        endpoint: "http://tempo:4317"
        tls: { insecure: true }
      prometheus:
        endpoint: "0.0.0.0:8889"
        namespace: aether
      loki:
        endpoint: "http://loki:3100/loki/api/v1/push"

    service:
      pipelines:
        traces:
          receivers:  [otlp]
          processors: [memory_limiter, batch, resource]
          exporters:  [otlp/tempo]
        metrics:
          receivers:  [otlp]
          processors: [memory_limiter, batch]
          exporters:  [prometheus]
---
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: otel-collector
  namespace: aether
spec:
  replicas: 1
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: otel-collector
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: otel-collector
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: otel-collector
          image: otel/opentelemetry-collector-contrib:0.101.0
          args: ["--config=/etc/otel/config.yaml"]
          ports:
            - { containerPort: 4317, name: otlp-grpc }
            - { containerPort: 4318, name: otlp-http }
            - { containerPort: 8889, name: prometheus }
          volumeMounts:
            - name: config
              mountPath: /etc/otel
          resources:
            requests: { cpu: "200m", memory: "256Mi" }
            limits:   { cpu: "500m", memory: "512Mi" }
      volumes:
        - name: config
          configMap: { name: otel-collector-config }
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: otel-collector
  namespace: aether
spec:
  selector:
    app: otel-collector
  ports:
    - { name: otlp-grpc, port: 4317 }
    - { name: otlp-http, port: 4318 }
    - { name: prometheus, port: 8889 }
```

### Part 5 · Terraform — EKS Production Cluster

#### `infra/terraform/modules/eks/main.tf`

```hcl
# EKS cluster with Karpenter just-in-time node provisioning.
# Karpenter eliminates ASG warmup delays (10 min → < 60s node launch).

terraform {
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source  = "hashicorp/aws"
      version = "~> 5.0"
    }
    kubernetes = {
      source  = "hashicorp/kubernetes"
      version = "~> 2.0"
    }
    helm = {
      source  = "hashicorp/helm"
      version = "~> 2.0"
    }
  }
}

# ── VPC ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

module "vpc" {
  source  = "terraform-aws-modules/vpc/aws"
  version = "~> 5.0"

  name = "${var.cluster_name}-vpc"
  cidr = "10.0.0.0/16"

  azs             = ["${var.region}a", "${var.region}b", "${var.region}c"]
  private_subnets = ["10.0.1.0/24", "10.0.2.0/24", "10.0.3.0/24"]
  public_subnets  = ["10.0.101.0/24", "10.0.102.0/24", "10.0.103.0/24"]

  enable_nat_gateway     = true
  single_nat_gateway     = false  # HA: one NAT GW per AZ
  enable_dns_hostnames   = true

  # Required tags for Karpenter node discovery
  private_subnet_tags = {
    "kubernetes.io/cluster/${var.cluster_name}" = "shared"
    "kubernetes.io/role/internal-elb"           = "1"
    "karpenter.sh/discovery"                    = var.cluster_name
  }
}

# ── EKS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

module "eks" {
  source  = "terraform-aws-modules/eks/aws"
  version = "~> 20.0"

  cluster_name    = var.cluster_name
  cluster_version = "1.30"

  vpc_id     = module.vpc.vpc_id
  subnet_ids = module.vpc.private_subnets

  # Karpenter manages worker nodes; we only need a small managed node group
  # for system components (CoreDNS, kube-proxy, Karpenter itself).
  eks_managed_node_groups = {
    system = {
      instance_types = ["t3.medium"]
      min_size       = 2
      max_size       = 4
      desired_size   = 2
      labels = {
        role = "system"
      }
      taints = [{
        key    = "CriticalAddonsOnly"
        value  = "true"
        effect = "NO_SCHEDULE"
      }]
    }
  }

  cluster_addons = {
    coredns                = { most_recent = true }
    kube-proxy             = { most_recent = true }
    vpc-cni                = { most_recent = true }
    aws-ebs-csi-driver     = { most_recent = true }
  }

  cluster_endpoint_public_access = true

  # IRSA — allows pods to assume IAM roles via service account annotations
  enable_irsa = true
}

# ── Karpenter ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

module "karpenter" {
  source  = "terraform-aws-modules/eks/aws//modules/karpenter"
  version = "~> 20.0"

  cluster_name = module.eks.cluster_name

  # IRSA for Karpenter controller
  irsa_oidc_provider_arn          = module.eks.oidc_provider_arn
  irsa_namespace_service_accounts = ["karpenter:karpenter"]

  # Spot instance interruption handling
  enable_spot_termination = true
}

resource "helm_release" "karpenter" {
  namespace        = "karpenter"
  create_namespace = true
  name             = "karpenter"
  repository       = "oci://public.ecr.aws/karpenter"
  chart            = "karpenter"
  version          = "0.37.0"
  wait             = false

  values = [yamlencode({
    settings = {
      clusterName       = module.eks.cluster_name
      clusterEndpoint   = module.eks.cluster_endpoint
      interruptionQueue = module.karpenter.queue_name
    }
    serviceAccount = {
      annotations = {
        "eks.amazonaws.com/role-arn" = module.karpenter.irsa_arn
      }
    }
  })]
}

# Karpenter NodePool — workload nodes
resource "kubectl_manifest" "karpenter_node_pool" {
  yaml_body = yamlencode({
    apiVersion = "karpenter.sh/v1beta1"
    kind       = "NodePool"
    metadata   = { name = "aether-workload" }
    spec = {
      template = {
        spec = {
          nodeClassRef = {
            apiVersion = "karpenter.k8s.aws/v1beta1"
            kind       = "EC2NodeClass"
            name       = "aether"
          }
          requirements = [
            { key = "karpenter.sh/capacity-type",  operator = "In", values = ["spot", "on-demand"] },
            { key = "kubernetes.io/arch",           operator = "In", values = ["amd64"] },
            { key = "karpenter.k8s.aws/instance-category", operator = "In", values = ["c", "m", "r"] },
            { key = "karpenter.k8s.aws/instance-generation", operator = "Gt", values = ["2"] },
          ]
        }
      }
      disruption = {
        consolidationPolicy = "WhenUnderutilized"
        consolidateAfter    = "30s"
      }
      limits = { cpu = "200", memory = "400Gi" }
    }
  })
  depends_on = [helm_release.karpenter]
}

resource "kubectl_manifest" "karpenter_node_class" {
  yaml_body = yamlencode({
    apiVersion = "karpenter.k8s.aws/v1beta1"
    kind       = "EC2NodeClass"
    metadata   = { name = "aether" }
    spec = {
      amiFamily     = "AL2023"
      role          = module.karpenter.node_iam_role_name
      subnetSelectorTerms = [
        { tags = { "karpenter.sh/discovery" = var.cluster_name } }
      ]
      securityGroupSelectorTerms = [
        { tags = { "karpenter.sh/discovery" = var.cluster_name } }
      ]
      blockDeviceMappings = [{
        deviceName = "/dev/xvda"
        ebs = { volumeSize = "50Gi", volumeType = "gp3", encrypted = true }
      }]
    }
  })
  depends_on = [helm_release.karpenter]
}
```

#### `infra/terraform/modules/eks/variables.tf`

```hcl
output "vpc_id"                { value = module.vpc.vpc_id }
output "private_subnet_ids"    { value = module.vpc.private_subnets }
output "karpenter_node_role"   { value = module.karpenter.node_iam_role_name }
```

#### `infra/terraform/environments/production/main.tf`

```hcl
terraform {
  required_version = ">= 1.8"
  backend "s3" {
    bucket         = "aether-terraform-state-prod"
    key            = "production/terraform.tfstate"
    region         = "us-east-1"
    dynamodb_table = "aether-tf-locks"
    encrypt        = true
  }
}

provider "aws" {
  region = var.region
  default_tags {
    tags = {
      Project     = "aether"
      Environment = "production"
      ManagedBy   = "terraform"
    }
  }
}

# ── EKS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

module "eks" {
  source       = "../../modules/eks"
  cluster_name = "aether-prod"
  region       = var.region
}

# ── CockroachDB Dedicated ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

terraform {
  required_providers {
    cockroach = {
      source  = "cockroachdb/cockroach"
      version = "~> 1.0"
    }
  }
}

resource "cockroach_cluster" "prod" {
  name           = "aether-prod"
  cloud_provider = "AWS"
  plan           = "ADVANCED"
  regions = [
    { name = "us-east-1", primary = true  },
    { name = "us-west-2", primary = false },
  ]
  serverless = null
  dedicated = {
    storage_gib    = 500
    num_virtual_cpus = 8
  }
}

# ── ElastiCache Redis (DragonflyDB on K8s in prod; fallback to ElastiCache) ───

resource "aws_elasticache_replication_group" "aether" {
  replication_group_id          = "aether-prod"
  description                   = "Aether Redis cluster"
  node_type                     = "cache.r7g.large"
  num_cache_clusters            = 3   # 1 primary + 2 replicas
  automatic_failover_enabled    = true
  multi_az_enabled              = true
  at_rest_encryption_enabled    = true
  transit_encryption_enabled    = true
  subnet_group_name             = aws_elasticache_subnet_group.aether.name

  log_delivery_configuration {
    destination      = aws_cloudwatch_log_group.redis.name
    destination_type = "cloudwatch-logs"
    log_format       = "json"
    log_type         = "slow-log"
  }
}

resource "aws_elasticache_subnet_group" "aether" {
  name       = "aether-redis"
  subnet_ids = module.eks.private_subnet_ids
}

resource "aws_cloudwatch_log_group" "redis" {
  name              = "/aether/redis"
  retention_in_days = 30
}

# ── Secrets Manager ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

resource "aws_secretsmanager_secret" "aether_secrets" {
  name                    = "aether/production"
  recovery_window_in_days = 7
}

# Secrets are populated manually or via CI/CD — never in Terraform state.
# Reference: aws secretsmanager put-secret-value --secret-id aether/production \
#            --secret-string '{"DATABASE_URL":"...","SERVER_TOKEN_KEK_BASE64":"..."}'

# ── IAM role for EKS pods (IRSA) ──────────────────────────────────────────────

resource "aws_iam_role" "aether_workload" {
  name = "aether-workload"
  assume_role_policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [{
      Effect = "Allow"
      Principal = {
        Federated = module.eks.oidc_provider_arn
      }
      Action = "sts:AssumeRoleWithWebIdentity"
      Condition = {
        StringEquals = {
          "${replace(module.eks.oidc_provider_arn, "/^.*oidc-provider\\//", "")}:sub" =
            "system:serviceaccount:aether:aether-workload"
        }
      }
    }]
  })
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "aether_workload_secrets" {
  name   = "secrets-access"
  role   = aws_iam_role.aether_workload.id
  policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [{
      Effect   = "Allow"
      Action   = ["secretsmanager:GetSecretValue", "secretsmanager:DescribeSecret"]
      Resource = aws_secretsmanager_secret.aether_secrets.arn
    }]
  })
}
```

### Part 6 · GitHub Actions CI/CD

#### `.github/workflows/ci.yml`

```yaml
name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, develop]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]

env:
  CARGO_TERM_COLOR: always
  REGISTRY: ghcr.io
  IMAGE_PREFIX: ghcr.io/${{ github.repository_owner }}/aether

jobs:
  # ── Rust ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  rust-test:
    name: Rust tests
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable
        with:
          targets: wasm32-unknown-unknown
      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@v2

      - name: Install wasm-pack
        run: cargo install wasm-pack --locked

      - name: Cargo test (native)
        run: cargo test --workspace --all-features

      - name: Build WASM (crypto-core)
        run: |
          cd packages/crypto-core
          wasm-pack build --target nodejs --out-dir pkg-node --release

      - name: CLI e2e ZK test
        run: |
          cd tools/cli
          pnpm install
          pnpm run test:e2e

  # ── TypeScript ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  ts-test:
    name: TypeScript typecheck + tests
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with: { version: 9 }
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with: { node-version: 20, cache: pnpm }

      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
      - run: turbo run typecheck
      - run: turbo run test --if-present

  # ── Build & push Docker images ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  build:
    name: Build images
    needs: [rust-test, ts-test]
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
    permissions:
      packages: write
      contents: read

    strategy:
      matrix:
        service:
          - { name: chunk-orchestrator, dockerfile: services/chunk-orchestrator/Dockerfile }
          - { name: metadata-service,   dockerfile: services/metadata-service/Dockerfile   }
          - { name: auth-service,       dockerfile: services/auth-service/Dockerfile       }
          - { name: delta-sync-service, dockerfile: services/delta-sync-service/Dockerfile }
          - { name: web,                dockerfile: apps/web/Dockerfile                    }

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Log in to GHCR
        uses: docker/login-action@v3
        with:
          registry: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3

      - name: Build and push
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          file:    ${{ matrix.service.dockerfile }}
          push:    true
          tags: |
            ${{ env.IMAGE_PREFIX }}/${{ matrix.service.name }}:latest
            ${{ env.IMAGE_PREFIX }}/${{ matrix.service.name }}:${{ github.sha }}
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to:   type=gha,mode=max

  # ── Deploy to EKS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  deploy:
    name: Deploy to production
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
    environment: production

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Configure AWS credentials
        uses: aws-actions/configure-aws-credentials@v4
        with:
          role-to-assume: arn:aws:iam::${{ secrets.AWS_ACCOUNT_ID }}:role/github-actions-deploy
          aws-region:     us-east-1

      - name: Update kubeconfig
        run: aws eks update-kubeconfig --name aether-prod --region us-east-1

      - name: Kustomize — set image tags
        run: |
          cd infra/k8s/overlays/production
          kustomize edit set image \
            ghcr.io/aether-os/chunk-orchestrator=ghcr.io/aether-os/chunk-orchestrator:${{ github.sha }} \
            ghcr.io/aether-os/auth-service=ghcr.io/aether-os/auth-service:${{ github.sha }} \
            ghcr.io/aether-os/web=ghcr.io/aether-os/web:${{ github.sha }}

      - name: Apply manifests
        run: kubectl apply -k infra/k8s/overlays/production

      - name: Wait for rollout
        run: |
          for svc in chunk-orchestrator auth-service metadata-service web; do
            kubectl rollout status deployment/$svc -n aether --timeout=5m
          done

      - name: Smoke test
        run: |
          INGRESS_IP=$(kubectl get ingress web-ingress -n aether \
            -o jsonpath='{.status.loadBalancer.ingress[0].hostname}')
          curl -sf "https://${INGRESS_IP}/api/health" | jq '.status == "ok"'
```

#### `services/chunk-orchestrator/Dockerfile`

```dockerfile
# Multi-stage Rust build — produces a minimal production image < 50 MB

# ── Build stage ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FROM rust:1.78-bookworm AS builder

WORKDIR /app

# Cache dependencies separately
COPY Cargo.toml Cargo.lock ./
COPY packages/crypto-core/Cargo.toml packages/crypto-core/
COPY services/chunk-orchestrator/Cargo.toml services/chunk-orchestrator/
RUN mkdir -p packages/crypto-core/src services/chunk-orchestrator/src \
 && echo "fn main() {}" > services/chunk-orchestrator/src/main.rs \
 && echo "pub fn noop() {}" > packages/crypto-core/src/lib.rs \
 && cargo build --release -p chunk-orchestrator \
 && rm -rf packages/crypto-core/src services/chunk-orchestrator/src

# Build actual source
COPY packages/crypto-core/src packages/crypto-core/src
COPY services/chunk-orchestrator/src services/chunk-orchestrator/src
COPY services/chunk-orchestrator/proto services/chunk-orchestrator/proto
COPY services/chunk-orchestrator/build.rs services/chunk-orchestrator/
COPY services/chunk-orchestrator/config services/chunk-orchestrator/config
RUN cargo build --release -p chunk-orchestrator

# ── Runtime stage ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
FROM debian:bookworm-slim AS runtime

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    ca-certificates libssl3 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=builder /app/target/release/chunk-orchestrator .
COPY --from=builder /app/services/chunk-orchestrator/config config

ENV RUST_LOG=info
EXPOSE 50051 3002 9090

USER 1000:1000
ENTRYPOINT ["./chunk-orchestrator"]
```

```dockerfile
# Next.js production image with standalone output

FROM node:20-alpine AS base

 && adduser  --system --uid 1001 nextjs

```

### Step 7 · Build & Deploy

```bash
# ── Full local verification ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

cd aether-os

# 1. Build WASM
cd packages/crypto-core
wasm-pack build --target nodejs --out-dir pkg-node --release
wasm-pack build --target web    --out-dir pkg-web  --release
cd ../..

# 2. Rust workspace
cargo build --workspace
cargo test  --workspace -- --nocapture 2>&1 | tail -20

# 3. TypeScript + e2e ZK test
pnpm install
turbo run typecheck
cd tools/cli && pnpm run test:e2e && cd ../..

# 4. Storage adapters unit test
cd packages/storage-adapters && pnpm test && cd ../..

# 5. Full stack local smoke test
docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up -d

export DATABASE_URL="postgresql://root@localhost:26257/aether?sslmode=disable"
sqlx migrate run --source services/metadata-service/migrations

(cd services/metadata-service   && cargo run) &
(cd services/chunk-orchestrator && cargo run) &
(cd services/auth-service       && pnpm dev)  &
(cd services/delta-sync-service && pnpm dev)  &
(cd apps/web                    && pnpm dev)  &

sleep 10

curl -sf http://localhost:3001/health | jq .
curl -sf http://localhost:3002/health | jq .
curl -sf http://localhost:3003        | jq .
curl -sf http://localhost:3000/api/health 2>/dev/null || echo "web starting..."

grpcurl -plaintext localhost:50051 list
grpcurl -plaintext localhost:50052 list

echo "=== All services healthy ==="

# ── Terraform production deploy ────────────────────────────────────────────────

cd infra/terraform/environments/production

# One-time state backend bootstrap
aws s3 mb s3://aether-terraform-state-prod --region us-east-1
aws dynamodb create-table \
  --table-name aether-tf-locks \
  --attribute-definitions AttributeName=LockID,AttributeType=S \
  --key-schema AttributeName=LockID,KeyType=HASH \
  --billing-mode PAY_PER_REQUEST \
  --region us-east-1

terraform init
terraform plan -out=tfplan
terraform apply tfplan

# Push secrets (after cluster is up)
aws secretsmanager put-secret-value \
  --secret-id aether/production \
  --secret-string "$(cat secrets.json)"  # never commit secrets.json

# Deploy to EKS
aws eks update-kubeconfig --name aether-prod --region us-east-1
kubectl apply -k infra/k8s/base/
kubectl rollout status deployment/chunk-orchestrator -n aether --timeout=5m
kubectl rollout status deployment/web -n aether --timeout=5m

# Final smoke test against production
INGRESS=$(kubectl get ingress web-ingress -n aether \
  -o jsonpath='{.status.loadBalancer.ingress[0].hostname}')
curl -sf "https://${INGRESS}/api/health"
```

### Phase 7 Complete

```
Provider adapters (packages/storage-adapters)
  OneDriveAdapter   Microsoft Graph v1.0 — simple + resumable upload,
                    delta via deltaLink cursor, quota, folder management
  DropboxAdapter    Dropbox API v2 — upload/session, stable id: object IDs,
                    list_folder/continue cursor, content_hash etag
  MegaAdapter       megajs wrapper — lazy-init credentials, snapshot-diff
                    delta (WebSocket push events deferred to Phase 8),
                    folder listing, stable nodeId references
  ProviderRegistry  Singleton per-(userId, providerId) cache, lazy credential
                    loading, invalidation on credential revocation

chunk-orchestrator (REST bridge — Axum :3002)
  POST /upload/sessions      Provider session URL preparation + shard assignment
  POST /upload/commit        Manifest receipt + physical_chunks persistence
  GET  /manifest/:version_id Shard manifest for download pipeline
  GET  /shard                Authenticated provider shard proxy
  GET  /health               Liveness endpoint

OpenTelemetry (all services)
  Rust:        tracing-opentelemetry → OTLP/gRPC → OTel Collector → Tempo/Prometheus
  TypeScript:  @opentelemetry/sdk-node → instrumented HTTP + PG + Winston

Kubernetes (infra/k8s/)
  Deployments:  all 5 services with resource limits, liveness/readiness probes,
                preStop hooks, IRSA service account
  HPA:          chunk-orchestrator (3–20 replicas), delta-sync (2–10)
  Ingress:      NGINX + cert-manager Let's Encrypt TLS
  OTel stack:   Collector → Tempo (traces) + Prometheus (metrics) + Loki (logs)

Terraform (infra/terraform/)
  EKS 1.30:     Karpenter just-in-time nodes (spot + on-demand, c/m/r families)
  CockroachDB:  Dedicated cluster, 2-region (us-east-1 primary, us-west-2)
  ElastiCache:  Redis 7, 3-node cluster, Multi-AZ, TLS
  IRSA:         Per-workload IAM roles, Secrets Manager access policy

CI/CD (.github/workflows/ci.yml)
  PR gate:      Rust cargo test + wasm-pack build + CLI e2e ZK test
                TypeScript turbo typecheck + vitest
  Main merge:   Multi-service Docker build matrix → GHCR
                Kustomize image tag update → kubectl apply
                Per-deployment rollout wait + smoke test
```

## Project Aether — Phase 8: ZK File Sharing, Audit Service & Version History

This phase delivers the three remaining core features from the RFC security model: zero-knowledge file sharing via X25519 key exchange, the append-only Ed25519-signed audit service, and file version history with client-side restore. It also closes the remaining open UI components and the shared `packages/types` + `packages/ui` libraries.

### Architecture

```
ZK Sharing flow:
  Sharer client (worker)
    X25519 ephemeral keypair → ECDH(eph_priv, recipient_pub) → share_key
    ChaCha20-Poly1305(share_key, DEK) → share_token
    POST /api/shares { share_token, eph_pub, inode_id, recipient_id }

  Recipient client (worker)
    GET  /api/shares/:id → { share_token, eph_pub }
    ECDH(recipient_priv, eph_pub) → share_key
    ChaCha20-Poly1305.decrypt(share_key, share_token) → DEK
    Normal download pipeline with recovered DEK

Audit service (:50053, gRPC)
  AppendEvent   — Ed25519-signed entry, hash-chained, immutable
  VerifyChain   — verify integrity of a user's event chain
  ListEvents    — paginated audit log for compliance export
  All services → audit-service via NATS aether.audit.> subject

Version history:
  GET /volumes/:id/files/:inodeId/versions  → InodeVersion[]
  POST /volumes/:id/files/:inodeId/restore  → new version from old DEK
```

### Part 1 · Shared Packages
