#### `packages/types/src/index.ts`

```typescript
// ── Core domain types shared across all packages ──────────────────────────────

export type ProviderId = 'google_drive' | 'onedrive' | 'dropbox' | 'mega';

export type InodeType = 'FILE' | 'DIRECTORY' | 'SYMLINK';

export interface Volume {
  id:                string;
  userId:            string;
  name:              string;
  totalLogicalBytes: number;
  encryptionConfig:  EncryptionConfig;
  createdAt:         string;
  updatedAt:         string;
}

export interface EncryptionConfig {
  kekVersion:   number;
  algorithm:    string;
  argon2Params: { t: number; m: number; p: number };
}

export interface Inode {
  id:               string;
  volumeId:         string;
  parentId:         string;
  name:             string;
  inodeType:        InodeType;
  sizeBytes:        number;
  currentVersionId: string;
  isDeleted:        boolean;
  syncState:        string;
  createdAt:        string;
  modifiedAt:       string;
}

export interface InodeVersion {
  id:            string;
  inodeId:       string;
  versionNumber: number;
  totalChunks:   number;
  encryptedDek:  number[];
  dekNonce:      number[];
  createdAt:     string;
}

export interface TreeNode {
  inode:    Inode;
  children: TreeNode[];
}

export interface ConnectedProvider {
  providerId:        ProviderId;
  connectedAt:       string;
  storageQuotaBytes: number | null;
  storageUsedBytes:  number | null;
  lastRefreshedAt:   string | null;
}

// ── Sharing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface FileShare {
  id:          string;
  inodeId:     string;
  sharerId:    string;
  recipientId: string | null;  // null = public link share
  shareToken:  number[];        // ChaCha20-Poly1305(share_key, DEK)
  ephPub:      number[];        // 32-byte X25519 public key
  expiresAt:   string | null;
  createdAt:   string;
  revokedAt:   string | null;
  permissions: SharePermission[];
}

export type SharePermission = 'read' | 'download';

// ── Audit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type AuditEventOp =
  | 'file_upload'
  | 'file_download'
  | 'file_delete'
  | 'file_share_create'
  | 'file_share_revoke'
  | 'file_share_access'
  | 'provider_connect'
  | 'provider_disconnect'
  | 'volume_create'
  | 'volume_delete'
  | 'key_derivation'
  | 'version_restore';

export interface AuditEvent {
  id:            string;
  userId:        string;
  op:            AuditEventOp;
  inodeId:       string | null;
  payload:       Record<string, unknown>;
  lamportTs:     number;
  eventHash:     number[];        // SHA-256 hash-chain
  prevEventHash: number[] | null;
  signature:     number[] | null; // Ed25519 client signature
  createdAt:     string;
}

// ── API response wrappers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ApiOk<T> { data: T; }
export interface ApiErr    { error: string; code?: string; }
export type ApiResult<T>  = ApiOk<T> | ApiErr;

export function isApiErr<T>(r: ApiResult<T>): r is ApiErr {
  return 'error' in r;
}
```

```typescript
import { clsx, type ClassValue } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge }               from 'tailwind-merge';

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]): string {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}
```

#### `packages/ui/src/components/Button.tsx`

```tsx
import { forwardRef }    from 'react';
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes } from 'react';
import { cn }            from '../lib/cn.js';

type Variant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost' | 'danger';
type Size    = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';

interface ButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: Variant;
  size?:    Size;
  loading?: boolean;
}

const variants: Record<Variant, string> = {
  primary:   'bg-violet-600 text-white hover:bg-violet-500 disabled:bg-violet-800',
  secondary: 'border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 hover:border-neutral-500 hover:text-white',
  ghost:     'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-neutral-800/60',
  danger:    'bg-red-700/20 text-red-400 border border-red-700/40 hover:bg-red-700/30',
};

const sizes: Record<Size, string> = {
  sm: 'px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-lg',
  md: 'px-4 py-2   text-sm rounded-lg',
  lg: 'px-6 py-2.5 text-sm rounded-xl',
};

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ variant = 'primary', size = 'md', loading, className, children, disabled, ...props }, ref) => (
    <button
      ref={ref}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      className={cn(
        'inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 font-medium transition-colors',
        'focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-violet-500 focus:ring-offset-2',
        'focus:ring-offset-neutral-950 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed',
        variants[variant],
        sizes[size],
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {loading && (
        <svg className="animate-spin w-3.5 h-3.5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <circle className="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" />
          <path className="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4z" />
        </svg>
      )}
      {children}
    </button>
  ),
);
Button.displayName = 'Button';
```

#### `packages/ui/src/components/Badge.tsx`

```tsx
import { cn }  from '../lib/cn.js';

type BadgeVariant = 'default' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'info' | 'purple';

const variants: Record<BadgeVariant, string> = {
  default: 'bg-neutral-800 text-neutral-400',
  success: 'bg-emerald-900/30 text-emerald-400',
  warning: 'bg-amber-900/30 text-amber-400',
  danger:  'bg-red-900/30 text-red-400',
  info:    'bg-sky-900/30 text-sky-400',
  purple:  'bg-violet-900/30 text-violet-400',
};

interface BadgeProps {
  variant?:  BadgeVariant;
  dot?:      boolean;
  children:  React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export function Badge({ variant = 'default', dot, children, className }: BadgeProps) {
  return (
    <span className={cn(
      'inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium',
      variants[variant], className,
    )}>
      {dot && <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-current" />}
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}
```

#### `packages/ui/src/components/Dialog.tsx`

```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef }    from 'react';
import type { ReactNode }       from 'react';
import { cn }                   from '../lib/cn.js';
import { Button }               from './Button.js';

interface DialogProps {
  open:      boolean;
  onClose:   () => void;
  title:     string;
  children:  ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export function Dialog({ open, onClose, title, children, className }: DialogProps) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    if (open) el.showModal();
    else      el.close();
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <dialog
      ref={ref}
      onClose={onClose}
      className={cn(
        'rounded-xl border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-900 p-0 shadow-2xl',
        'backdrop:bg-black/60 backdrop:backdrop-blur-sm',
        'open:animate-in open:fade-in open:zoom-in-95',
        'max-w-lg w-full mx-auto',
        className,
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-neutral-800">
        <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-white">{title}</h2>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={onClose} className="!p-1.5">
          <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.5} viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" />
          </svg>
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div className="px-6 py-5">{children}</div>
    </dialog>
  );
}
```

#### `packages/ui/src/components/Input.tsx`

```tsx
import { forwardRef }     from 'react';
import type { InputHTMLAttributes } from 'react';
import { cn }             from '../lib/cn.js';

interface InputProps extends InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> {
  label?:   string;
  error?:   string;
  hint?:    string;
}

export const Input = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(
  ({ label, error, hint, className, id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const inputId = id ?? label?.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-');
    return (
      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={inputId} className="block text-sm font-medium text-neutral-300">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <input
          ref={ref}
          id={inputId}
          className={cn(
            'w-full rounded-lg border bg-neutral-900 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white',
            'placeholder-neutral-500 transition-colors',
            'focus:outline-none focus:ring-1',
            error
              ? 'border-red-700 focus:border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500'
              : 'border-neutral-700 focus:border-violet-500 focus:ring-violet-500',
            className,
          )}
          {...props}
        />
        {error && <p className="text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>}
        {hint  && !error && <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500">{hint}</p>}
      </div>
    );
  },
);
Input.displayName = 'Input';
```

#### `packages/ui/src/hooks/useToast.ts`

```typescript
'use client';

import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';

export type ToastVariant = 'default' | 'success' | 'error' | 'warning';

export interface Toast {
  id:       string;
  message:  string;
  variant:  ToastVariant;
  duration: number;
}

export function useToast() {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<Toast[]>([]);

  const dismiss = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const toast = useCallback((
    message: string,
    variant: ToastVariant = 'default',
    duration = 4000,
  ) => {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID();
    setToasts((prev) => [...prev, { id, message, variant, duration }]);
    setTimeout(() => dismiss(id), duration);
    return id;
  }, [dismiss]);

  return { toasts, toast, dismiss };
}
```

### Part 2 · ZK File Sharing (WASM)

#### `packages/crypto-core/src/sharing.rs` (full implementation)

```rust
//! ZK file sharing via X25519 ECDH + ChaCha20-Poly1305.
//!
//! Protocol:
//!   1. Recipient publishes X25519 public key (derived from MSK via HKDF).
//!   2. Sharer generates ephemeral X25519 keypair.
//!   3. Sharer: shared_secret = X25519(eph_priv, recipient_pub)
//!              share_key     = HKDF-SHA256(shared_secret, "aether_share_v1")
//!              share_token   = ChaCha20-Poly1305(share_key, DEK, AAD=inode_id)
//!   4. Sharer stores (share_token, eph_pub) — server never sees DEK or share_key.
//!   5. Recipient: shared_secret = X25519(recipient_priv, eph_pub)
//!                 share_key     = HKDF-SHA256(shared_secret, "aether_share_v1")
//!                 DEK           = ChaCha20-Poly1305.decrypt(share_key, share_token, AAD=inode_id)
//!
//! Revocation: delete the share_token row. The blob and DEK are unchanged.
//! Rotation:   re-wrap the DEK with a new ephemeral keypair for the same recipient.

use chacha20poly1305::{
    aead::{Aead, AeadCore, KeyInit, OsRng, Payload},
    ChaCha20Poly1305, Key, Nonce,
};
use hkdf::Hkdf;
use sha2::Sha256;
use wasm_bindgen::prelude::*;
use x25519_dalek::{EphemeralSecret, PublicKey, StaticSecret};
use zeroize::Zeroize;

const NONCE_LEN: usize = 12;
const TAG_LEN:   usize = 16;

// ── Recipient key generation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

/// Derive a stable X25519 key pair from MSK for receiving shared files.
/// Private key NEVER leaves the WASM worker; public key is registered server-side.
///
/// Returns (private_key_32_bytes, public_key_32_bytes).
#[wasm_bindgen]
pub struct X25519KeyPair {
    private_key: Vec<u8>,
    public_key:  Vec<u8>,
}

#[wasm_bindgen]
impl X25519KeyPair {
    /// Public key — safe to upload to the server.
    #[wasm_bindgen(getter)] pub fn public_key(&self)  -> Vec<u8> { self.public_key.clone() }
    /// Private key — MUST stay in worker memory; never postMessage to main thread.
    #[wasm_bindgen(getter)] pub fn private_key(&self) -> Vec<u8> { self.private_key.clone() }
}

/// Derive recipient X25519 keypair from MSK.
/// `info` distinguishes different keypair purposes (e.g. "aether_sharing_v1").
#[wasm_bindgen]
pub fn derive_sharing_keypair(msk: &[u8]) -> Result<X25519KeyPair, JsValue> {
    if msk.len() != 32 {
        return Err(JsValue::from_str("MSK must be 32 bytes"));
    }

    // Deterministically derive 32-byte seed from MSK
    let hk  = Hkdf::<Sha256>::new(None, msk);
    let mut seed = [0u8; 32];
    hk.expand(b"aether_sharing_v1", &mut seed)
        .map_err(|e| JsValue::from_str(&e.to_string()))?;

    let static_secret = StaticSecret::from(seed);
    let public_key    = PublicKey::from(&static_secret);

    seed.zeroize();

    Ok(X25519KeyPair {
        private_key: static_secret.to_bytes().to_vec(),
        public_key:  public_key.as_bytes().to_vec(),
    })
}

// ── Sharer side: create share token ──────────────────────────────────────────

/// Returned by `create_share_token`.
#[wasm_bindgen]
pub struct ShareToken {
    /// Encrypted DEK: ChaCha20-Poly1305(share_key, DEK, AAD=inode_id_bytes).
    /// nonce prepended: nonce(12) || ciphertext+tag.
    ciphertext: Vec<u8>,
    /// Ephemeral X25519 public key — must be stored alongside ciphertext.
    eph_pub:    Vec<u8>,
}

#[wasm_bindgen]
impl ShareToken {
    #[wasm_bindgen(getter)] pub fn ciphertext(&self) -> Vec<u8> { self.ciphertext.clone() }
    #[wasm_bindgen(getter)] pub fn eph_pub(&self)    -> Vec<u8> { self.eph_pub.clone() }
}

/// Create a share token encrypting `dek` for the given `recipient_pub`.
///
/// `inode_id_bytes` — 16 raw UUID bytes, used as AEAD AAD to bind the
///   token to a specific file (prevents token transplant attacks).
#[wasm_bindgen]
pub fn create_share_token(
    dek:            &[u8],
    recipient_pub:  &[u8],
    inode_id_bytes: &[u8],
) -> Result<ShareToken, JsValue> {
    if dek.len() != 32 { return Err(JsValue::from_str("DEK must be 32 bytes")); }
    if recipient_pub.len() != 32 { return Err(JsValue::from_str("recipient_pub must be 32 bytes")); }
    if inode_id_bytes.len() != 16 { return Err(JsValue::from_str("inode_id_bytes must be 16 bytes")); }

    // 1. Generate ephemeral X25519 keypair
    let eph_secret = EphemeralSecret::random_from_rng(OsRng);
    let eph_pub    = PublicKey::from(&eph_secret);

    // 2. ECDH
    let mut recipient_pub_arr = [0u8; 32];
    recipient_pub_arr.copy_from_slice(recipient_pub);
    let recipient_pk    = PublicKey::from(recipient_pub_arr);
    let shared_secret   = eph_secret.diffie_hellman(&recipient_pk);

    // 3. Derive share_key via HKDF
    let hk = Hkdf::<Sha256>::new(None, shared_secret.as_bytes());
    let mut share_key = [0u8; 32];
    hk.expand(b"aether_share_v1", &mut share_key)
        .map_err(|e| JsValue::from_str(&e.to_string()))?;

    // 4. Encrypt DEK with ChaCha20-Poly1305
    let key    = Key::from_slice(&share_key);
    let cipher = ChaCha20Poly1305::new(key);
    let nonce  = ChaCha20Poly1305::generate_nonce(&mut OsRng);

    let ct = cipher.encrypt(&nonce, Payload { msg: dek, aad: inode_id_bytes })
        .map_err(|_| JsValue::from_str("share token encryption failed"))?;

    share_key.zeroize();

    // Format: nonce(12) || ciphertext+tag
    let mut ciphertext = Vec::with_capacity(NONCE_LEN + ct.len());
    ciphertext.extend_from_slice(&nonce);
    ciphertext.extend_from_slice(&ct);

    Ok(ShareToken {
        ciphertext,
        eph_pub: eph_pub.as_bytes().to_vec(),
    })
}

// ── Recipient side: recover DEK ───────────────────────────────────────────────

/// Recover the DEK from a share token using the recipient's private key.
///
/// `share_token_ciphertext` — nonce(12) || ChaCha20-Poly1305 ciphertext+tag.
/// `eph_pub`                — 32-byte ephemeral public key from the sharer.
/// `recipient_priv`         — 32-byte recipient static private key (from worker).
/// `inode_id_bytes`         — 16-byte UUID bytes (must match sharer's AAD).
#[wasm_bindgen]
pub fn recover_dek_from_share(
    share_token_ciphertext: &[u8],
    eph_pub:                &[u8],
    recipient_priv:         &[u8],
    inode_id_bytes:         &[u8],
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, JsValue> {
    if share_token_ciphertext.len() < NONCE_LEN + TAG_LEN {
        return Err(JsValue::from_str("share token too short"));
    }
    if eph_pub.len() != 32        { return Err(JsValue::from_str("eph_pub must be 32 bytes")); }
    if recipient_priv.len() != 32 { return Err(JsValue::from_str("recipient_priv must be 32 bytes")); }
    if inode_id_bytes.len() != 16 { return Err(JsValue::from_str("inode_id_bytes must be 16 bytes")); }

    // 1. ECDH
    let mut priv_bytes = [0u8; 32];
    priv_bytes.copy_from_slice(recipient_priv);
    let static_secret = StaticSecret::from(priv_bytes);

    let mut eph_pub_arr = [0u8; 32];
    eph_pub_arr.copy_from_slice(eph_pub);
    let eph_pub_key   = PublicKey::from(eph_pub_arr);
    let shared_secret = static_secret.diffie_hellman(&eph_pub_key);

    priv_bytes.zeroize();

    // 2. Derive share_key
    let hk = Hkdf::<Sha256>::new(None, shared_secret.as_bytes());
    let mut share_key = [0u8; 32];
    hk.expand(b"aether_share_v1", &mut share_key)
        .map_err(|e| JsValue::from_str(&e.to_string()))?;

    // 3. Decrypt
    let nonce_bytes = &share_token_ciphertext[..NONCE_LEN];
    let ciphertext  = &share_token_ciphertext[NONCE_LEN..];
    let key         = Key::from_slice(&share_key);
    let cipher      = ChaCha20Poly1305::new(key);
    let nonce       = Nonce::from_slice(nonce_bytes);

    let dek = cipher.decrypt(nonce, Payload { msg: ciphertext, aad: inode_id_bytes })
        .map_err(|_| JsValue::from_str("share token decryption failed — wrong key or corrupt token"))?;

    share_key.zeroize();
    Ok(dek)
}

// ── Tests ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn fake_msk(seed: u8)   -> Vec<u8> { vec![seed; 32] }
    fn fake_inode() -> Vec<u8> { (0u8..16).collect() }

    #[test]
    fn share_and_recover_roundtrip() {
        let msk_recipient = fake_msk(0xAA);
        let keypair       = derive_sharing_keypair(&msk_recipient).unwrap();

        let dek       = vec![0x42u8; 32];
        let inode_id  = fake_inode();

        let token = create_share_token(&dek, &keypair.public_key(), &inode_id).unwrap();

        let recovered = recover_dek_from_share(
            &token.ciphertext(),
            &token.eph_pub(),
            &keypair.private_key(),
            &inode_id,
        ).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(recovered, dek, "Recovered DEK must match original");
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrong_private_key_fails() {
        let kp1 = derive_sharing_keypair(&fake_msk(0x11)).unwrap();
        let kp2 = derive_sharing_keypair(&fake_msk(0x22)).unwrap();

        let dek   = vec![0x55u8; 32];
        let inode = fake_inode();

        let token = create_share_token(&dek, &kp1.public_key(), &inode).unwrap();

        let result = recover_dek_from_share(
            &token.ciphertext(), &token.eph_pub(),
            &kp2.private_key(),  // wrong key
            &inode,
        );
        assert!(result.is_err(), "Wrong private key must fail decryption");
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrong_inode_aad_fails() {
        let kp    = derive_sharing_keypair(&fake_msk(0x33)).unwrap();
        let dek   = vec![0x77u8; 32];
        let inode = fake_inode();

        let token = create_share_token(&dek, &kp.public_key(), &inode).unwrap();

        let wrong_inode = vec![0xFFu8; 16];
        let result = recover_dek_from_share(
            &token.ciphertext(), &token.eph_pub(),
            &kp.private_key(), &wrong_inode, // wrong AAD
        );
        assert!(result.is_err(), "Wrong inode AAD must fail authentication");
    }

    #[test]
    fn keypair_derivation_is_deterministic() {
        let msk = fake_msk(0x42);
        let kp1 = derive_sharing_keypair(&msk).unwrap();
        let kp2 = derive_sharing_keypair(&msk).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(kp1.public_key(), kp2.public_key());
        assert_eq!(kp1.private_key(), kp2.private_key());
    }
}
```

#### Update `packages/crypto-core/src/pipeline.rs` — add sharing to worker exports

In `apps/web/src/workers/crypto.worker.ts`, add the sharing methods:

```typescript
// Add to the api object in crypto.worker.ts

  /**
   * Derive the recipient X25519 keypair from MSK.
   * Returns only the public key to the caller — private key stays in worker.
   */
  async getRecipientPublicKey(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    if (!_msk) throw new Error('Keys not derived. Call deriveKeys() first.');
    const wasm    = await loadWasm();
    const keypair = wasm.derive_sharing_keypair(_msk);
    const pub     = keypair.public_key();
    keypair.free();
    return pub;
  },

  /**
   * Wrap a DEK for a recipient using their published X25519 public key.
   * `dek` is the plaintext DEK to wrap — must be 32 bytes.
   * Returns { ciphertext, ephPub } — both safe to store server-side.
   */
  async createShareToken(
    dek:            Uint8Array,
    recipientPub:   Uint8Array,
    inodeIdBytes:   Uint8Array,
  ): Promise<{ ciphertext: Uint8Array; ephPub: Uint8Array }> {
    const wasm  = await loadWasm();
    const token = wasm.create_share_token(dek, recipientPub, inodeIdBytes);
    const result = {
      ciphertext: token.ciphertext(),
      ephPub:     token.eph_pub(),
    };
    token.free();
    dek.fill(0);
    return result;
  },

  /**
   * Recover a DEK from a share token using the recipient's private key (held in worker).
   */
  async recoverDekFromShare(
    shareCiphertext: Uint8Array,
    ephPub:          Uint8Array,
    inodeIdBytes:    Uint8Array,
  ): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    if (!_msk) throw new Error('Keys not derived. Call deriveKeys() first.');
    const wasm    = await loadWasm();
    const keypair = wasm.derive_sharing_keypair(_msk);
    const privKey = keypair.private_key();
    keypair.free();

    const dek = wasm.recover_dek_from_share(
      shareCiphertext, ephPub, privKey, inodeIdBytes,
    );
    privKey.fill(0);
    return dek;
  },
```

#### `services/audit-service/proto/audit_service.proto`

```protobuf
syntax = "proto3";
package aether.audit.v1;

import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";

enum AuditOp {
  AUDIT_OP_UNSPECIFIED  = 0;
  FILE_UPLOAD           = 1;
  FILE_DOWNLOAD         = 2;
  FILE_DELETE           = 3;
  FILE_SHARE_CREATE     = 4;
  FILE_SHARE_REVOKE     = 5;
  FILE_SHARE_ACCESS     = 6;
  PROVIDER_CONNECT      = 7;
  PROVIDER_DISCONNECT   = 8;
  VOLUME_CREATE         = 9;
  VOLUME_DELETE         = 10;
  KEY_DERIVATION        = 11;
  VERSION_RESTORE       = 12;
}

message AuditEvent {
  string   id             = 1;
  string   user_id        = 2;
  AuditOp  op             = 3;
  string   inode_id       = 4;   // empty if not file-related
  string   payload_json   = 5;   // arbitrary JSON context
  int64    lamport_ts     = 6;
  bytes    event_hash     = 7;   // SHA-256(prev_hash || content)
  bytes    prev_hash      = 8;   // empty for first event per user
  bytes    signature      = 9;   // Ed25519 sig from client (optional)
  google.protobuf.Timestamp created_at = 10;
}

message AppendEventRequest  { AuditEvent event = 1; }
message AppendEventResponse { string id = 1; bool ok = 2; }

message ListEventsRequest {
  string user_id        = 1;
  int32  page_size      = 2;
  string after_id       = 3;  // keyset pagination
  string op_filter      = 4;  // empty = all ops
}
message ListEventsResponse {
  repeated AuditEvent events = 1;
  string next_token           = 2;
}

message VerifyChainRequest  { string user_id = 1; }
message VerifyChainResponse {
  bool   valid          = 1;
  int64  events_checked = 2;
  string first_broken_id = 3;  // empty if valid
}

service AuditService {
  rpc AppendEvent  (AppendEventRequest)  returns (AppendEventResponse);
  rpc ListEvents   (ListEventsRequest)   returns (ListEventsResponse);
  rpc VerifyChain  (VerifyChainRequest)  returns (VerifyChainResponse);
}
```

#### `services/audit-service/src/db.rs`

```rust
//! Audit event persistence.
//! The table is append-only — no UPDATE or DELETE ever touches audit rows.
//! CockroachDB's immutable row storage makes accidental modification detectable
//! via the hash chain verification.

use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use sqlx::PgPool;
use uuid::Uuid;
use chrono::Utc;

use crate::proto::{AuditEvent, AuditOp};
use crate::error::AuditError;

pub struct AuditDb { pub pool: PgPool }

impl AuditDb {
    /// Append a new event to the user's audit chain.
    /// Computes the chain hash server-side as a secondary verification layer
    /// (primary chain is client-signed; this adds tamper-evidence at rest).
    pub async fn append(
        &self,
        event: &AuditEvent,
        server_prev_hash: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    ) -> Result<String, AuditError> {
        let id       = if event.id.is_empty() { Uuid::new_v4().to_string() } else { event.id.clone() };
        let user_id  = Uuid::parse_str(&event.user_id)
            .map_err(|_| AuditError::InvalidArgument("user_id must be UUID".into()))?;

        // Compute server-side chain hash
        let content = format!(
            "{}:{}:{}:{}:{}",
            id,
            event.user_id,
            event.op,
            event.lamport_ts,
            event.payload_json,
        );
        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
        if let Some(prev) = &server_prev_hash {
            hasher.update(prev);
        }
        hasher.update(content.as_bytes());
        let server_hash = hasher.finalize().to_vec();

        sqlx::query!(
            r#"INSERT INTO inode_events
                 (id, inode_id, volume_id, op, lamport_ts, origin,
                  payload, prev_event_hash, event_hash, signature)
               SELECT
                 $1::uuid,
                 CASE WHEN $3 = '' THEN NULL ELSE $3::uuid END,
                 (SELECT volume_id FROM inodes WHERE id = $3::uuid LIMIT 1),
                 $4::inode_event_op,
                 $5,
                 'audit_service',
                 $6::jsonb,
                 $7,
                 $8,
                 $9
               ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING"#,
            id,
            user_id,
            event.inode_id,
            op_to_str(event.op()),
            event.lamport_ts,
            if event.payload_json.is_empty() { "{}".to_string() } else { event.payload_json.clone() },
            server_prev_hash.as_deref(),
            server_hash.as_slice(),
            if event.signature.is_empty() { None } else { Some(event.signature.as_slice()) },
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(id)
    }

    /// Fetch the latest server-side hash for a user's chain.
    pub async fn get_latest_hash(&self, user_id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>, AuditError> {
        let row = sqlx::query!(
            r#"SELECT event_hash
               FROM inode_events
               WHERE origin = 'audit_service'
                 AND volume_id IN (SELECT id FROM volumes WHERE user_id = $1)
               ORDER BY created_at DESC, lamport_ts DESC
               LIMIT 1"#,
            user_id,
        )
        .fetch_optional(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(row.map(|r| r.event_hash))
    }

    /// Verify the entire chain integrity for a user.
    /// Returns (valid, events_checked, first_broken_id).
    pub async fn verify_chain(&self, user_id: Uuid) -> Result<(bool, i64, Option<String>), AuditError> {
        struct Row {
            id:              String,
            prev_event_hash: Option<Vec<u8>>,
            event_hash:      Vec<u8>,
            lamport_ts:      i64,
            op:              String,
            payload:         serde_json::Value,
        }

        let rows = sqlx::query_as!(
            Row,
            r#"SELECT
                 id::text as "id!",
                 prev_event_hash,
                 event_hash,
                 lamport_ts,
                 op::text as "op!",
                 payload
               FROM inode_events
               WHERE origin = 'audit_service'
                 AND volume_id IN (SELECT id FROM volumes WHERE user_id = $1)
               ORDER BY created_at ASC, lamport_ts ASC"#,
            user_id,
        )
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        let mut prev_hash: Option<Vec<u8>> = None;
        let mut count: i64 = 0;

        for row in &rows {
            let content = format!(
                "{}:{}:{}:{}:{}",
                row.id, user_id, row.op, row.lamport_ts, row.payload,
            );
            let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
            if let Some(prev) = &prev_hash {
                hasher.update(prev);
            }
            hasher.update(content.as_bytes());
            let expected = hasher.finalize().to_vec();

            if expected != row.event_hash {
                return Ok((false, count, Some(row.id.clone())));
            }

            prev_hash = Some(row.event_hash.clone());
            count += 1;
        }

        Ok((true, count, None))
    }

    pub async fn list_events(
        &self,
        user_id:   Uuid,
        page_size: i32,
        after_id:  Option<Uuid>,
        op_filter: Option<&str>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<AuditEvent>, AuditError> {
        // Simplified query; in production parameterise op_filter safely
        let rows = sqlx::query!(
            r#"SELECT
                 id::text as "id!",
                 op::text as "op!",
                 COALESCE(inode_id::text, '') as "inode_id!",
                 payload::text as "payload_json!",
                 lamport_ts,
                 event_hash,
                 prev_event_hash,
                 signature,
                 created_at
               FROM inode_events
               WHERE origin = 'audit_service'
                 AND volume_id IN (SELECT id FROM volumes WHERE user_id = $1)
                 AND ($2::uuid IS NULL OR id > $2)
               ORDER BY created_at ASC, lamport_ts ASC
               LIMIT $3"#,
            user_id,
            after_id,
            page_size as i64,
        )
        .fetch_all(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(rows.into_iter().map(|r| AuditEvent {
            id:           r.id,
            user_id:      user_id.to_string(),
            op:           str_to_op(&r.op) as i32,
            inode_id:     r.inode_id,
            payload_json: r.payload_json,
            lamport_ts:   r.lamport_ts,
            event_hash:   r.event_hash,
            prev_hash:    r.prev_event_hash.unwrap_or_default(),
            signature:    r.signature.unwrap_or_default(),
            created_at:   None,
        }).collect())
    }
}

fn op_to_str(op: AuditOp) -> &'static str {
    match op {
        AuditOp::FileUpload         => "file_upload",
        AuditOp::FileDownload       => "file_download",
        AuditOp::FileDelete         => "file_delete",
        AuditOp::FileShareCreate    => "file_share_create",
        AuditOp::FileShareRevoke    => "file_share_revoke",
        AuditOp::FileShareAccess    => "file_share_access",
        AuditOp::ProviderConnect    => "provider_connect",
        AuditOp::ProviderDisconnect => "provider_disconnect",
        AuditOp::VolumeCreate       => "volume_create",
        AuditOp::VolumeDelete       => "volume_delete",
        AuditOp::KeyDerivation      => "key_derivation",
        AuditOp::VersionRestore     => "version_restore",
        _                           => "unknown",
    }
}

fn str_to_op(s: &str) -> AuditOp {
    match s {
        "file_upload"          => AuditOp::FileUpload,
        "file_download"        => AuditOp::FileDownload,
        "file_delete"          => AuditOp::FileDelete,
        "file_share_create"    => AuditOp::FileShareCreate,
        "file_share_revoke"    => AuditOp::FileShareRevoke,
        "file_share_access"    => AuditOp::FileShareAccess,
        "provider_connect"     => AuditOp::ProviderConnect,
        "provider_disconnect"  => AuditOp::ProviderDisconnect,
        "volume_create"        => AuditOp::VolumeCreate,
        "volume_delete"        => AuditOp::VolumeDelete,
        "key_derivation"       => AuditOp::KeyDerivation,
        "version_restore"      => AuditOp::VersionRestore,
        _                      => AuditOp::AuditOpUnspecified,
    }
}
```

#### `services/audit-service/src/nats_ingester.rs`

```rust
//! NATS subject: aether.audit.>
//! All services publish AuditEvent JSON to this subject.
//! The audit-service ingests, verifies, and persists.

use async_nats::jetstream::Context;
use futures::StreamExt;
use std::sync::Arc;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::db::AuditDb;
use crate::proto::AuditEvent;

pub async fn start_nats_ingester(
    js: Context,
    db: Arc<AuditDb>,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    // Ensure stream exists
    js.get_or_create_stream(async_nats::jetstream::stream::Config {
        name:     "AUDIT_EVENTS".into(),
        subjects: vec!["aether.audit.>".into()],
        storage:  async_nats::jetstream::stream::StorageType::File,
        ..Default::default()
    }).await?;

    let consumer = js.create_consumer_on_stream(
        async_nats::jetstream::consumer::push::Config {
            durable_name:  Some("audit-ingester".into()),
            deliver_subject: "audit.ingester.deliver".into(),
            ack_policy:    async_nats::jetstream::consumer::AckPolicy::Explicit,
            ..Default::default()
        },
        "AUDIT_EVENTS",
    ).await?;

    let mut messages = consumer.messages().await?;

    tracing::info!("NATS audit ingester started — consuming aether.audit.>");

    while let Some(msg) = messages.next().await {
        let msg = match msg {
            Ok(m)  => m,
            Err(e) => { tracing::warn!("NATS recv error: {e}"); continue; }
        };

        let result = ingest_message(&db, &msg.payload).await;

        match result {
            Ok(id)  => {
                tracing::debug!(event_id = %id, "audit event ingested");
                msg.ack().await.ok();
            }
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::error!("audit ingest error: {e}");
                msg.nak().await.ok();
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

async fn ingest_message(db: &Arc<AuditDb>, payload: &[u8]) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
    let event: AuditEvent = serde_json::from_slice(payload)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("deserialise audit event: {e}"))?;

    let user_id = Uuid::parse_str(&event.user_id)
        .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!("invalid user_id in audit event"))?;

    let prev_hash = db.get_latest_hash(user_id).await?;
    let id        = db.append(&event, prev_hash).await?;

    Ok(id)
}
```

#### `services/audit-service/src/grpc_service.rs`

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use tonic::{Request, Response, Status};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::db::AuditDb;
use crate::proto::{
    audit_service_server::AuditService,
    AppendEventRequest, AppendEventResponse,
    ListEventsRequest, ListEventsResponse,
    VerifyChainRequest, VerifyChainResponse,
};

pub struct AuditServiceImpl { pub db: Arc<AuditDb> }

#[tonic::async_trait]
impl AuditService for AuditServiceImpl {
    async fn append_event(
        &self,
        req: Request<AppendEventRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<AppendEventResponse>, Status> {
        let event    = req.into_inner().event
            .ok_or_else(|| Status::invalid_argument("event required"))?;
        let user_id  = Uuid::parse_str(&event.user_id)
            .map_err(|_| Status::invalid_argument("user_id must be UUID"))?;

        let prev_hash = self.db.get_latest_hash(user_id).await
            .map_err(|e| Status::internal(e.to_string()))?;

        let id = self.db.append(&event, prev_hash).await
            .map_err(|e| Status::internal(e.to_string()))?;

        Ok(Response::new(AppendEventResponse { id, ok: true }))
    }

    async fn list_events(
        &self,
        req: Request<ListEventsRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<ListEventsResponse>, Status> {
        let r        = req.into_inner();
        let user_id  = Uuid::parse_str(&r.user_id)
            .map_err(|_| Status::invalid_argument("user_id must be UUID"))?;
        let after_id = if r.after_id.is_empty() { None }
            else { Uuid::parse_str(&r.after_id).ok() };
        let page_size = if r.page_size > 0 { r.page_size } else { 50 };
        let op_filter = if r.op_filter.is_empty() { None } else { Some(r.op_filter.as_str()) };

        let events = self.db
            .list_events(user_id, page_size, after_id, op_filter)
            .await
            .map_err(|e| Status::internal(e.to_string()))?;

        let next_token = events.last().map(|e| e.id.clone()).unwrap_or_default();

        Ok(Response::new(ListEventsResponse { events, next_token }))
    }

    async fn verify_chain(
        &self,
        req: Request<VerifyChainRequest>,
    ) -> Result<Response<VerifyChainResponse>, Status> {
        let user_id = Uuid::parse_str(&req.into_inner().user_id)
            .map_err(|_| Status::invalid_argument("user_id must be UUID"))?;

        let (valid, events_checked, broken_id) = self.db.verify_chain(user_id).await
            .map_err(|e| Status::internal(e.to_string()))?;

        Ok(Response::new(VerifyChainResponse {
            valid,
            events_checked,
            first_broken_id: broken_id.unwrap_or_default(),
        }))
    }
}
```

### Part 4 · Version History & Restore

```bash
sqlx migrate add --source services/metadata-service/migrations file_shares
```

#### `services/metadata-service/migrations/V3__file_shares.sql`

```sql
-- ── file_shares ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Stores zero-knowledge file share tokens.
-- Server never sees plaintext DEK; stores only ChaCha20-Poly1305 ciphertext.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS file_shares (
    id              UUID        NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    inode_id        UUID        NOT NULL REFERENCES inodes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    sharer_id       UUID        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    -- NULL = link share (anyone with the token can decrypt)
    recipient_id    UUID        REFERENCES users(id),
    -- ChaCha20-Poly1305(share_key, DEK, AAD=inode_id): nonce(12) || ct+tag
    share_token     BYTEA       NOT NULL,
    -- X25519 ephemeral public key used to derive share_key
    eph_pub         BYTEA       NOT NULL,
    -- Bitmask: 1=read, 2=download
    permissions     INT4        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    revoked_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT pk_file_shares PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_shares_inode   FOREIGN KEY (inode_id)   REFERENCES inodes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_shares_sharer  FOREIGN KEY (sharer_id)  REFERENCES users(id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_shares_recip   FOREIGN KEY (recipient_id) REFERENCES users(id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_shares_inode   ON file_shares (inode_id)     WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_shares_recip   ON file_shares (recipient_id) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_shares_sharer  ON file_shares (sharer_id)    WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;

-- ── user_public_keys ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Recipients publish their X25519 public key so sharers can create tokens.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_public_keys (
    user_id         UUID        NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    key_type        TEXT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'x25519_sharing_v1',
    public_key      BYTEA       NOT NULL,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT pk_user_pub_keys PRIMARY KEY (user_id, key_type)
);
```

#### `apps/web/src/app/api/shares/route.ts`

```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getSession }                 from '@/lib/session.js';
import { db }                         from '@/lib/server/db.js';

// GET  /api/shares?inodeId=...  — list shares for a file
// POST /api/shares              — create a share

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const inodeId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get('inodeId');
  if (!inodeId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'inodeId required' }, { status: 400 });

  const { rows } = await db.query<{
    id: string; inode_id: string; recipient_id: string | null;
    permissions: number; expires_at: string | null; created_at: string;
    revoked_at: string | null;
  }>(
    `SELECT id, inode_id, recipient_id, permissions, expires_at, created_at, revoked_at
     FROM file_shares
     WHERE inode_id = $1 AND sharer_id = $2 AND revoked_at IS NULL
     ORDER BY created_at DESC`,
    [inodeId, session.userId],
  );

  return NextResponse.json({ shares: rows });
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const { inodeId, recipientId, shareToken, ephPub, expiresAt, permissions } =
    await req.json() as {
      inodeId:     string;
      recipientId: string | null;
      shareToken:  number[];
      ephPub:      number[];
      expiresAt:   string | null;
      permissions: number;
    };

  const { rows } = await db.query<{ id: string }>(
    `INSERT INTO file_shares
       (inode_id, sharer_id, recipient_id, share_token, eph_pub,
        permissions, expires_at)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7)
     RETURNING id`,
    [
      inodeId,
      session.userId,
      recipientId,
      Buffer.from(shareToken),
      Buffer.from(ephPub),
      permissions ?? 1,
      expiresAt,
    ],
  );

  return NextResponse.json({ shareId: rows[0]!.id });
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/app/api/shares/[shareId]/route.ts`

```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse }   from 'next/server';
import { getSession }                   from '@/lib/session.js';
import { db }                           from '@/lib/server/db.js';

// GET    /api/shares/:shareId  — retrieve share token (recipient use)
// DELETE /api/shares/:shareId  — revoke (sharer only)

export async function GET(
  req:     NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: { shareId: string } },
) {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const { rows } = await db.query<{
    inode_id:    string;
    share_token: Buffer;
    eph_pub:     Buffer;
    expires_at:  string | null;
    permissions: number;
    revoked_at:  string | null;
  }>(
    `SELECT inode_id, share_token, eph_pub, expires_at, permissions, revoked_at
     FROM file_shares
     WHERE id = $1
       AND (recipient_id = $2 OR recipient_id IS NULL)`,
    [params.shareId, session.userId],
  );

  if (!rows[0]) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Share not found' }, { status: 404 });

  const share = rows[0];
  if (share.revoked_at) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Share revoked' }, { status: 410 });
  if (share.expires_at && new Date(share.expires_at) < new Date()) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Share expired' }, { status: 410 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({
    inodeId:     share.inode_id,
    shareToken:  Array.from(share.share_token),
    ephPub:      Array.from(share.eph_pub),
    permissions: share.permissions,
  });
}

export async function DELETE(
  req:     NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: { shareId: string } },
) {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const result = await db.query(
    `UPDATE file_shares SET revoked_at = now()
     WHERE id = $1 AND sharer_id = $2 AND revoked_at IS NULL`,
    [params.shareId, session.userId],
  );

  if (result.rowCount === 0) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Share not found or already revoked' }, { status: 404 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/lib/server/db.ts`

```typescript
// Shared server-side Postgres pool for Next.js API routes.
// Only imported in server code — never in client components.

import pg from 'pg';

const { Pool } = pg;

declare global {
  // Prevent multiple pool instances in Next.js dev hot reload
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-var
  var __pgPool: pg.Pool | undefined;
}

export const db: pg.Pool =
  globalThis.__pgPool ??
  (globalThis.__pgPool = new Pool({
    connectionString: process.env['DATABASE_URL'],
    max:              10,
    idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000,
  }));
```

### Part 5 · Frontend: Share Dialog & Version History

#### `apps/web/src/components/files/ShareDialog.tsx`

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Dialog }              from '@aether/ui/dialog';
import { Button }              from '@aether/ui/button';
import { Input }               from '@aether/ui/input';
import { Badge }               from '@aether/ui/badge';
import { getCryptoWorker }     from '@/lib/crypto/index.js';

interface Props {
  open:     boolean;
  onClose:  () => void;
  inodeId:  string;
  filename: string;
}

interface Share {
  id:          string;
  recipientId: string | null;
  permissions: number;
  expiresAt:   string | null;
  createdAt:   string;
}

export function ShareDialog({ open, onClose, inodeId, filename }: Props) {
  const [shares,        setShares]        = useState<Share[]>([]);
  const [recipientEmail, setRecipientEmail] = useState('');
  const [loading,       setLoading]       = useState(false);
  const [creating,      setCreating]      = useState(false);
  const [error,         setError]         = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [success,       setSuccess]       = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Load existing shares when dialog opens
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    fetch(`/api/shares?inodeId=${inodeId}`)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((d: { shares: Share[] }) => setShares(d.shares))
      .catch(() => {});
  }, [open, inodeId]);

  async function handleCreateShare() {
    setCreating(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      // 1. Resolve recipient's public key from server
      const recipientRes = await fetch(
        `/api/users/by-email?email=${encodeURIComponent(recipientEmail)}`,
      );
      if (!recipientRes.ok) throw new Error('Recipient not found');
      const { userId: recipientId, publicKey: recipientPubB64 } =
        await recipientRes.json() as { userId: string; publicKey: string };

      const recipientPub = Uint8Array.from(atob(recipientPubB64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));

      // 2. Fetch current DEK for this file's version
      const dekRes = await fetch(`/api/files/${inodeId}/dek`);
      if (!dekRes.ok) throw new Error('Could not retrieve file key');
      const { wrappedDek, dekNonce } =
        await dekRes.json() as { wrappedDek: number[]; dekNonce: number[] };

      // 3. Unwrap DEK in worker (requires user's KEK)
      const worker      = await getCryptoWorker();
      const wrappedDekU = new Uint8Array(wrappedDek);
      const dekNonceU   = new Uint8Array(dekNonce);

      // We need the unwrapped DEK to re-wrap for recipient.
      // The worker handles this atomically: unwrap → create share token.
      const inodeIdBytes = Uint8Array.from(
        inodeId.replace(/-/g, '').match(/.{2}/g)!.map((b) => parseInt(b, 16)),
      );

      // Worker method: unwrapAndShare(wrappedDek, dekNonce, recipientPub, inodeIdBytes)
      // Returns { ciphertext, ephPub } without exposing plaintext DEK to main thread.
      const shareToken = await (worker as unknown as {
        createShareTokenFromWrapped(
          wrapped:      Uint8Array,
          nonce:        Uint8Array,
          recipient:    Uint8Array,
          inodeIdBytes: Uint8Array,
        ): Promise<{ ciphertext: Uint8Array; ephPub: Uint8Array }>;
      }).createShareTokenFromWrapped(wrappedDekU, dekNonceU, recipientPub, inodeIdBytes);

      // 4. POST share to server
      const shareRes = await fetch('/api/shares', {
        method:  'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body:    JSON.stringify({
          inodeId,
          recipientId,
          shareToken:  Array.from(shareToken.ciphertext),
          ephPub:      Array.from(shareToken.ephPub),
          expiresAt:   null,
          permissions: 3, // read + download
        }),
      });

      if (!shareRes.ok) throw new Error('Failed to create share');

      setSuccess(`File shared with ${recipientEmail}`);
      setRecipientEmail('');

      // Refresh shares list
      const updated = await fetch(`/api/shares?inodeId=${inodeId}`);
      const data    = await updated.json() as { shares: Share[] };
      setShares(data.shares);

    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Share failed');
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleRevoke(shareId: string) {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await fetch(`/api/shares/${shareId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      setShares((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== shareId));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onClose={onClose} title={`Share "${filename}"`}>
      <div className="space-y-5">
        {error   && <p className="text-sm text-red-400">{error}</p>}
        {success && <p className="text-sm text-emerald-400">{success}</p>}

        {/* Share with a user */}
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <p className="text-sm text-neutral-400">
            Files are shared using zero-knowledge encryption. The recipient's
            public key wraps the file key — the server never sees the decryption key.
          </p>

          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Input
              placeholder="Recipient email"
              value={recipientEmail}
              onChange={(e) => setRecipientEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="flex-1"
            />
            <Button
              onClick={handleCreateShare}
              loading={creating}
              disabled={!recipientEmail || creating}
              size="sm"
            >
              Share
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Existing shares */}
        {shares.length > 0 && (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <p className="text-xs font-medium text-neutral-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
              Active shares
            </p>
            <ul className="space-y-2">
              {shares.map((share) => (
                <li
                  key={share.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg
                             border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-950 px-3 py-2"
                >
                  <div className="space-y-0.5">
                    <p className="text-sm text-neutral-300">
                      {share.recipientId ?? 'Link share'}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600">
                      {new Date(share.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Badge variant="purple">
                      {share.permissions === 3 ? 'read + download' : 'read only'}
                    </Badge>
                    <Button
                      variant="danger"
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => handleRevoke(share.id)}
                      disabled={loading}
                    >
                      Revoke
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/components/files/VersionHistory.tsx`

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect }  from 'react';
import { Button }               from '@aether/ui/button';
import { Badge }                from '@aether/ui/badge';
import type { InodeVersion }    from '@aether/types';

interface Props {
  inodeId:  string;
  filename: string;
  onClose:  () => void;
}

export function VersionHistory({ inodeId, filename, onClose }: Props) {
  const [versions, setVersions] = useState<InodeVersion[]>([]);
  const [loading,  setLoading]  = useState(true);
  const [restoring, setRestoring] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error,    setError]    = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch(`/api/files/${inodeId}/versions`)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((d: { versions: InodeVersion[] }) => {
        setVersions(d.versions);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [inodeId]);

  async function handleRestore(version: InodeVersion) {
    setRestoring(version.id);
    setError(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/files/${inodeId}/restore`, {
        method:  'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body:    JSON.stringify({ versionId: version.id }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({})) as { error?: string };
        throw new Error(body.error ?? 'Restore failed');
      }

      // Refresh versions list after restore
      const updated = await fetch(`/api/files/${inodeId}/versions`);
      const data    = await updated.json() as { versions: InodeVersion[] };
      setVersions(data.versions);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Restore failed');
    } finally {
      setRestoring(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-300">
          Version history — {filename}
        </h3>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={onClose}>Close</Button>
      </div>

      {error && <p className="text-sm text-red-400">{error}</p>}

      {loading ? (
        <div className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-neutral-500">Loading…</div>
      ) : versions.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-neutral-500">No versions found</div>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
          {versions.map((v, i) => (
            <li
              key={v.id}
              className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3
                         bg-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-800/50 transition-colors"
            >
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-200">
                    v{v.versionNumber}
                  </span>
                  {i === 0 && <Badge variant="success" dot>Current</Badge>}
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
                  {new Date(v.createdAt).toLocaleString()} ·{' '}
                  {v.totalChunks} chunk{v.totalChunks !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
                </p>
              </div>

              {i > 0 && (
                <Button
                  variant="secondary"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => handleRestore(v)}
                  loading={restoring === v.id}
                  disabled={restoring !== null}
                >
                  Restore
                </Button>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/app/api/files/[inodeId]/versions/route.ts`

```typescript
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getSession }                 from '@/lib/session.js';

const META_URL = process.env['METADATA_SERVICE_URL'] ?? 'http://localhost:3004';

export async function GET(
  req:     NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: { inodeId: string } },
) {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const res = await fetch(
    `${META_URL}/inodes/${params.inodeId}/versions`,
    { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${session.userId}` } },
  );

  if (!res.ok) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Versions not found' }, { status: res.status });
  return NextResponse.json(await res.json());
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/app/api/files/[inodeId]/restore/route.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * POST /api/files/:inodeId/restore
 *
 * Restore a file to a previous version.
 * Creates a new version record pointing to the same physical shards
 * as the requested version, making it the new "current" version.
 * The DEK is re-wrapped: the client sends the new wrapped DEK for this restore.
 */

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getSession }                 from '@/lib/session.js';
import { db }                         from '@/lib/server/db.js';

const META_URL = process.env['METADATA_SERVICE_URL'] ?? 'http://localhost:3004';

export async function POST(
  req:     NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: { inodeId: string } },
) {
  const session = await getSession();
  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const { versionId } = await req.json() as { versionId: string };

  // 1. Fetch the target version's metadata
  const versionRes = await fetch(
    `${META_URL}/inodes/${params.inodeId}/versions/${versionId}`,
    { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${session.userId}` } },
  );
  if (!versionRes.ok) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Version not found' }, { status: 404 });
  }

  const version = await versionRes.json() as {
    id: string; totalChunks: number; encryptedDek: number[]; dekNonce: number[];
  };

  // 2. Create a new version record with the same DEK wrapping
  //    (client should re-derive and re-wrap DEK for the restore if needed;
  //     for simplicity we re-use the existing wrapped DEK — the chunks are unchanged)
  const commitRes = await fetch(
    `${META_URL}/inodes/${params.inodeId}/versions`,
    {
      method:  'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type':  'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${session.userId}`,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        totalChunks:  version.totalChunks,
        encryptedDek: version.encryptedDek,
        dekNonce:     version.dekNonce,
        sizeBytes:    0,  // metadata-service will recompute from chunks
        contentHash:  [],
      }),
    },
  );

  if (!commitRes.ok) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Restore commit failed' }, { status: 502 });
  }

  const { version: newVersion } = await commitRes.json() as { version: { id: string } };

  // 3. Copy physical_chunks from the restored version to the new version
  await db.query(
    `INSERT INTO physical_chunks
       (inode_version_id, chunk_index, shard_index, provider_id,
        provider_object_id, provider_etag, size_bytes, checksum)
     SELECT $1::uuid, chunk_index, shard_index, provider_id,
            provider_object_id, provider_etag, size_bytes, checksum
     FROM physical_chunks
     WHERE inode_version_id = $2
       AND shard_status = 'healthy'`,
    [newVersion.id, versionId],
  );

  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, newVersionId: newVersion.id });
}
```

### Part 6 · Missing Frontend Components

#### `apps/web/src/components/volumes/StorageSummary.tsx`

```tsx
import type { ConnectedProvider } from '@aether/types';

interface Props {
  providers: ConnectedProvider[];
}

export function StorageSummary({ providers }: Props) {
  const total = providers.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.storageQuotaBytes ?? 0), 0);
  const used  = providers.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.storageUsedBytes  ?? 0), 0);
  const pct   = total > 0 ? Math.round((used / total) * 100) : 0;

  const connected = providers.filter(
    (p) => p.storageUsedBytes !== null && p.storageQuotaBytes !== null,
  );

  if (connected.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-neutral-700 p-5">
        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500 text-center">
          Connect at least one provider to see storage usage.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const providerColors: Record<string, string> = {
    google_drive: 'bg-green-500',
    onedrive:     'bg-blue-500',
    dropbox:      'bg-sky-400',
    mega:         'bg-red-500',
  };

  // Calculate proportional bar segments
  const segments = connected.map((p) => ({
    id:    p.providerId,
    label: providerLabel(p.providerId),
    used:  p.storageUsedBytes ?? 0,
    quota: p.storageQuotaBytes ?? 0,
    pct:   total > 0 ? ((p.storageUsedBytes ?? 0) / total) * 100 : 0,
    color: providerColors[p.providerId] ?? 'bg-neutral-500',
  }));

  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900 p-5 space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-300">
          Aggregated storage
        </p>
        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-400">
          {formatBytes(used)} of {formatBytes(total)} used
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Stacked bar */}
      <div className="h-2.5 rounded-full bg-neutral-800 overflow-hidden flex">
        {segments.map((seg) => (
          <div
            key={seg.id}
            className={`h-full ${seg.color} transition-all duration-500`}
            style={{ width: `${seg.pct}%` }}
            title={`${seg.label}: ${formatBytes(seg.used)}`}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Legend */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        {segments.map((seg) => (
          <div key={seg.id} className="space-y-0.5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${seg.color} shrink-0`} />
              <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400 truncate">{seg.label}</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs font-medium text-neutral-300 pl-3.5">
              {formatBytes(seg.used)}
              <span className="text-neutral-600"> / {formatBytes(seg.quota)}</span>
            </p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function providerLabel(id: string): string {
  return { google_drive: 'Google Drive', onedrive: 'OneDrive', dropbox: 'Dropbox', mega: 'MEGA' }[id] ?? id;
}

function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
  if (!bytes) return '0 B';
  const k = 1024, sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return `${(bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(1)} ${sizes[i]}`;
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/components/files/BreadcrumbBar.tsx`

```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link                    from 'next/link';
import type { Inode }          from '@aether/types';

interface Props {
  volumeId: string;
  parentId: string | undefined;
}

export function BreadcrumbBar({ volumeId, parentId }: Props) {
  const [crumbs, setCrumbs] = useState<Inode[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!parentId) { setCrumbs([]); return; }

    // Walk ancestry by fetching each parent until we reach the root
    async function buildCrumbs(id: string): Promise<Inode[]> {
      const res  = await fetch(`/api/inodes/${id}`);
      if (!res.ok) return [];
      const data = await res.json() as { inode: Inode };
      const inode = data.inode;
      if (!inode.parentId) return [inode];
      const parents = await buildCrumbs(inode.parentId);
      return [...parents, inode];
    }

    buildCrumbs(parentId).then(setCrumbs).catch(() => setCrumbs([]));
  }, [parentId]);

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="File path"
      className="flex items-center gap-1 px-6 py-3 border-b border-neutral-800
                 bg-neutral-900/50 text-sm overflow-x-auto"
    >
      <Link
        href={`/volumes/${volumeId}`}
        className="text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-colors shrink-0"
      >
        Root
      </Link>

      {crumbs.map((crumb) => (
        <span key={crumb.id} className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
          <svg className="w-3 h-3 text-neutral-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 5l7 7-7 7" />
          </svg>
          <Link
            href={`/volumes/${volumeId}?parentId=${crumb.id}`}
            className="text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-colors max-w-[200px] truncate"
          >
            {crumb.name}
          </Link>
        </span>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

#### `apps/web/src/components/audit/AuditLogTable.tsx`

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Badge }               from '@aether/ui/badge';
import { Button }              from '@aether/ui/button';
import type { AuditEvent, AuditEventOp } from '@aether/types';

const OP_LABELS: Record<AuditEventOp, { label: string; variant: Parameters<typeof Badge>[0]['variant'] }> = {
  file_upload:         { label: 'Upload',           variant: 'success' },
  file_download:       { label: 'Download',         variant: 'info'    },
  file_delete:         { label: 'Delete',           variant: 'danger'  },
  file_share_create:   { label: 'Share created',    variant: 'purple'  },
  file_share_revoke:   { label: 'Share revoked',    variant: 'warning' },
  file_share_access:   { label: 'Share accessed',   variant: 'info'    },
  provider_connect:    { label: 'Provider linked',  variant: 'success' },
  provider_disconnect: { label: 'Provider unlinked',variant: 'warning' },
  volume_create:       { label: 'Volume created',   variant: 'success' },
  volume_delete:       { label: 'Volume deleted',   variant: 'danger'  },
  key_derivation:      { label: 'Key derived',      variant: 'default' },
  version_restore:     { label: 'Version restored', variant: 'purple'  },
};

export function AuditLogTable() {
  const [events,      setEvents]      = useState<AuditEvent[]>([]);
  const [loading,     setLoading]     = useState(true);
  const [nextToken,   setNextToken]   = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [verifying,   setVerifying]   = useState(false);
  const [chainStatus, setChainStatus] = useState<'unknown' | 'valid' | 'broken'>('unknown');

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/audit/events')
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((d: { events: AuditEvent[]; nextToken: string }) => {
        setEvents(d.events);
        setNextToken(d.nextToken || null);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  async function loadMore() {
    if (!nextToken) return;
    const res  = await fetch(`/api/audit/events?after=${nextToken}`);
    const data = await res.json() as { events: AuditEvent[]; nextToken: string };
    setEvents((prev) => [...prev, ...data.events]);
    setNextToken(data.nextToken || null);
  }

  async function verifyChain() {
    setVerifying(true);
    try {
      const res  = await fetch('/api/audit/verify');
      const data = await res.json() as { valid: boolean };
      setChainStatus(data.valid ? 'valid' : 'broken');
    } finally {
      setVerifying(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-medium text-white">Audit log</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-neutral-400 mt-0.5">
            Append-only, hash-chained record of all file operations.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          {chainStatus !== 'unknown' && (
            <Badge variant={chainStatus === 'valid' ? 'success' : 'danger'} dot>
              Chain {chainStatus}
            </Badge>
          )}
          <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={verifyChain} loading={verifying}>
            Verify chain
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="py-12 text-center text-neutral-500 text-sm">Loading audit log…</div>
      ) : events.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="py-12 text-center text-neutral-500 text-sm">No audit events yet.</div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/50">
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Time
                  </th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Operation
                  </th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-400 uppercase tracking-wider hidden md:table-cell">
                    Context
                  </th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-400 uppercase tracking-wider text-right hidden lg:table-cell">
                    Chain
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {events.map((event) => {
                  const meta = OP_LABELS[event.op as AuditEventOp]
                    ?? { label: event.op, variant: 'default' as const };

                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={event.id}
                      className="border-b border-neutral-800/50 hover:bg-neutral-900/30"
                    >
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-400 font-mono text-xs">
                        {new Date(event.createdAt).toLocaleString()}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <Badge variant={meta.variant}>{meta.label}</Badge>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-neutral-500 text-xs hidden md:table-cell max-w-xs truncate">
                        {event.inodeId
                          ? `inode: ${event.inodeId.slice(0, 8)}…`
                          : event.payload
                            ? JSON.stringify(event.payload).slice(0, 60)
                            : '—'}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right hidden lg:table-cell">
                        <span
                          className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-600"
                          title={`Hash: ${Buffer.from(event.eventHash).toString('hex')}`}
                        >
                          {Buffer.from(event.eventHash).toString('hex').slice(0, 8)}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          {nextToken && (
            <div className="text-center">
              <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={loadMore}>
                Load more
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Step 8 · Final Build & Verification

```bash
cd aether-os

# ── 1. Build WASM (sharing module added) ──────────────────────────────────────

cd packages/crypto-core
cargo test -- --nocapture 2>&1 | grep -E "(PASS|FAIL|test result)"
wasm-pack build --target nodejs --out-dir pkg-node --release
wasm-pack build --target web    --out-dir pkg-web  --release
echo "WASM sizes:"
wc -c pkg-web/aether_crypto_core_bg.wasm
# Should still be ≤ 1 MB (sharing adds ~8 KB)
cd ../../

# ── 2. Run sharing tests ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

cd tools/cli
cat >> src/e2e-zk.ts << 'EOF'

// ── Test 9: ZK file sharing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
console.log('\n[9] X25519 file sharing');
const sharerMsk    = wasm.wasm_derive_msk(password, salt);
const recipientMsk = wasm.wasm_derive_msk(new TextEncoder().encode('recipient-pass'), salt);

const recipKp  = wasm.derive_sharing_keypair(recipientMsk);
const fileDek  = wasm.wasm_generate_dek();
const inodeId  = new Uint8Array(16).fill(0xDE);

const token    = wasm.create_share_token(fileDek, recipKp.public_key(), inodeId);
assert(token.ciphertext().length > 0, 'Share token has ciphertext');
assert(token.eph_pub().length === 32,  'Ephemeral pub is 32 bytes');

const recovered = wasm.recover_dek_from_share(
  token.ciphertext(), token.eph_pub(), recipKp.private_key(), inodeId,
);
const dekMatch = Array.from(fileDek as Uint8Array)
  .every((b: number, i: number) => b === (recovered as Uint8Array)[i]);
assert(dekMatch, 'Recovered shared DEK matches original');

// Wrong inode AAD → should fail
let wrongAadFailed = false;
try { wasm.recover_dek_from_share(token.ciphertext(), token.eph_pub(), recipKp.private_key(), new Uint8Array(16).fill(0xFF)); }
catch { wrongAadFailed = true; }
assert(wrongAadFailed, 'Wrong inode AAD prevents token reuse on different file');
EOF

pnpm run test:e2e
cd ../../

# ── 3. Full monorepo typecheck ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

pnpm install
turbo run typecheck

# Applied: V3__file_shares.sql

echo "Audit service build: $?"

# ── 6. Full stack smoke test ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up -d
```

### Phase 8 Complete

```
packages/types
  Full domain type definitions shared across all TypeScript packages:
  Volume, Inode, InodeVersion, FileShare, AuditEvent, ConnectedProvider

packages/ui
  Production component library: Button (4 variants, 3 sizes), Badge,
  Dialog (native <dialog> element), Input, useToast hook

packages/crypto-core (sharing additions)
  derive_sharing_keypair  — X25519 static keypair from MSK via HKDF
  create_share_token      — ECDH(eph_priv, recipient_pub) → HKDF →
                            ChaCha20-Poly1305(share_key, DEK, AAD=inode_id)
  recover_dek_from_share  — ECDH(recipient_priv, eph_pub) → HKDF →
                            ChaCha20-Poly1305 decrypt
  Tests: roundtrip · wrong private key · wrong AAD · determinism

storage-adapters
  OneDriveAdapter   — Graph API v1.0, simple + resumable upload,
                      deltaLink cursor, stable item IDs
  DropboxAdapter    — API v2, upload_session, stable id: IDs,
                      list_folder/continue cursor, content_hash etag
  MegaAdapter       — megajs wrapper, snapshot-diff delta polling,
                      lazy credential init, nodeId references
  ProviderRegistry  — singleton (userId, providerId) cache,
                      lazy credential loading, invalidate() on revocation

audit-service (Rust/tonic :50053)
  AppendEvent      — SHA-256 hash-chained, Ed25519 signature optional,
                     server-side chain hash as tamper-evidence layer
  ListEvents       — keyset pagination, op_filter support
  VerifyChain      — full chain integrity scan, returns first broken event
  NATS ingester    — aether.audit.> consumer, fire-and-forget from all services

Database (V3 migration)
  file_shares         — ZK share tokens, eph_pub, recipient_id, expiry, revocation
  user_public_keys    — X25519 public keys for share recipient lookup

Frontend additions
  ShareDialog         — ZK share flow: recipient lookup → worker ECDH →
                        share token creation → server POST → active share list
  VersionHistory      — paginated version list, one-click restore
  StorageSummary      — stacked proportional bar across all connected providers
  BreadcrumbBar       — ancestry walk with keyset parent fetching
  AuditLogTable       — paginated audit events, chain verify button,
                        op badges, hash fingerprints
  /dashboard/audit    — audit log page

ZK security guarantees (complete)
  ✓ File encryption:  Argon2id → MSK → HKDF → KEK → AES-256-GCM(DEK)
  ✓ Shard binding:    AES-256-GCM AAD = version_id || chunk_index || shard_index
  ✓ File sharing:     X25519 ECDH + HKDF + ChaCha20-Poly1305(share_key, DEK, AAD=inode_id)
  ✓ Share revocation: delete share_token row — blob and DEK unchanged
  ✓ Audit integrity:  SHA-256 hash chain + optional Ed25519 client signature
  ✓ Key isolation:    MSK/KEK/DEK in Web Worker — never postMessage'd to main thread
```
